# AIDRPG Production Chunk 01 — V0.17 Foundation Acceptance and Freeze

## Document status

Production sequence: 01 of 25 remaining production chunks.
Build target: WorldLedger V0.17 / AIDRPG Core MVP Foundation Candidate.
Purpose: Accept, verify, freeze, and protect the V0.17 foundation before feature production begins.

## Doctrine

```text
Freeze the foundation before adding features.
Do not build upward from an unstable base.
Do not treat V0.17 as the full game.
Do not restart the 1–32 design chunks.
Do not make the user rediscover old failures manually.
```

## What V0.17 is allowed to be

Allowed:

```text
foundation candidate
hook-orchestration lock
safe state/runtime/command baseline
parser/proposal/validator/reducer boundary baseline
support-layer non-canon baseline
ReleaseCheck/regression baseline
```

Not allowed:

```text
complete full RPG engine
all 1–32 design chunks implemented
advanced combat engine
economy/crafting/reputation/domain engine
generated card engine
manual mutation/admin command engine
```

## Exact acceptance identity

Preserve or clearly evolve from:

```text
PROJECT_NAME: WorldLedger
BUILD_NAME: WorldLedger Core MVP
BUILD_VERSION: 0.17.0-foundation-codepass1-candidate
BUILD_STAGE: Foundation Code Pass 1 / Foundation Candidate and Hook-Orchestration Lock
SCHEMA_ID: worldledger.schema.v1.locked
ROOT_KEY: worldledger
```

If patched, recommended visible title:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.1 — Core MVP Foundation Script / Accepted Foundation Freeze
```

Do not rename the root to `aidrpg`. Preserve the bridge:

```text
AIDRPG = broader RPG-engine project goal.
WorldLedger = script-engine implementation name for the AIDRPG Core MVP rebuild.
```

## Foundation acceptance metadata

Add or verify:

```text
foundationAcceptance: {
  accepted: boolean,
  acceptedVersion: string,
  acceptedFromVersion: string,
  acceptedStage: string,
  acceptedAtTurn: number,
  acceptedByCommand: string,
  requiredChecks: string[],
  lastResult: string,
  warnings: string[],
  blockers: string[],
  notes: string[]
}
```

Recommended location:

```text
state.worldledger.meta.foundationAcceptance
```

This is diagnostic metadata, not gameplay truth.

## Active foundation systems to verify

```text
Constants and version identity
Feature flags and safe defaults
Utility helpers
State schema defaults
StateManager
Runtime hook contract
AuditLogger
OperationLedger
CommandRouter
Command renderers
ReleaseCheck foundation and gates
Resolver and owner-specific sanitizers
EventScannerLite safe shell
ProposalBuilder safe shell
CanonValidator safe shell
ReducerRegistry
Core reducer shells / limited reducers
DirtyQueue
ContextBuilderLite read-only shell
CardApiAdapter shell
CardSyncLite dry-run/protected shell
Migration/Quarantine conservative shell
CleanupManagerLite safe caps
Hook orchestration
Export/platform binding
```

If any are shell-only, ReleaseCheck must report honestly.

## Deferred systems that must stay disabled

```text
full combat automation
advanced progression scaling
level 1–1000 full stat engine
full economy/shop/barter/debt
crafting/material recipes
investigation/case/evidence automation
domain/labor/workers/projects
reputation/faction/title automation
absorption/evolution/devour systems
advanced party/summon controls
multi-protagonist support
warfare/group combat/army systems
generated Story Cards
manual mutation commands
live CardSync writes by default
full old-save conversion from prose
full WorldStructure settlement/project extraction
```

## Required command surface

Verify foundation commands are registered, safe, and prefixed with `[AIDRPG]`, including:

```text
/help /version /sheet /status /inventory /abilities /quests /actors /location /time
/debuglast /cardaudit /contextaudit /stateaudit /ledger /dirty /migration /quarantine
/releasecheck /startupaudit /schema /schemaaudit /owners /commandformat /commands
/statemanager /integrity /repairlog /runtime /hookaudit /runtimecheck /failsoft
/audit /ledgercheck /commandrouter /commandcheck /parser /parsercheck /proposal
/proposalcheck /validator /validatorcheck /resolver /resolvercheck /controlmap
/salvage /borrowledger /supportdepth /supportschemas /supportcheck
/playerbreakercheck /regressioncheck /runfoundationtests /gatecheck /releaseprofile
/knownfailures /regressionmatrix /bc16check /foundation /codepass /foundationcheck
/hookcheck /packagingcheck
```

Aliases should route safely and not bypass command suppression.

## Hook acceptance

Verify:

```text
globalThis.WorldLedger
WorldLedger.onInput
WorldLedger.onContext
WorldLedger.onOutput
WorldLedger.processOutput
```

Input hook initializes state, routes commands, suppresses command output, and never treats input as completed success.

Context hook builds read-only context and never mutates gameplay state.

Output hook skips command output, skips duplicate output consequences, scans only valid story output, validates before reducer, routes only accepted results, marks dirty targets, runs support-layer caps, and returns original output text.

## PC01 ReleaseCheck gates

```text
PC01-G01 V0.17 build identity active or accepted freeze version active
PC01-G02 AIDRPG/WorldLedger bridge present
PC01-G03 root key remains stable
PC01-G04 required foundation commands registered
PC01-G05 hook exports present
PC01-G06 deferred feature flags disabled
PC01-G07 CardSync dry-run/generated disabled
PC01-G08 manual mutation commands disabled
PC01-G09 command output suppression active
PC01-G10 duplicate output replay protection active
PC01-G11 player-breaker minimum set registered and passing
PC01-G12 regressioncheck registered and passing
PC01-G13 support layers non-canon
PC01-G14 ReleaseCheck does not fake PASS
PC01-G15 foundation accepted marker present only after checks pass
```

## Required tests

```text
PC01-001 Build identity stable
PC01-002 Foundation commands registered
PC01-003 Hook exports exist
PC01-004 Deferred flags remain disabled
PC01-005 Command output non-canon
PC01-006 Story Card text non-canon
PC01-007 Context text non-canon
PC01-008 Player-breaker minimum set passes
PC01-009 Regression baseline passes
PC01-010 ReleaseCheck no fake pass
PC01-011 Duplicate output no repeat effects
PC01-012 Foundation accepted marker writes once
```

Minimum player-breaker set:

```text
denied key -> no item
failed grab -> no item
abstract key -> no item
dream Crown -> no item
quoted key handoff -> no item
output-only Moonblade -> no stable ability
failed Void Lantern Veil -> no stable ability
visible chapel -> no scene overwrite
road then enter Drunken Huntsman -> inn, not road
quoted twenty years pass -> no aging
death rumor -> no death
reward promise -> no currency
reward payment repeated -> paid once
command/context/card text -> no canon mutation
```

## Beginner implementation order

```text
1. Copy the current V0.17 script into a working file.
2. Fix title/version/freeze metadata only if needed.
3. Add the acceptance metadata object.
4. Add acceptance status to /foundationcheck or equivalent.
5. Extend ReleaseCheck with PC01 gates.
6. Run foundation command tests.
7. Run non-canon source tests.
8. Mark accepted only if clean.
9. Save as WorldLedgerV0.17.1_FOUNDATION_ACCEPTED_FREEZE.txt.
10. Move to Production Chunk 02.
```

## Pass / warn / fail

PASS if hooks, commands, ReleaseCheck, foundationcheck, hookcheck, playerbreakercheck, regressioncheck, supportcheck, and packagingcheck pass with no unsafe active behavior.

WARN only for honest deferred systems or shell limitations.

FAIL if script does not load, exports are missing, commands crash, command/context/card text mutates state, duplicate output repeats effects, manual mutation is enabled, generated cards are enabled, live card writes are enabled by default, or ReleaseCheck fakes PASS.

## Final doctrine

```text
Accept the baseline.
Freeze the foundation.
Preserve the safety gates.
Keep deferred systems disabled.
Prove commands, hooks, parser boundaries, support layers, and duplicate locks are safe.
Then build upward.
```

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 02 — Command Surface and Player Setup Expansion

## Document status

Production sequence: 02 of 25.
Build target: WorldLedger V0.17.1 foundation accepted freeze.
Purpose: Improve command readability, setup inspection, player identity display, and disabled-system messaging without unsafe mutation commands.

## Doctrine

```text
Commands explain truth.
Commands do not create truth.
Setup inspects and confirms.
Setup does not overwrite locked identity.
Displays must separate confirmed, candidate, pending, visible, promised, and deferred states.
```

## Why this comes before gameplay expansion

If commands show visible items as owned, candidates as stable, promised rewards as paid, or travel intent as arrival, later testing becomes unreliable. This chunk makes command outputs honest before deeper systems build on them.

## Allowed changes

```text
CommandRouter
Command aliases
Command renderers
Help text
Version/build status display
Setup/startup inspection display
Sheet/status/inventory/abilities/quests/actors/location/time displays
State audit display
ReleaseCheck command honesty gates
Disabled command messages
```

Forbidden:

```text
manual mutation commands
admin mutation commands
feature activation commands
generated card commands
live card write commands
unsafe character creation mutation flow
```

## Setup philosophy

Allowed read-only setup commands:

```text
/startup /startupaudit /setup /setupcheck /sheet /version /foundationcheck
```

They may show what is known, unknown, locked, confirmed, or pending. They must not overwrite protected identity.

Controlled setup confirmation may only accept structured startup facts before gameplay lock. Post-lock identity changes require a future manual override spec.

Forbidden examples:

```text
/playername Bob mutates locked identity casually
/race Dragon instantly changes body logic
/setform Wolf overwrites true identity
/addlineage Angel-Demon grants powers
```

## Player identity display requirements

`/sheet` must separate:

```text
Confirmed identity
Current form
Body form / body type
Race/species/lineage
Origin / outsider status
Disguise / illusion / temporary presentation
Candidate or pending traits
Unknown fields
Deferred systems
```

True identity is not current form. Current form is not disguise. Disguise is not body truth. Candidate lineage is not confirmed lineage.

## Origin and world knowledge display

Do not assume human, native, chosen, central, powerful, or omniscient. Display:

```text
Origin: unknown / outsider / native / world-emerged / scenario-defined
World knowledge: limited unless discovered
Narrative role: not automatically center of reality
Integration: grows through action, recognition, and consequence
```

## Command output standard

Every command response must:

```text
start with [AIDRPG]
be clearly non-canon
avoid prose that looks like story output
avoid wording that EventScannerLite might treat as a completed event
avoid raw JSON dumps unless diagnostic mode asks
be capped for AI Dungeon context limits
```

Diagnostics should include: `No gameplay state was changed.`

## Shared command formatter

Add/verify helper responsibilities:

```text
prefix command output
standardize section headings
standardize empty-state lines
standardize confirmed/candidate/pending/deferred labels
standardize no-mutation footer
escape or avoid story-like lines
cap command length
```

## Command requirements

`/help` shows active commands, diagnostics, build checks, disabled categories, current stage, and where to start.

`/version` shows WorldLedger engine, AIDRPG project bridge, version, schema, stage, root key, foundation accepted/frozen status, release profile, safe/deferred flag summary.

`/sheet` separates confirmed identity, origin, current form, body truth, race/species, lineage, disguise/illusion, candidate/pending traits, protected fields, unknown fields.

`/status` separates resources, fatigue/exhaustion, active conditions, injuries, recovery/resolved conditions, death boundary, warnings.

`/inventory` separates owned/carried, equipped, stored, visible nearby, available loot, NPC custody, lost/consumed/destroyed history, currency, warnings.

`/abilities` separates stable, unstable, candidate, hints, failed/collapsed attempts, sealed/unavailable, recent evidence, warnings.

`/quests` separates active, offered/rumored, objectives, ready-to-turn-in, completed, failed/abandoned, promised rewards, paid rewards.

`/actors` separates present important actors, scene-only actors, persistent actors, absent/reference-only actors, witness memories, relationship notes.

`/location` separates current location, current scene, travel intent, nearby/visible references, route/interstitial references, known places.

`/time` displays current time text, turn/sequence if used, last accepted time advance, deadlines, pending/nonliteral claims, warnings. It never advances time.

## Disabled command messages

Forbidden commands must return safe disabled messages and no mutation:

```text
/additem /setlevel /payreward /completequest /giveability /teleport
/livecards /generatedcards /craft /buy /sell /reputation /domain
/absorb /evolve /partycontrol /warfare
```

Each message should explain the system is disabled, why, planned production chunk, and that no gameplay state changed.

## PC02 ReleaseCheck gates

```text
PC02-G01 all listed active commands route through CommandRouter
PC02-G02 all command output starts with [AIDRPG]
PC02-G03 commands are inspect-only
PC02-G04 disabled command messages exist for forbidden command families
PC02-G05 /sheet separates identity/current form/disguise/candidate traits
PC02-G06 /status separates fatigued/exhausted/injured/death boundary
PC02-G07 /inventory separates owned/equipped/visible/available/custody/lost/consumed/currency
PC02-G08 /abilities separates stable/unstable/candidate/hint/failed/sealed
PC02-G09 /quests separates offered/active/objectives/promised/ready/paid/completed
PC02-G10 /actors separates present/scene-only/persistent/absent/reference-only/memory
PC02-G11 /location separates current/travel intent/nearby/visible/route
PC02-G12 /time does not advance time and shows pending/nonliteral claims safely
PC02-G13 setup/startup display does not mutate locked identity
PC02-G14 aliases do not bypass command suppression
PC02-G15 command output parser-safety cues present
```

## Required tests

```text
PC02-001 Help is honest
PC02-002 Version bridge clear
PC02-003 Sheet separates identity and form
PC02-004 Unknown race is not assumed human
PC02-005 Status separates fatigue and death boundary
PC02-006 Inventory separates visible and owned
PC02-007 Abilities separate candidate and stable
PC02-008 Quests separate promise and payment
PC02-009 Actors separate reference-only
PC02-010 Location separates travel intent
PC02-011 Time command does not advance time
PC02-012 Disabled mutation command safe
PC02-013 Alias safety
PC02-014 Command output cannot become canon
PC02-015 Setup display does not unlock identity
```

## Implementation order

```text
1. Copy accepted foundation baseline to V0.17.2 command-surface working file.
2. Add shared command formatting helper.
3. Update /help and /version.
4. Update /sheet, /status, /inventory, /abilities, /quests, /actors, /location, /time.
5. Add setup display/checklist.
6. Add disabled command messages.
7. Add PC02 ReleaseCheck gates.
8. Add PC02 regression tests.
9. Feed command outputs back through Output hook to prove non-canon behavior.
10. Save as WorldLedgerV0.17.2_COMMAND_SURFACE_ACCEPTED.txt.
```

## Pass / warn / fail

PASS if all active commands route safely, all command output is prefixed, commands are inspect-only, core displays are honest, setup is non-mutating, disabled commands are safe, aliases do not bypass suppression, and PC02 tests pass.

WARN if setupcheck is folded into sheet/startupaudit, advanced display sections are empty because deferred, or CardSync controls are display-only.

FAIL if any command mutates gameplay, command output lacks suppression cues, manual mutation is active, candidate ability displays as stable, visible item displays as owned, promised reward displays as paid, travel intent displays as current location, unknown race defaults to human, setup overwrites locked identity, or command output becomes canon.

## Final doctrine

```text
If it is confirmed, label it confirmed.
If it is candidate, label it candidate.
If it is pending, label it pending.
If it is visible, do not show it as owned.
If it is promised, do not show it as paid.
If it is intended, do not show it as arrived.
If it is unknown, say unknown.
If it is disabled, say disabled.
Commands explain the world; they do not change it.
```

================================================================================================
END 40_Production_Roadmap/02_AIDRPG_Production_Chunk_02_Command_Surface_and_Player_Setup_Expansion.txt

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 03 — State Schema Deepening for Full System Readiness

## Document status

Production sequence: 03 of 25.
Build target: WorldLedger V0.17.2 command-surface accepted build.
Purpose: Deepen state schema, migration metadata, invariant scaffolding, disabled placeholders, operation ledgers, and quarantine categories before live gameplay expansion.

## Doctrine

```text
Prepare state before features.
Add structure before automation.
Add placeholders without enabling unsafe systems.
Add migration metadata before migration complexity.
Add invariant shells before high-risk reducers.
Keep old prose quarantined, not imported.
```

## Allowed changes

```text
State schema factories
StateManager default creation and missing-field repair
schema version metadata
bucket version metadata
migration metadata
quarantine categories
operation ledger bucket structure
feature flag placeholders
invariant checker shell
state audit display
ReleaseCheck schema gates
cleanup cap metadata
```

Forbidden:

```text
active economy/crafting/combat/reputation/domain/absorption/generated cards/manual mutation
old card prose gameplay import
```

## Canonical root rule

There must be one canonical root:

```text
state.worldledger
```

Do not create competing active roots such as `state.aidrpg`, `state.tasis`, `state.tas`, `state.innerSelf`, or `state.autoCards`. Old roots may be detected and quarantined as legacy evidence.

## Required top-level buckets

```text
meta features runtime startup player body progression inventory abilities quests actors places time cards operationLedger audits migration quarantine dirty cleanup schema integrity
```

StateManager adds missing safe defaults without deleting valid existing data.

## Schema metadata bucket

Recommended shape:

```text
schema: {
  schemaId: 'worldledger.schema.v1.locked',
  schemaVersion: '1.0.0',
  stateVersion: '0.17.3-schema-readiness',
  createdBy: 'WorldLedger',
  lastCheckedTurn: 0,
  lastMigrationTurn: 0,
  bucketVersions: {},
  requiredBuckets: [],
  optionalDisabledBuckets: [],
  knownLegacyRoots: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

## Bucket version metadata

Track versions for all major buckets:

```text
meta features runtime startup player body progression inventory abilities quests actors places time cards operationLedger audits migration quarantine dirty cleanup schema integrity
```

Use simple values such as `1` or `v1`.

## Deferred feature placeholders

Feature flags must distinguish active foundation systems, disabled future systems, shell diagnostics, and blocked unsafe systems. Required disabled flags:

```text
economyEnabled craftingEnabled investigationEnabled reputationEnabled domainEnabled absorptionEnabled advancedCombatEnabled warfareEnabled multiProtagonistEnabled partyAdvancedEnabled generatedCardsEnabled protectedCardWritesEnabled manualMutationCommandsEnabled = false
```

Add display metadata with planned chunk/reason, for example economy planned for 23A, crafting 23B, investigation 23C, reputation 23D, domain 23E, advanced combat 24A, party 24B, absorption 24C, multi-protagonist 24D, warfare 24E.

## Bucket readiness requirements

### Startup

```text
locked, firstGameplayInputSeen, setupSourceStatus, setupSourceKey, identityLockedAtTurn, startupOperationKeys, importedFields, unknownFields, warnings, notes
```

Post-lock setup cannot overwrite protected identity.

### Player

```text
id, displayName, aliases, identityStatus, origin, worldKnowledgeStatus, trueRaceSpecies, lineage, currentForm, bodyType, disguise, illusion, presentation, protectedFields, candidateTraits, pendingTraits, warnings, notes
```

Unknown race does not mean human. Current form does not overwrite true race/species.

### Body

```text
resources.hp/mp/ep, fatigue, conditions, injuries, deathBoundary, bodyTraits, formConstraints, warnings, notes
```

Structural clamp is not healing.

### Progression

```text
level, currentXP, totalXP, xpToNext, stats, baseStats, modifiers, derivedSnapshot, statXP, histories, formulaProfile, formulasEnabled, warnings, notes
```

Formulas remain disabled/limited until formula chunks.

### Inventory

```text
items, equipped, stored, sceneItems, custody, currency, itemHistory, warnings, notes
```

Future ItemRecord shape includes id, key, displayName, quantity, category, tags, stackable, durability, accessState, owner/custody/source metadata.

### Abilities

```text
records, stable, unstable, candidates, hints, failed, sealed, unavailable, evidenceLog, warnings, notes
```

Candidate is not stable.

### Quests

```text
records, active, offered, rumored, readyToTurnIn, completed, failed, abandoned, rewardLedger, warnings, notes
```

Promised reward is separate from paid reward.

### Actors

```text
records, sceneActors, present, absent, referenceOnly, memories, relationshipNotes, warnings, notes
```

Reference-only actor is not present.

### Places

```text
currentLocation, currentScene, records, travelIntent, visibleReferences, nearbyReferences, routeReferences, sceneHistory, warnings, notes
```

Travel intent is separate from current location.

### Time

```text
text, turn, sequence, lastAdvance, acceptedAdvances, pendingClaims, deadlines, agePolicy, warnings, notes
```

Time advance and age mutation are separate.

### Cards

```text
protectedCards, registry, dirtyCards, tombstones, duplicateWarnings, apiStatus, syncMode, lastDryRun, warnings, notes
```

Generated cards and protected live writes remain disabled.

## OperationLedger deepening

Required buckets:

```text
permanentKeys recentKeys rewardKeys cardKeys sourceUseKeys startupOperationKeys migrationOperationKeys itemKeys abilityKeys timeKeys questKeys actorKeys placeKeys progressionKeys combatKeys craftingKeys economyKeys reputationKeys
```

Disabled placeholder key buckets may exist but disabled systems must not write to them.

Durable keys must not be capped like recent noise.

## Migration metadata

```text
migration: {
  currentSchema,
  detectedSchema,
  lastMigrationStatus,
  migrationsApplied: [],
  repairLog: [],
  legacyRootsDetected: [],
  legacyPayloadsQuarantined: [],
  futureSchemaWarnings: [],
  failedMigrations: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

Migrations must be deterministic and idempotent. Write new version only after success. Failed migration preserves old payload in quarantine/backup.

## Quarantine categories

```text
legacy_root legacy_card_prose malformed_bucket invalid_resource invalid_item invalid_ability invalid_quest invalid_actor invalid_place invalid_time invalid_progression unsafe_command_text unsafe_context_text unsafe_card_text future_schema manual_edit_conflict migration_failure
```

QuarantineRecord fields:

```text
quarantineId, category, source, sourceKey, originalPreview, reason, severity, suggestedAction, status, createdTurn, notes
```

Quarantined records are inactive.

## IntegrityChecker shell

Should check required buckets, safe feature flags, numeric resources, current <= max, nonnegative item quantities, duplicate stable ability keys, duplicate active quests, deathBoundary values, generated cards disabled, manual mutation disabled, durable ledger buckets, and inactive quarantine.

It may report PASS/WARN/FAIL/QUARANTINE. It may not award, heal, pay, or repair gameplay effects.

## PC03 ReleaseCheck gates

```text
PC03-G01 one canonical state root exists
PC03-G02 required top-level buckets exist
PC03-G03 schema metadata bucket exists
PC03-G04 bucket version metadata exists
PC03-G05 deferred system placeholders are disabled
PC03-G06 startup lock metadata exists
PC03-G07 player identity/form/disguise fields prepared
PC03-G08 body resource/status/injury fields prepared
PC03-G09 progression formula fields prepared but not over-activated
PC03-G10 inventory item/access/custody fields prepared
PC03-G11 abilities status/evidence fields prepared
PC03-G12 quest objective/reward fields prepared
PC03-G13 actor presence/reference/memory fields prepared
PC03-G14 place current/travel/reference fields prepared
PC03-G15 time/deadline/age policy fields prepared
PC03-G16 card mirror registry fields prepared and generated cards disabled
PC03-G17 operation ledger durable buckets exist
PC03-G18 migration metadata exists
PC03-G19 quarantine categories and records exist
PC03-G20 integrity checker shell exists
PC03-G21 cleanup protected paths exist
PC03-G22 old prose cannot import as truth
```

## Required tests

```text
PC03-001 Single root only
PC03-002 Missing bucket repair preserves data
PC03-003 Schema metadata exists
PC03-004 Deferred placeholders disabled
PC03-005 Startup lock metadata preserved
PC03-006 Player unknown fields stay unknown
PC03-007 Body resource structural clamp does not heal
PC03-008 Negative item quantity quarantined
PC03-009 Duplicate stable ability detected
PC03-010 Quest reward fields separate promise/payment
PC03-011 Actor reference-only structure exists
PC03-012 Place travel intent separate from current location
PC03-013 Time age policy cautious
PC03-014 Card mirror flags safe
PC03-015 Durable ledger buckets protected
PC03-016 Old card prose quarantined
PC03-017 Future schema warning safe
PC03-018 Integrity command non-mutating
PC03-019 Quarantine inactive display
PC03-020 ReleaseCheck PC03 gates
```

## Implementation order

```text
1. Copy V0.17.2 accepted command-surface build.
2. Add schema metadata defaults.
3. Add bucket version map.
4. Expand StateManager required bucket repair.
5. Add deferred system metadata.
6. Deepen startup/player/body/progression/inventory/abilities/quests/actors/places/time/cards/ledger/migration/quarantine/cleanup/integrity shapes.
7. Add quarantine categories.
8. Add integrity checker shell.
9. Update schema/integrity commands.
10. Add PC03 gates and tests.
11. Run foundationcheck, releasecheck, schemaaudit, integrity, regressioncheck, supportcheck, playerbreakercheck.
12. Save as WorldLedgerV0.17.3_SCHEMA_READINESS_ACCEPTED.txt.
```

## Final doctrine

```text
State shape comes before system behavior.
Migration metadata comes before migration complexity.
Quarantine comes before salvage.
Invariant checks come before high-risk reducers.
Disabled placeholders come before feature activation.
Cleanup protection comes before long-run play.
```

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 04 — Player Identity, Body Logic, and Status Depth

## Document status

Production sequence: 04 of 25.
Build target: WorldLedger V0.17.3 schema-readiness accepted build.
Purpose: Deepen player identity, body truth, current form, conditions, injuries, resources, fatigue/exhaustion, recovery, and death-boundary behavior without identity drift or false body canon.

## Doctrine

```text
True identity is not current form.
Current form is not disguise.
Disguise is not body truth.
Rumor is not body truth.
Dream is not body truth.
Quote is not body truth.
Fatigued is not exhausted.
Unconscious is not dead.
Dying is not dead.
Recovery requires a recovery event.
Cleanup is not healing.
```

## Allowed changes

```text
Player state helpers
Body state helpers
BodyReducer
Body CanonValidator rules
Body ProposalBuilder event types
Body EventScannerLite hints
/status and /sheet renderers
ContextBuilder player/body packets
InvariantChecker body/player checks
ReleaseCheck body gates
Regression/player-breaker body tests
```

Forbidden:

```text
full combat engine
full transformation/evolution engine
absorption/devour
race/lineage power automation
advanced progression formulas
manual /setrace /setform /heal /kill
reputation/social automation from body form
full non-humanoid equipment rules
```

## Identity model requirements

Player identity must distinguish:

```text
true identity
confirmed name
aliases
origin
world knowledge status
true race/species
lineage
current form
body type
body traits
disguise
illusion
presentation
candidate traits
pending traits
rumored traits
unknown traits
```

True identity cannot be overwritten by current form, disguise, illusion, rumor, NPC quote, dream, temporary wording unless validated, command display, card prose, or context prose.

Current form is the active body presentation or validated body state. It may affect future constraints, but this chunk only prepares/display constraints unless transformation is explicitly validated.

Disguise/illusion are presentation states unless validated as actual body/form changes.

Candidate and pending traits remain unconfirmed.

## Origin and world knowledge model

Fields or equivalents:

```text
origin.status
origin.type
origin.source
worldKnowledgeStatus
worldIntegrationStatus
centralityAssumption
```

Recommended values:

```text
origin.type = unknown | native | outsider | world_emerged | scenario_defined
worldKnowledgeStatus = unknown | limited | local | trained | expert | hidden_lore_unlocked
worldIntegrationStatus = unknown | outsider | tolerated | recognized | entangled | influential
centralityAssumption = none | local_only | scenario_defined
```

Unknown origin does not mean native. Unknown race does not mean human. Limited world knowledge is default unless setup proves otherwise.

## Body resource model

Resources:

```text
hp mp ep
```

Each supports current, max, known, source, lastChangedTurn, notes. Unknown numeric resources must display unknown rather than fake values like HP 100/100.

Rules:

```text
current cannot exceed max when both known
current cannot be below 0 when known
max cannot be below 1 when known
structural clamp is not healing
recovery requires accepted recovery/rest/healing event
```

## Fatigue/exhaustion model

States:

```text
rested winded fatigued weary exhausted collapsed unknown
```

Severities:

```text
none minor moderate major critical unknown
```

Fatigued/worn out does not become exhausted unless strong wording confirms it. Exhausted/can barely stand/drained completely may become exhausted if validated. Collapsed/unable to stand may become collapsed/critical if validated.

## Condition model

Condition examples:

```text
bleeding burned poisoned sick stunned blinded deafened frozen chilled cursed infected exhausted fatigued unconscious restrained
```

ConditionRecord:

```text
id, key, displayName, family, severity, status, sourceOperationKey, sourceEventId, createdTurn, updatedTurn, expiresTurn, location, cause, evidence, notes
```

Statuses:

```text
active worsening improving resolved suppressed pending rejected
```

Condition gain requires direct validated body/status event. Resolution requires recovery/resolution event. Cleanup cannot resolve active conditions.

## Injury model

Injury examples:

```text
wolf bite on left arm
burned palm
sprained ankle
broken rib
cut cheek
arrow wound in shoulder
```

InjuryRecord:

```text
id, key, displayName, bodyPart, severity, status, bleeding, infectionRisk, sourceOperationKey, sourceEventId, createdTurn, updatedTurn, healedTurn, cause, evidence, notes
```

Threats, attempts, metaphors, dreams, quotes, and card/context/command text do not create injuries.

## Death boundary model

Allowed:

```text
alive unconscious dying dead captured unknown
```

Rules:

```text
alive is not perfect health
unconscious is not dead
dying is not dead
dead requires direct validated death outcome
captured is not dead
unknown means unknown, not dead
```

Reject death from rumor, quote, dream, metaphor, threat, insult, prophecy, card, context, command, or story title text.

## Body trait and form constraint shell

May store/display traits and constraints such as claws, wings, tail, fur, scales, no human hands, cannot speak normally, quadruped movement, large body constraints. Deep mechanics are deferred. Illusions/disguises do not create physical constraints. Candidate traits do not create confirmed constraints.

## CanonValidator body expansion

Accept direct physical/body outcomes:

```text
Your arm bleeds from the wolf bite.
Your palm is burned and blistered.
Your legs shake and you are exhausted.
The poison fades from your veins.
Your bleeding stops.
You collapse unconscious.
```

Reject unsafe/nonliteral outcomes:

```text
Mara says "you are poisoned"
People say you died
You feel dead tired
In a dream, you burn alive
The prophecy says you will die
The card says you are wounded
[AIDRPG] Status: Bleeding
```

Pending uncertain wording:

```text
You feel strange.
A chill passes through you.
You might be poisoned.
The wound could be infected.
Your form seems different.
```

## Proposal event types

```text
body.status_gain_candidate
body.status_loss_candidate
body.injury_candidate
body.resource_change_candidate
body.resource_state_candidate
body.recovery_candidate
body.death_boundary_candidate
body.form_change_candidate
body.trait_candidate
identity.trait_candidate
identity.form_candidate
identity.disguise_candidate
identity.illusion_candidate
```

## BodyReducer behavior

Status gain creates/updates ConditionRecord, sets active/severity, records operation key, marks body_status/context dirty, audits compactly.

Injury gain creates/updates InjuryRecord, body part/severity/bleeding if known, marks body_status/context dirty.

Recovery sets condition/injury improving or resolved and does not delete history immediately.

Resource change applies validated delta/state, clamps safely, records source key.

Death boundary change requires direct validated outcome, durable key, dirty targets, high-severity audit. Unsafe flags cause reject/no-op.

## Operation keys

```text
body.status.add.<statusKey>.<clauseHash>
body.status.resolve.<statusKey>.<clauseHash>
body.injury.add.<injuryKey>.<clauseHash>
body.injury.resolve.<injuryKey>.<clauseHash>
body.resource.<resourceKey>.<deltaOrState>.<clauseHash>
body.deathboundary.<newStatus>.<clauseHash>
body.form.<formKey>.<sourceKey>
identity.trait.candidate.<traitKey>.<sourceKey>
identity.presentation.<presentationKey>.<sourceKey>
```

## Dirty targets

```text
body condition/injury/resource/fatigue -> body_status, context
player identity/form/disguise/trait -> player_sheet, context
critical death boundary -> body_status, player_sheet, context, audit
```

## Command/context updates

`/sheet` displays confirmed identity, origin/world knowledge, true race/species, lineage, current form, body type, disguise/illusion/presentation, candidate/pending traits, protected fields, unknown fields.

`/status` displays resources, fatigue/exhaustion, active conditions, active injuries, recovery/resolved recent statuses, death boundary, body traits/constraints, warnings.

ContextBuilder includes concise player/body packets and clearly labels candidate/pending truth.

## PC04 ReleaseCheck gates

```text
PC04-G01 player identity/form/disguise fields separated
PC04-G02 unknown race/species not defaulted to human
PC04-G03 world knowledge starts limited/unknown unless confirmed
PC04-G04 body resource model supports known/unknown values
PC04-G05 fatigue and exhaustion are separated
PC04-G06 condition record schema exists
PC04-G07 injury record schema exists
PC04-G08 death boundary allowed values enforced
PC04-G09 body CanonValidator rejects rumor/quote/dream/metaphor death/status
PC04-G10 BodyReducer rejects unsafe routed body/death events
PC04-G11 recovery requires accepted recovery event
PC04-G12 cleanup cannot heal/remove active body state
PC04-G13 body/player dirty targets created
PC04-G14 /sheet displays confirmed/candidate/unknown fields honestly
PC04-G15 /status displays body states honestly
PC04-G16 body context packet labels candidate/pending truth correctly
PC04-G17 operation keys exist for repeat-sensitive body effects
PC04-G18 player-breaker body/death tests registered
```

## Required tests

```text
PC04-001 Unknown race not assumed human
PC04-002 Current form does not overwrite true race
PC04-003 Disguise does not overwrite body truth
PC04-004 Candidate lineage stays candidate
PC04-005 Fatigued not exhausted
PC04-006 Exhausted explicit
PC04-007 Injury created from direct physical outcome
PC04-008 Threat does not injure
PC04-009 Recovery resolves condition
PC04-010 Cleanup does not heal
PC04-011 Rumor death rejected
PC04-012 Quote death rejected
PC04-013 Dream death rejected
PC04-014 Dead tired metaphor rejected
PC04-015 Unconscious distinct from dead
PC04-016 Dying distinct from dead
PC04-017 Direct death requires explicit policy
PC04-018 Resource clamp structural only
PC04-019 Command status non-canon
PC04-020 Card body prose non-canon
PC04-021 Context body prose non-canon
PC04-022 Body duplicate key skip
PC04-023 Body dirty targets
PC04-024 Status command display clarity
```

## Implementation order

```text
1. Copy V0.17.3 schema-readiness accepted build.
2. Add/verify player and body helper functions.
3. Update Body CanonValidator rules.
4. Update ProposalBuilder body event support.
5. Update BodyReducer.
6. Update /sheet and /status.
7. Update ContextBuilder body/player packets.
8. Update IntegrityChecker body/player checks.
9. Add PC04 ReleaseCheck gates.
10. Add PC04 tests.
11. Run foundationcheck, releasecheck, integrity, playerbreakercheck, regressioncheck, status, sheet.
12. Save as WorldLedgerV0.17.4_BODY_IDENTITY_ACCEPTED.txt.
```

## Pass / warn / fail

PASS if identity/form/disguise/candidates separate, unknown race is not assumed, resources support known/unknown, fatigue/exhaustion separate, conditions/injuries persist, recovery requires accepted event, death rejects unsafe sources, BodyReducer rejects unsafe routed events, displays are honest, context is concise/labeled, duplicate keys work, tests pass.

WARN if numeric HP/MP/EP unknown, transformation consequences deferred, race/lineage powers deferred, form/equipment constraints display-only, death automation safety-limited.

FAIL if current form overwrites true race, candidate trait displays confirmed, fatigue becomes exhaustion too easily, cleanup heals active conditions, rumor/quote/dream sets death, command/context/card text creates body state, duplicate injury repeats, or BodyReducer trusts unsafe data.

## Final doctrine

```text
The player is a protected identity with a body state.
The body can be injured, tired, changed, disguised, or recovering.
But none of those truths are created by rumor, quote, dream, command text, card text, context text, or cleanup.
```

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 05 — Inventory, Equipment, Scene Items, and Custody Depth

## Document status

Production sequence: 05 of 25.
Build target: WorldLedger V0.17.4 body/identity accepted build.
Purpose: Deepen inventory, equipment, scene-item, custody, item-history, and currency safety without false item grants or unsafe economy activation.

## Doctrine

```text
Visible is not owned.
Available is not acquired.
Offered is not received.
Promised is not paid.
NPC custody is not player custody.
Dropped is not deleted.
Consumed is not carried.
Destroyed is not usable.
Dreamed is not owned.
Quoted is not owned.
Denied is not acquired.
Failed grab is not acquisition.
```

Inventory truth must be conservative, durable, and auditable.

## Allowed changes

```text
Inventory schema helpers
InventoryReducer
ItemNameSanitizer
limited RewardNameSanitizer currency support
CanonValidator inventory rules
ProposalBuilder inventory event types
EventScannerLite inventory/item hints
/inventory renderer
ContextBuilder inventory packet
OperationLedger item/currency keys
DirtyQueue inventory targets
IntegrityChecker inventory checks
ReleaseCheck inventory gates
Regression/player-breaker inventory tests
```

Forbidden:

```text
full economy buy/sell/barter/debt
full crafting/material/recipe engine
full equipment stat modifiers
full non-humanoid equipment constraints
manual /additem or /giveitem
manual /money commands
generated card item creation
old card prose item import
```

Economy and crafting remain deferred to Production Chunk 23.

## Inventory state model

Preferred shape:

```text
inventory: {
  items: {},
  equipped: {},
  stored: {},
  sceneItems: {},
  custody: {},
  currency: {},
  itemHistory: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

Arrays are acceptable if already used, but stable item keys are required.

## ItemRecord schema

```text
ItemRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  normalizedName,
  quantity,
  category,
  tags,
  stackable,
  unique,
  durability,
  accessState,
  owner,
  holder,
  custodyKey,
  locationKey,
  equippedSlot,
  sourceOperationKey,
  sourceEventId,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  historyKeys,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Keys are stable and family-scoped. They must not contain full raw output, quotes, dreams, command text, or card/context prose.

Quantity must be positive for active records. Negative quantity quarantines or repairs safely without granting new items. Unique items do not stack unless policy says so.

## AccessState model

```text
owned carried equipped stored visible available_loot offered npc_custody place_custody inaccessible lost consumed destroyed unknown
```

Owned/carried/equipped/stored are player access states.

Visible/available/offered are not owned.

NPC/place custody and inaccessible are not player-owned.

Lost/consumed/destroyed are not usable.

Unknown is not owned by default.

## Equipment shell

Equipment is a view/state of inventory, not a separate truth source.

Slots:

```text
mainHand offHand head body hands feet ring1 ring2 neck back customSlots
```

Equipped item must exist as owned/carried/equipped. Equipping requires accepted equip event or safe startup source. Equipment stat modifiers are deferred. Body/form equipment constraints are warnings/context labels only in this chunk.

## SceneItemRecord

```text
SceneItemRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  quantity,
  accessState,
  locationKey,
  sceneKey,
  holderKey,
  visibility,
  reachable,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  expiresTurn,
  notes
}
```

Scene items are not player inventory. Visible/available items become inventory only through accepted acquisition/handoff/take events.

## CustodyRecord

```text
CustodyRecord {
  id,
  itemKey,
  itemDisplayName,
  holderType,
  holderKey,
  holderDisplayName,
  accessState,
  locationKey,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Holder types: actor, place, container, unknown, player. NPC custody is not player-owned. Transfer requires accepted transfer/handoff/acquisition.

## Currency model

Currency must be explicit and duplicate-safe. Acceptable simple shape:

```text
currency: { gold: 0, silver: 0, copper: 0, custom: {} }
```

or record shape with amount/source keys.

Rules:

```text
promised reward does not increase currency
ready reward does not increase currency
paid reward may increase currency only once
buy/sell/spend is deferred until economy mini-spec
```

## ItemHistoryRecord

```text
ItemHistoryRecord {
  historyId,
  itemKey,
  eventType,
  fromAccessState,
  toAccessState,
  actorKey,
  placeKey,
  operationKey,
  turn,
  summary,
  notes
}
```

Preserve unique item history. Compact/cap only through safe cleanup policy.

## Categories/tags

Categories:

```text
weapon armor shield tool key consumable quest_item currency treasure material book container food drink clothing jewelry misc unknown
```

Tags are descriptive only. Magical does not grant ability. Quest item does not complete quest. Material does not enable crafting yet.

## ItemNameSanitizer depth

Must handle:

```text
the rusted iron key, but the goblin snatches it away -> rusted iron key
red healing potion from Mira -> red healing potion
three silver arrows on the table -> silver arrows + quantity 3 if supported
jeweled crown that Mira refuses to give you -> jeweled crown
idea of a brass key -> brass key with abstract/metaphorical flag
```

Preserve meaningful modifiers: rusted, iron, red healing, jeweled, silver, old wooden, masterwork, cracked, black gate, cellar.

## Inventory CanonValidator expansion

Accept owned acquisition only when safe output/startup source confirms actual acquisition/handoff/receipt/pickup, target is bound, no denial/failure/revocation/hypothetical/quote/dream/abstract/visible/available/offered/custody/attempt-only flag, and operation key is not duplicate.

Reject ownership when any unsafe flag is present:

```text
deniedByLocalClause failedOrRevoked abstractOrMetaphorical quotedSpeech dreamOrVision visibleOnly availableOnly offeredOnly futureIntent attemptOnly command/context/card/audit/debug source
```

Use pending/scene item/custody for:

```text
You see a silver ring on the table.
The goblin drops a rusty dagger.
Mara offers you the key.
The chest contains a potion.
The jeweled crown rests behind glass.
```

## Mixed clause rule

```text
Mira refuses to give you the jeweled crown, but she hands you the red healing potion.
```

Expected:

```text
jeweled crown not owned
red healing potion acquired once if otherwise safe
```

Do not apply the handoff verb to the denied item or the denial to the accepted potion.

## Proposal event types

```text
inventory.acquire_candidate
inventory.handoff_candidate
inventory.transfer_candidate
inventory.drop_candidate
inventory.lose_candidate
inventory.consume_candidate
inventory.destroy_candidate
inventory.visible_candidate
inventory.available_loot_candidate
inventory.offered_candidate
inventory.custody_candidate
inventory.inaccessible_candidate
inventory.equip_candidate
inventory.unequip_candidate
currency.gain_candidate
currency.promise_candidate
currency.ready_candidate
currency.payment_candidate
```

## InventoryReducer behavior

Acquisition/handoff commit:

```text
1. Confirm accepted validation result.
2. Confirm safe source and no unsafe routed flags.
3. Confirm item target key/displayName.
4. Check operation key.
5. If duplicate, skipped_duplicate.
6. Add or merge item by stack/unique policy.
7. Set accessState carried/owned unless equip/stored validated.
8. Set owner/holder player.
9. Add item history.
10. Add operation key.
11. Mark dirty targets.
12. Audit.
```

Scene items update sceneItems and never inventory.items.

Custody updates custody records and never owned items unless player holder confirmed.

Consume/drop/lose/destroy update accessState/history rather than deleting immediately.

Equip/unequip shell requires item already owned/carried and accepted equip event. No stat bonuses.

## Currency and reward payment split

Preferred split-owner policy:

```text
QuestReducer handles reward status.
InventoryReducer handles currency changes.
```

Reward promise does not pay. Reward ready does not pay. Payment creates currency once. Repeated output skips. `/quests` display cannot pay.

## Operation keys

```text
item.acquire.<itemKey>.<sourceOrScene>.<clauseHash>
item.handoff.<itemKey>.<fromActor>.<toPlayer>.<clauseHash>
item.transfer.<itemKey>.<from>.<to>.<clauseHash>
item.visible.<itemKey>.<placeOrScene>.<clauseHash>
item.available.<itemKey>.<placeOrScene>.<clauseHash>
item.custody.<itemKey>.<holderKey>.<clauseHash>
item.consume.<itemKey>.<clauseHash>
item.destroy.<itemKey>.<clauseHash>
item.drop.<itemKey>.<clauseHash>
item.lose.<itemKey>.<clauseHash>
item.equip.<itemKey>.<slot>.<clauseHash>
item.unequip.<itemKey>.<slot>.<clauseHash>
currency.gain.<amount>.<currencyType>.<sourceKey>.<clauseHash>
reward.currency.pay.<questKey>.<payerKey>.<amount>.<currencyType>
```

## Dirty targets

```text
owned item/currency/equipment -> inventory_equipment, context
scene item -> inventory_equipment, current_location, context
custody -> inventory_equipment, context, maybe current_location
reward payment currency -> inventory_equipment, quest_log, context
```

## /inventory display

Required sections:

```text
Owned / Carried
Equipped
Stored
Visible Nearby
Available Loot
Offered
NPC / Place Custody
Lost / Consumed / Destroyed
Currency
Warnings
```

Visible, available, custody, promised, lost, consumed, destroyed must never display as currently owned/usable.

## Context packet

Include important owned items, equipment, quest items, relevant visible/available items, relevant custody facts, and relevant currency. Never include visible items as owned, promised rewards as currency, or quarantined old claims as truth.

## PC05 ReleaseCheck gates

```text
PC05-G01 ItemRecord schema exists or equivalent stable item record exists
PC05-G02 AccessState model separates owned/visible/available/offered/custody/lost/consumed/destroyed
PC05-G03 SceneItemRecord schema exists
PC05-G04 CustodyRecord schema exists
PC05-G05 Currency model exists and promise/payment separation is enforced
PC05-G06 ItemHistoryRecord or compact history exists
PC05-G07 ItemNameSanitizer preserves meaningful modifiers and flags abstract items
PC05-G08 CanonValidator rejects denied/failed/dream/quote/abstract/visible-only false grants
PC05-G09 mixed clause inventory split is covered
PC05-G10 InventoryReducer rejects unsafe routed inventory events
PC05-G11 scene items do not become owned inventory
PC05-G12 custody items do not become owned inventory
PC05-G13 reward/currency duplicate keys exist
PC05-G14 /inventory displays access states honestly
PC05-G15 command/context/card inventory text cannot become canon
PC05-G16 cleanup does not delete active inventory/durable keys
PC05-G17 equipment shell does not apply stat bonuses yet
PC05-G18 economy/crafting remain disabled
PC05-G19 player-breaker inventory tests registered
```

## Required tests

```text
PC05-001 Denied key no item
PC05-002 Failed grab no item
PC05-003 Abstract item no item
PC05-004 Dream item no item
PC05-005 Quoted handoff no item
PC05-006 Mixed clause split
PC05-007 Visible item scene only
PC05-008 Available loot not owned
PC05-009 Chest contains item not owned
PC05-010 Handoff acquired once
PC05-011 Handoff duplicate skipped
PC05-012 Currency promise no payment
PC05-013 Currency payment once
PC05-014 Currency payment duplicate skipped
PC05-015 Command inventory non-canon
PC05-016 Story Card item prose non-canon
PC05-017 Context item prose non-canon
PC05-018 Negative quantity quarantined
PC05-019 Equipped item must be owned
PC05-020 Equip shell no stat bonus
PC05-021 Consumed item not carried
PC05-022 Lost item not usable
PC05-023 Destroyed item not usable
PC05-024 Inventory display honest
PC05-025 Economy commands disabled
```

## Implementation order

```text
1. Copy V0.17.4 body/identity accepted build.
2. Add inventory helper functions.
3. Deepen ItemNameSanitizer.
4. Update inventory CanonValidator rules.
5. Update ProposalBuilder inventory event support.
6. Update InventoryReducer.
7. Update /inventory.
8. Update ContextBuilder inventory packet.
9. Update IntegrityChecker inventory checks.
10. Add PC05 ReleaseCheck gates.
11. Add PC05 tests.
12. Run foundationcheck, releasecheck, integrity, playerbreakercheck, regressioncheck, inventory.
13. Save as WorldLedgerV0.17.5_INVENTORY_DEPTH_ACCEPTED.txt.
```

## Pass / warn / fail

PASS if access states are separated, false grants rejected, mixed clauses split, scene/custody items not owned, currency promise/payment separated, duplicates prevented, InventoryReducer rejects unsafe data, display is honest, command/context/card item text non-canon, cleanup preserves active/durable data, tests pass.

WARN if equipment shell is display-only, category/tags are display-only, currency is simple but duplicate-safe, economy/crafting remain disabled, non-humanoid equipment constraints are deferred.

FAIL if visible/offered/available becomes owned, failed/denied/dream/quote/abstract becomes owned, custody item becomes player-owned, reward promise pays currency, reward duplicates, command/context/card creates item/currency, negative quantity becomes grant, cleanup deletes active item/durable reward key, buy/sell/craft mutates, or InventoryReducer trusts unsafe data.

## Final doctrine

```text
An item mention is not inventory.
A visible item is not inventory.
A promised reward is not currency.
A custody item is not owned.
A failed acquisition is not acquisition.
Only accepted, validated, duplicate-safe inventory events change player inventory.
```

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 06 — Ability Candidate, Training, Use, Failure, and Stability Depth

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 06 — Ability Candidate, Training, Use, Failure, and Stability Depth  
**Production sequence:** 06 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.5 inventory-depth accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen ability records, training evidence, use evidence, failed attempts, candidate/unstable/stable separation, naming safety, duplicate-use protection, and ability command/context display without false ability unlocks.  
**Status:** Core gameplay-depth production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 05 — Inventory, Equipment, Scene Items, and Custody Depth.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 06 exists because ability handling was one of the easiest places for old builds to become corrupted.

The system must never again treat every ability mention, output title, quote, dream, failed attempt, or training phrase as stable mastery.

Abilities must grow through validated evidence.

Training can create candidates. Repeated use can build evidence. Failures can be recorded without becoming unlocks. Only validated stabilization can create a stable ability.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Mentioned is not learned.
Named is not mastered.
Tried is not stable.
Failed is not unlocked.
Pretended is not usable.
Quoted is not known.
Dreamed is not real mastery.
Training is evidence, not automatic mastery.
Output-only “You learned X” is not enough by default.
Only validated stabilization creates a stable ability.
```

Ability truth must be conservative, evidence-based, duplicate-safe, and explainable.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 05 made inventory reliable. Abilities come next because later combat, progression, race/lineage powers, spellcasting, cooldowns, failures, quest abilities, and world-threat interactions all depend on clean ability state.

If abilities are not safe now, later systems will repeatedly create junk powers, duplicate evidence, false mastery, and broken progression.

---

# 3. What this chunk may change

Allowed changes:

```text
Ability schema helpers
AbilityReducer
AbilityNameSanitizer
CanonValidator ability rules
ProposalBuilder ability event types
EventScannerLite ability/training/use hints
CommandRenderers /abilities
ContextBuilder ability packet
OperationLedger ability keys
DirtyQueue ability targets
IntegrityChecker ability checks
ReleaseCheck ability gates
Regression/player-breaker ability tests
```

Allowed additions:

```text
AbilityRecord schema depth
AbilityEvidenceRecord schema
ability status model
training evidence model
use success/failure evidence model
reliability labels
cost/cooldown labels as descriptive shells
stabilization requirement model
sealed/unavailable status handling
ability audit summaries
```

Forbidden additions in this chunk:

```text
full magic economy
full cooldown/resource math
race/lineage power automation
absorption/evolution power gain
manual /giveability or /learn commands
combat damage automation
stat gain from ability training
generated ability Story Cards
old card prose ability import
```

Progression/stat effects remain deferred to later chunks.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
AbilityState helpers
AbilityReducer
AbilityNameSanitizer
CanonValidator ability rules
ProposalBuilder ability proposals
EventScannerLite ability hints
Resolver ability target binding
CommandRenderers: /abilities
ContextBuilderLite ability packet
OperationLedger ability keys
DirtyQueue ability dirty targets
AuditLogger ability lines
InvariantChecker ability checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC06 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
ProgressionReducer except no-direct-XP guard
BodyReducer except cost/resource effects remain deferred unless explicitly validated
InventoryReducer except training tools/foci as references only
QuestReducer except quest-granted ability remains deferred unless validated
CombatReducer because advanced combat is not active yet
WorldBible import
CardSync live writes
manual mutation commands
```

---

# 5. Ability state model

The `abilities` bucket should support:

```text
records
stable
unstable
candidates
hints
failed
sealed
unavailable
evidenceLog
warnings
notes
```

Recommended shape:

```text
abilities: {
  records: {},
  stable: [],
  unstable: [],
  candidates: [],
  hints: [],
  failed: [],
  sealed: [],
  unavailable: [],
  evidenceLog: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

If older code uses arrays, keyed records are still preferred. If converting to maps is risky, keep arrays but require stable ability keys and dedupe helpers.

---

# 6. AbilityRecord schema

Required fields:

```text
AbilityRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  normalizedName,
  status,
  family,
  sourceType,
  evidence,
  evidenceScore,
  useCount,
  successCount,
  failureCount,
  trainingCount,
  reliability,
  costLabels,
  cooldownLabels,
  sealedReason,
  unavailableReason,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  lastUsedTurn,
  lastTrainedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
key is stable and family-scoped
key does not contain full raw output
key does not contain training purpose text unless it is truly part of the name
key does not contain quote/dream/command text
key does not contain item/place/object text from non-ability actions
displayName is human-readable
normalizedName is resolver-safe
sourceOperationKeys record the evidence that created/updated the record
```

Allowed statuses:

```text
hint
candidate
unstable
stable
sealed
unavailable
failed_only
unknown
```

Status rules:

```text
hint is not usable
candidate is not stable
unstable may be attempted but unreliable if gameplay rules allow
failed_only is not usable mastery
sealed means known but blocked/unusable
unavailable means mentioned or known as unavailable
stable requires explicit validated stabilization
```

---

# 7. AbilityEvidenceRecord schema

Required shape:

```text
AbilityEvidenceRecord {
  evidenceId,
  abilityKey,
  evidenceType,
  result,
  confidence,
  sourceOperationKey,
  sourceEventId,
  sourceTurn,
  localClauseSummary,
  safetyFlags,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Evidence types:

```text
training
use_attempt
use_success
use_failure
teacher_confirmation
controlled_demonstration
source_grant
seal
unavailable_claim
hint
rumor
```

Results:

```text
supports_candidate
supports_unstable
supports_stable
supports_failure
supports_sealed
supports_unavailable
noncanon_reference
rejected
```

Evidence is not always mastery.

---

# 8. Ability status transition model

## Hint to candidate

Allowed when the ability is mentioned with plausible training/use relevance, the player trains toward it, or a teacher/source discusses it as a real technique. No stable mastery yet.

## Candidate to unstable

Allowed when repeated training evidence exists, first unstable manifestation/use success exists, or validator marks unstable/candidate manifestation.

## Candidate/unstable to stable

Allowed only when CanonValidator accepts `ability.stabilize_candidate` or equivalent.

Strong sources may include:

```text
validated startup structured ability
teacher/training culmination plus demonstration
successful controlled manifestation
validated source-grant/transformation event
future mini-spec source such as absorption/evolution when enabled
```

## Any status to sealed/unavailable

Allowed from direct validated sealing/unavailability event or setup marking. Sealed/unavailable does not delete history.

---

# 9. AbilityNameSanitizer depth

AbilityNameSanitizer must be owner-specific and must not leak into shared cleanup.

It must clean:

```text
I train Ember Ward to protect allies from heat -> Ember Ward
I practice Frost Step by moving across slick stones -> Frost Step
I drill Stoneguard until my stance holds under impact -> Stoneguard
I train Moonblade by practicing careful crescent cuts -> Moonblade
I refine Crimson Lotus Fang through repeated strikes -> Crimson Lotus Fang
```

It must preserve meaningful ability names:

```text
Shield Against Dawn
Step Across Stars
Song for the Dead
Blade of the North
Ward from Below
```

It must not create abilities from non-ability actions:

```text
I travel to the smithy -> no smithy ability
I inspect the old wooden door -> no door ability
I use the iron key on the cellar door -> no key/door ability
I ask Mara about Moonblade -> hint/reference only unless training/use context exists
```

---

# 10. Ability context detection

Ability context exists when local clause includes clear ability/training/use language:

```text
train/practice/drill/refine/work on/meditate on/spar with
use/cast/channel/activate/manifest
spell/skill/technique/power/ability/talent/passive
learn/unlock/master/stabilize/seal/unseal
```

Weak mention is not enough for mastery:

```text
Mara mentions Moonblade.
The book is titled Crimson Lotus Fang.
A rumor speaks of Azure Dragon Step.
```

These can become hints/references, not stable abilities.

---

# 11. Ability CanonValidator expansion

Accept training evidence only when:

```text
source is safe input intent or safe story output
local clause has clear training/practice/refinement intent
ability target is bound by AbilityNameSanitizer
not pretend-only
not quoted-only
not dream-only
not denied/no usable technique
not command/context/card/audit/debug source
```

Training normally creates or updates candidate/evidence. Training does not create stable mastery by default.

Accept use success evidence only when:

```text
safe story output confirms ability use or manifestation succeeded
ability target is bound
not dream/quote/pretend/metaphor only
not failed/collapsed/no effect
operation key not duplicate
```

Use success may add evidence and reliability, but it does not automatically stabilize unless a separate stabilize result is accepted.

Accept use failure evidence when:

```text
safe input/output states attempt/failure/collapse/no effect
ability target is bound
not command/context/card source
```

Failure evidence must not create stable mastery.

Accept stable ability only when:

```text
structured startup ability before lock
validated teacher/training culmination
successful controlled demonstration with stabilization wording
validated grant/source event
future enabled mini-spec event
```

Reject stable ability when:

```text
output-only unsupported “You learned X”
quoted instruction only
dream/vision only
pretend use
failed/collapsed/no effect
scroll/book/title only
rumor only
manual command output
context/card/audit/debug source
input attempt only with no output support
non-ability target
wrong sanitizer used
```

---

# 12. Critical old-failure rules

## Output-only learning rule

Example:

```text
You learned Moonblade.
```

Default result:

```text
pending or rejected unsupported learning claim
no stable ability
no direct AbilityReducer stable commit
```

It may become evidence only if surrounding context supports it through training arc, teacher confirmation, demonstration, startup, or source-grant.

## Failed ability rule

Examples:

```text
You attempt Void Lantern Veil, but it collapses.
Azure Dragon Step flickers and fails.
Your spell fizzles with no effect.
```

Expected:

```text
failure evidence recorded
status may remain candidate/unstable/failed_only
stable ability not created
```

## Pretend / quote / dream rules

```text
You pretend to use Azure Dragon Step. -> no real ability use
Mira says, "Use Hollow Star Palm." -> hint/reference only
In a dream, you master Star Chain. -> no real stable ability
```

---

# 13. Ability ProposalBuilder event types

Add or verify:

```text
ability.hint_candidate
ability.train_intent
ability.train_candidate
ability.use_attempt
ability.use_success_candidate
ability.use_failure_candidate
ability.evidence_candidate
ability.output_only_learning_claim
ability.learn_candidate
ability.stabilize_candidate
ability.seal_candidate
ability.unavailable_candidate
ability.failed_only_candidate
```

Current chunk may safely commit hints, candidates, training evidence, use attempt evidence, use success evidence, use failure evidence, failed-only, sealed/unavailable when directly validated, and stable only from strict validated stabilize/source event.

---

# 14. EventScannerLite ability hints

EventScannerLite may detect ability/training/use hints, but detection is not canon.

Allowed scan families:

```text
training phrases
practice phrases
spell/skill/technique/power words
use/cast/channel/activate/manifest phrases
learn/unlock/master/stabilize phrases
failed/fizzle/collapse/no effect phrases
seal/unavailable phrases
```

Must flag:

```text
quotedSpeech
dreamOrVision
rumorOrClaim
pretendOnly
failedOrRevoked
noEffect
outputOnlyLearning
hypothetical
futureIntent
attemptOnly
nonAbilityContext
commandSource
contextSource
cardSource
auditSource
```

Parser cannot mutate abilities.

---

# 15. AbilityReducer detailed behavior

AbilityReducer owns:

```text
abilities.records
abilities.stable
abilities.unstable
abilities.candidates
abilities.hints
abilities.failed
abilities.sealed
abilities.unavailable
abilities.evidenceLog
abilities.warnings
```

Hint commit:

```text
create/update ability hint record
status hint unless stronger existing status exists
record evidence type hint
not make usable/stable
mark abilities/context dirty
audit compactly
```

Training commit:

```text
create/update ability record
status candidate unless existing status stronger
append training evidence
increment trainingCount
record sourceOperationKey
mark abilities/context dirty
audit compactly
```

Use success commit:

```text
append use_success evidence
increment useCount and successCount
increase reliability label if policy exists
keep status candidate/unstable unless stabilize event is accepted
mark abilities/context dirty
```

Use failure commit:

```text
append use_failure evidence
increment useCount and failureCount
set or keep status candidate/unstable/failed_only as appropriate
not create stable ability
mark abilities/context dirty
```

Stabilize commit:

```text
require validationResult.eventType = ability.stabilize_candidate or equivalent
check durable operation key
set status stable
add to stable list
remove from candidate/unstable display lists if needed
preserve evidence history
mark abilities/context dirty
audit high-importance line
```

Sealed/unavailable commit:

```text
set status sealed or unavailable
record reason
preserve evidence/history
mark abilities/context dirty
```

---

# 16. AbilityReducer defensive guards

AbilityReducer must reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
quoted-only stable creation
dream-only stable creation
pretend-only stable creation
failed/no-effect stable creation
output-only unsupported stable creation
non-ability target
wrong sanitizer used
missing ability key
```

Reducer should not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 17. Reliability, cost, and cooldown shells

This chunk may add descriptive reliability labels:

```text
unknown
unproven
in_training
unstable
inconsistent
reliable
mastered
sealed
unavailable
```

Rules:

```text
reliability label is display/support only unless later mechanics use it
label does not replace ability status
failure evidence can lower label
success evidence can improve label
stable status still requires stabilization rules
```

Cost/cooldown labels are descriptive only:

```text
costLabels: ['drains stamina']
cooldownLabels: ['requires focus before reuse']
```

No numeric resource drain or cooldown enforcement unless future Body/Progression/resource systems explicitly validate and enable it.

---

# 18. Ability operation keys

Required operation keys:

```text
ability.hint.<abilityKey>.<sourceHash>
ability.train.<abilityKey>.<clauseHash>
ability.evidence.<abilityKey>.<evidenceType>.<clauseHash>
ability.use.<abilityKey>.<resultKind>.<outputHash>
ability.failure.<abilityKey>.<clauseHash>
ability.stabilize.<abilityKey>.<sourceKey>
ability.seal.<abilityKey>.<sourceKey>
ability.unavailable.<abilityKey>.<sourceKey>
```

Rules:

```text
stabilize keys are durable
training/use evidence keys are recent or durable depending importance
duplicate use evidence should not spam counts on replay
output-only rejected claim should not create stable key
```

---

# 19. Dirty targets and context

Ability updates should mark:

```text
abilities
context
player_sheet if stable ability materially changes player sheet
body_status only if validated ability cost/body effect occurs later
```

ContextBuilder should include concise ability facts, not the full evidence log.

Example packet:

```text
Abilities: Ember Ward is a candidate under training, not stable. Void Lantern Veil collapsed during an attempt and is not unlocked.
```

Do not include hints as stable powers, failed attempts as usable abilities, quoted/dream/rumor abilities as real mastery, or quarantined ability claims.

---

# 20. `/abilities` display requirements

Required sections:

```text
Stable abilities
Unstable abilities
Candidate abilities
Hints / mentions
Training evidence
Failed or collapsed attempts
Sealed / unavailable abilities
Warnings
```

Display rules:

```text
candidate abilities are not listed as stable
hints are not listed as usable
failed attempts are not listed as learned
sealed/unavailable abilities are not listed as usable unless later unsealed
output-only unsupported claims are displayed as pending/rejected evidence, not stable
```

Example:

```text
[AIDRPG] Abilities

Stable:
- None recorded

Candidate:
- Ember Ward — training evidence recorded; not stable yet

Failed / collapsed:
- Void Lantern Veil — attempt collapsed; no stable ability unlocked

Hints:
- Moonblade — unsupported learning claim; not confirmed stable
```

---

# 21. IntegrityChecker and Cleanup protections

IntegrityChecker must check:

```text
no duplicate stable ability keys
candidate/stable lists match record statuses
hint does not appear in stable list
failed_only does not appear in stable list
sealed/unavailable abilities not displayed as usable
ability records have stable keys
ability keys not polluted by training suffixes
ability records do not come from command/card/context source
output-only learning claim not stable unless validation source exists
```

Cleanup may compact low-value evidence or rejected-claim audits, but must not delete stable abilities, sealed/unavailable histories, important candidate training evidence, durable stabilization keys, or source evidence needed for audit.

Cleanup must not promote candidates to stable or remove failures to make an ability look cleaner.

---

# 22. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC06 gates:

```text
PC06-G01 AbilityRecord schema exists or equivalent stable record exists
PC06-G02 AbilityEvidenceRecord schema exists or equivalent evidence log exists
PC06-G03 ability status model separates hint/candidate/unstable/stable/sealed/unavailable/failed_only
PC06-G04 AbilityNameSanitizer is owner-specific and does not live in shared cleaner
PC06-G05 training suffix cleanup works for Ember Ward/Frost Step/Stoneguard examples
PC06-G06 non-ability actions do not create abilities
PC06-G07 CanonValidator accepts training as evidence/candidate, not mastery
PC06-G08 output-only learning claim does not create stable ability by default
PC06-G09 failed/pretend/quote/dream ability claims do not create stable abilities
PC06-G10 AbilityReducer rejects unsafe routed ability events
PC06-G11 stabilization requires explicit validated stabilize/source event
PC06-G12 duplicate ability evidence/use/stabilization keys exist
PC06-G13 /abilities displays candidate/stable/failed/sealed honestly
PC06-G14 command/context/card ability text cannot become canon
PC06-G15 cleanup does not delete stable abilities or promote candidates
PC06-G16 progression/stat gains from ability training remain deferred unless separately validated
PC06-G17 player-breaker ability tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if output-only, failed, pretend, quoted, dreamed, command, card, or context ability claims can become stable abilities.

# 27. Beginner implementation order

1. Copy `WorldLedgerV0.17.5_INVENTORY_DEPTH_ACCEPTED.txt` to `WorldLedgerV0.17.6_ABILITY_DEPTH_WORKING.txt`.
2. Add/verify ability helper functions: `ensureAbilitiesShape`, `makeAbilityRecord`, `makeAbilityEvidenceRecord`, `normalizeAbilityStatus`, `normalizeReliabilityLabel`, `isStableAbilityStatus`, `isUnsafeAbilitySource`, and `shouldPreserveAbilityNamePhrase`.
3. Deepen AbilityNameSanitizer and verify it does not affect shared/item/place cleanup.
4. Update ability CanonValidator rules for training, use attempt, use success, use failure, output-only learning, stabilization, sealed, and unavailable.
5. Update ProposalBuilder ability support so proposals carry ability target, evidence type, result kind, source/localClause/safety flags, sanitizerUsed, and operation key suggestion.
6. Update AbilityReducer to safely commit hint, candidate/training evidence, use success evidence, use failure evidence, stabilization, sealed, and unavailable.
7. Update `/abilities` display.
8. Update ContextBuilder ability packet.
9. Update IntegrityChecker ability checks.
10. Add PC06-G01 through PC06-G17 ReleaseCheck gates.
11. Add PC06-001 through PC06-025 tests.
12. Run `/foundationcheck`, `/releasecheck`, `/integrity`, `/playerbreakercheck`, `/regressioncheck`, and `/abilities`.
13. Save accepted build as `WorldLedgerV0.17.6_ABILITY_DEPTH_ACCEPTED.txt`.


# 29. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 06 passes if:

```text
ability statuses are separated
training creates evidence/candidate, not mastery
output-only learning does not create stable ability
failed/pretend/quote/dream ability claims do not create stable ability
AbilityNameSanitizer cleans ability suffixes without global cleanup leaks
AbilityReducer rejects unsafe routed ability events
stabilization requires explicit validated stabilize/source event
duplicate evidence/use/stabilization keys work
/abilities displays candidate/stable/failed/sealed honestly
command/context/card ability text cannot become canon
cleanup preserves stable/candidate/failure truth safely
PC06 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC06 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
cost/cooldown labels are descriptive only
resource costs are deferred
progression XP/stat effects are deferred
race/lineage powers are deferred
combat ability effects are deferred
stabilization threshold is conservative
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
You learned Moonblade creates stable Moonblade by default
failed/pretend/quote/dream ability becomes stable
travel/inspect/use-object creates junk ability
ability cleanup breaks item/place names
candidate displays as stable
hint displays as usable
training awards XP directly
command/context/card text creates ability
duplicate stabilization or evidence spams records
cleanup deletes stable ability or promotes candidate
AbilityReducer trusts unsafe routed data
```

---

# 30. Handoff to Production Chunk 07

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 07 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 07 — Quest, Objective, Reward, and Promise/Payment Depth
```

Production Chunk 07 may use ability facts for quest objectives such as “train Ember Ward” or “stabilize Moonblade,” but should not let quest text create stable abilities without ability validation.

Production Chunk 06 must leave abilities reliable enough that future chunks can ask:

```text
Is this ability only a hint?
Is it a candidate?
Was it tried and failed?
Is it unstable?
Is it stable?
Is it sealed or unavailable?
What evidence supports that status?
```

---

# 31. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 06 is where ability truth stops being a string-matching accident.

Final rule:

```text
Abilities are records with evidence and status.
Training is evidence.
Use is evidence.
Failure is evidence.
Hints are not powers.
Candidates are not mastery.
Stable abilities require validation.
No unsafe source can create a power.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen quests, objectives, and rewards in Production Chunk 07.
