# AIDRPG Production Chunk 07 — Quest, Objective, Reward, and Promise/Payment Depth

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 07 — Quest, Objective, Reward, and Promise/Payment Depth  
**Production sequence:** 07 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.6 ability-depth accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen quest records, objective states, quest offers, quest acceptance, objective progress, completion, reward promises, reward readiness, reward payment, and duplicate-safe reward behavior without rumor activation or promise/payment confusion.  
**Status:** Core gameplay-depth production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 06 — Ability Candidate, Training, Use, Failure, and Stability Depth.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 07 exists because quest and reward handling is one of the most dangerous RPG automation areas.

Old systems often blurred:

```text
rumor
offer
accepted quest
active objective
objective progress
objective completion
ready to turn in
quest completion
promised reward
paid reward
duplicate paid reward
```

That cannot happen in the final system.

A quest must move through explicit states.

Rewards must be promised, ready, paid, denied, disputed, or duplicate-skipped with clear audit.

A promised reward is not currency.

A completed objective is not payment.

A command display is not reward collection.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Rumor is not a quest.
Offer is not acceptance.
Acceptance is not completion.
Progress is not completion.
Completion is not payment.
Ready reward is not paid reward.
Promised gold is not currency.
Paid once means paid once.
Quest text is not inventory.
Command display is not reward collection.
```

Quest and reward truth must be stateful, duplicate-safe, and explainable.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 05 made inventory/currency safe.

Production Chunk 06 made abilities safe.

Quest/reward depth comes next because it depends on both:

```text
quest item custody and ownership
currency payment through InventoryReducer
ability training objectives
body injury/recovery objectives
actor quest givers
location objectives
future progression XP rewards
future reputation/faction/domain consequences
```

If quest and reward states are not safe now, future systems will duplicate rewards, activate rumor quests, auto-complete objectives, and hide broken progression behind command displays.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 07 may change:

```text
Quest schema helpers
QuestReducerLite
Reward handling helpers
QuestObjectiveSanitizer
RewardNameSanitizer
CanonValidator quest/reward rules
ProposalBuilder quest/reward event types
EventScannerLite quest/reward hints
CommandRenderers /quests
ContextBuilder quest packet
OperationLedger quest/reward keys
DirtyQueue quest targets
IntegrityChecker quest/reward checks
ReleaseCheck quest/reward gates
Regression/player-breaker quest/reward tests
```

Production Chunk 07 may add:

```text
QuestRecord schema depth
ObjectiveRecord schema
RewardRecord schema
QuestStatus model
ObjectiveStatus model
RewardStatus model
quest giver/turn-in actor references
quest location references
quest progress history
reward promise/readiness/payment states
reward duplicate ledger
split-result reward payment contract with InventoryReducer
quest audit summaries
```

Production Chunk 07 may not add:

```text
full reputation automation
full economy buy/sell/barter/debt
full crafting quest material system
full progression XP/level automation unless explicit shell only
manual /completequest /payreward commands
quest generation engine
generated quest Story Cards
old card prose quest import
```

Progression, reputation, domain, economy, crafting, and generated cards remain deferred unless specifically scoped later.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
QuestState helpers
QuestReducerLite
Reward helpers
QuestObjectiveSanitizer
RewardNameSanitizer
CanonValidator quest/reward rules
ProposalBuilder quest/reward proposals
EventScannerLite quest/reward hints
Resolver quest/reward target binding
CommandRenderers: /quests and reward references in /inventory
ContextBuilderLite quest packet
OperationLedger quest/reward keys
DirtyQueue quest dirty targets
AuditLogger quest/reward lines
InvariantChecker quest/reward checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC07 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
InventoryReducer except explicit split-result currency gain contract
ProgressionReducer except XP reward shell/deferred display
ActorReducer except quest giver/turn-in references
PlaceReducer except location objective references
AbilityReducer except ability objective references only
BodyReducer except injury/recovery objective references only
Economy/crafting/reputation/domain systems
CardSync live writes
WorldBible import
manual mutation commands
```

---

# 5. Quest state model

Quests bucket should support:

```text
records
active
offered
rumored
readyToTurnIn
completed
failed
abandoned
rewardLedger
warnings
notes
```

Recommended shape:

```text
quests: {
  records: {},
  active: [],
  offered: [],
  rumored: [],
  readyToTurnIn: [],
  completed: [],
  failed: [],
  abandoned: [],
  rewardLedger: {},
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

If existing code uses arrays, keep stable quest keys and owner helpers.

Do not maintain multiple conflicting quest records for the same quest key.

---

# 6. QuestRecord schema

Required quest record fields:

```text
QuestRecord {
  id,
  key,
  title,
  normalizedTitle,
  status,
  originStatus,
  giverActorKey,
  giverDisplayName,
  turnInActorKey,
  turnInPlaceKey,
  locationKeys,
  objectives,
  rewards,
  promisedRewards,
  paidRewards,
  failedReasons,
  abandonedReason,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  completedTurn,
  rewardedTurn,
  historyKeys,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

## 6.1 Quest identity rules

```text
quest key is stable
quest key is family-scoped
quest key does not contain full raw output
quest key does not contain command/card/context text
quest title is readable
quest title is not just raw objective paragraph unless no better title exists
same giver + same core objective should usually bind to existing quest
```

## 6.2 Quest origin status

Quest origin status should identify how the quest entered state:

```text
rumored
offered
accepted_from_input
accepted_from_output
structured_startup
system_generated_later_disabled
unknown
```

Origin status helps prevent rumor/offer from becoming active automatically.

---

# 7. Quest status model

Allowed quest statuses:

```text
rumored
offered
active
progressing
ready_to_turn_in
completed
rewarded
failed
abandoned
expired
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
rumored is not active
offered is not accepted
active is not completed
ready_to_turn_in is not rewarded
completed is not automatically rewarded
rewarded requires payment/status event
failed/abandoned should preserve history
expired requires explicit time/deadline policy later
```

---

# 8. ObjectiveRecord schema

Required objective fields:

```text
ObjectiveRecord {
  id,
  key,
  questKey,
  verb,
  targetKey,
  targetDisplayName,
  description,
  status,
  progressCurrent,
  progressRequired,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  completedTurn,
  evidence,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Objective statuses:

```text
inactive
active
in_progress
complete
failed
optional
hidden
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
objective mention is not completion
partial progress is not completion
completion requires accepted objective_complete event
completion of one objective does not complete unrelated objectives
automatic all-objectives-complete status change must be explicit and tested
```

---

# 9. Objective verb support

QuestObjectiveSanitizer must support broad objective types without over-confirming them.

Required verbs:

```text
recover
find
repair
escort
clear
deliver
investigate
protect
bring
retrieve
rescue
defeat
survey
track
report to
speak with
collect
gather
return
seal
open
close
survive
train
learn
stabilize
heal
cure
```

Adding a verb to objective support does not mean the objective is completed when the verb is mentioned.

Examples:

```text
repair the north wall -> objective title Repair the North Wall
escort Bram to the chapel -> Escort Bram to the Chapel
investigate the burned tavern -> Investigate the Burned Tavern
train Ember Ward -> Train Ember Ward, ability objective only
```

---

# 10. RewardRecord schema

Required reward fields:

```text
RewardRecord {
  id,
  key,
  questKey,
  rewardType,
  description,
  amount,
  currencyType,
  itemKey,
  itemDisplayName,
  xpAmount,
  status,
  promisedByActorKey,
  paidByActorKey,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  paymentOperationKey,
  promisedTurn,
  readyTurn,
  paidTurn,
  deniedTurn,
  disputedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Reward types:

```text
currency
item
xp
favor
information
access
title
reputation_deferred
unknown
```

Reward statuses:

```text
promised
ready
paid
denied
disputed
duplicate_skipped
forfeited
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
promised is not paid
ready is not paid
paid requires accepted payment event
item reward requires InventoryReducer item event if actually handed over
currency reward requires InventoryReducer currency event
XP reward requires ProgressionReducer event if progression active
reputation reward remains deferred unless reputation spec active
```

---

# 11. QuestObjectiveSanitizer depth

QuestObjectiveSanitizer should clean objective text without losing meaning.

Examples:

```text
repair the north wall -> Repair the North Wall
escort Bram to the chapel -> Escort Bram to the Chapel
investigate the burned tavern -> Investigate the Burned Tavern
clear the mill wolves -> Clear the Mill Wolves
retrieve the Crown of Dawn from the ash shrine -> Retrieve the Crown of Dawn from the Ash Shrine
train Ember Ward until stable -> Train Ember Ward Until Stable
```

It must not:

```text
strip destination from escort objectives
turn objective mention into completion
turn reward text into objective completion
turn quest title into active quest without acceptance
```

---

# 12. RewardNameSanitizer depth

RewardNameSanitizer must separate promise, readiness, and payment.

Must recognize:

```text
15 gold
fifteen gold
3 silver
one hundred copper
red healing potion as reward
training scroll as reward
20 XP if progression reward active later
favor from Old Bren
access to the north gate
```

Promise markers:

```text
promises to pay
will pay
reward will be
offers if
payment after
```

Ready markers:

```text
your reward is ready
come collect your reward
Bren has the coins waiting
```

Payment markers:

```text
pays you
hands you the promised coins
gives you 15 gold
places the reward in your hand
counts out the gold
```

Denial/dispute markers:

```text
refuses to pay
denies the reward
claims you failed
offers less than promised
disputes the reward
withholds payment
```

---

# 13. Quest CanonValidator expansion

CanonValidator must enforce quest state transitions.

## 13.1 Accept quest offer when

```text
safe story output has NPC/job/board/request offering a task
objective target is bound enough
not quoted hypothetically only
not dream/rumor only unless status = rumored
not command/context/card source
```

Result:

```text
quest offered or rumored
not active unless accepted
```

## 13.2 Accept quest acceptance when

```text
player explicitly accepts in input and output/structure supports quest start
or safe output clearly states quest accepted/started
or structured startup quest starts active before lock
```

Result:

```text
quest active
objectives initialized
```

## 13.3 Accept objective progress when

```text
safe output directly shows partial progress toward active objective
objective exists or can bind to active quest
not merely intent
not command/context/card source
```

## 13.4 Accept objective completion when

```text
safe output directly confirms required objective completed
objective target binds to active quest objective
not duplicate
not merely arriving near objective
not merely seeing target
```

## 13.5 Accept quest completion when

```text
safe output directly confirms quest completed
or all required objectives complete and policy allows status ready/completed
not duplicate
```

Completion should normally lead to:

```text
ready_to_turn_in
```

unless reward/payment is also explicitly validated.

---

# 14. Reward CanonValidator expansion

## 14.1 Promise accepted as promise only

Example:

```text
Old Bren promises to pay you 15 gold if you clear the wolves.
```

Expected:

```text
reward.promise_candidate accepted/pending
currency.gain rejected
payment rejected
```

## 14.2 Ready reward accepted as ready only

Example:

```text
Old Bren says your 15 gold reward is ready.
```

Expected:

```text
reward.ready_candidate accepted
currency unchanged
```

## 14.3 Payment accepted only when

```text
safe output directly confirms payment/handoff
amount/item reward target is bound
payer/source is plausible
not denied/disputed/withheld
operation key not duplicate
```

## 14.4 Payment rejected when

```text
promises to pay
will pay later
could pay
reward is listed on quest board
/ref command displays reward
Story Card says reward paid
context says reward paid
refuses/denies/disputes payment
```

---

# 15. Quest ProposalBuilder event types

Add or verify event types:

```text
quest.rumor_candidate
quest.offer_candidate
quest.accept_candidate
quest.objective_progress_candidate
quest.objective_complete_candidate
quest.ready_to_turn_in_candidate
quest.completed_candidate
quest.failed_candidate
quest.abandoned_candidate
quest.expired_candidate
reward.promise_candidate
reward.ready_candidate
reward.payment_candidate
reward.denied_candidate
reward.disputed_candidate
currency.gain_candidate
item.reward_handoff_candidate
xp.reward_candidate_deferred
```

Current chunk may commit safe:

```text
quest rumor/offer/acceptance
objective progress/completion
ready_to_turn_in
quest completion status
reward promise/ready/paid/denied/disputed
currency gain only through explicit InventoryReducer split-result payment
item reward handoff only through InventoryReducer split-result item event
```

XP reward remains deferred unless ProgressionReducer exact XP handling is already active and ReleaseCheck allows it.

---

# 16. EventScannerLite quest/reward hints

EventScannerLite may detect quest/reward hints, but detection is not canon.

Allowed scan families:

```text
request/help/job/task/quest/mission
accept/agree/take the job
repair/recover/escort/clear/deliver/investigate/protect/rescue/defeat
progress markers
completion markers
turn-in markers
reward promise markers
reward ready markers
reward payment markers
reward denial/dispute markers
```

Must flag:

```text
rumorOrClaim
quotedSpeech
hypothetical
futureIntent
attemptOnly
dreamOrVision
commandSource
contextSource
cardSource
auditSource
duplicateRisk
promiseOnly
readyOnly
paymentConfirmed
deniedOrDisputed
```

Parser cannot mutate quest or reward state.

---

# 17. QuestReducerLite detailed behavior

QuestReducerLite owns:

```text
quests.records
quests.active
quests.offered
quests.rumored
quests.readyToTurnIn
quests.completed
quests.failed
quests.abandoned
quests.rewardLedger
quests.warnings
```

## 17.1 Rumor commit

Accepted rumor should:

```text
create/update quest as rumored
store source/evidence
not activate quest
mark quest_log/context dirty if useful
```

## 17.2 Offer commit

Accepted offer should:

```text
create/update quest as offered
store giver if known
store objective candidates
store promised rewards if present
not activate quest unless accepted separately
```

## 17.3 Acceptance commit

Accepted acceptance should:

```text
create/update quest as active
initialize objectives
preserve promised rewards
record operation key
mark quest_log/context dirty
```

## 17.4 Objective progress commit

Accepted progress should:

```text
update objective progressCurrent/status
append evidence/history
not complete unless threshold/direct completion reached and accepted
```

## 17.5 Objective completion commit

Accepted completion should:

```text
set objective status complete
record completedTurn
check duplicate key
if all required objectives complete, mark quest ready_to_turn_in or completed according to policy
not pay reward automatically
```

## 17.6 Quest completion commit

Accepted quest completion should:

```text
set quest status completed or ready_to_turn_in according to source
preserve reward status as promised/ready unless payment exists
not award currency/items/XP automatically
```

## 17.7 Failure/abandonment commit

Accepted failure/abandonment should:

```text
set status failed/abandoned
record reason/source
preserve history
not delete quest immediately
```

---

# 18. Reward behavior details

## 18.1 Reward promise commit

Accepted promise should:

```text
create/update RewardRecord
status promised
store amount/item/xp/favor/access if parsed
store promisedByActorKey if known
mark quest_log/context dirty
no inventory/currency/progression mutation
```

## 18.2 Reward ready commit

Accepted ready should:

```text
set reward status ready
record readyTurn
no inventory/currency/progression mutation
```

## 18.3 Reward payment commit

Accepted payment should:

```text
check reward payment key
set reward status paid
record paidTurn/paidByActorKey
mark quest_log/context dirty
emit or pair with explicit InventoryReducer currency/item event if reward is currency/item
```

Preferred safe policy:

```text
QuestReducer marks reward paid.
InventoryReducer receives separate accepted currency.gain_candidate or item.reward_handoff_candidate.
Both share a related payment key.
```

If split result cannot be implemented yet, mark payment status and defer currency mutation rather than paying unsafely.

## 18.4 Reward denied/disputed commit

Accepted denial/dispute should:

```text
set status denied or disputed
record reason/source
no payment
no currency/item/XP mutation
```

---

# 19. QuestReducer defensive guards

QuestReducer must reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
rumor-only as active quest
hypothetical offer as active quest
promise as payment
ready as payment
objective mention as completion
unbound quest target for durable completion
duplicate payment key
missing quest key
wrong owner reducer
```

Reducer should not blindly trust upstream.

If unsafe routed data reaches QuestReducer, it must not mutate quest/reward truth.

---

# 20. Split-owner reward payment contract

Because inventory/currency is owned by InventoryReducer, reward payment needs explicit handoff.

## 20.1 Currency reward flow

Example:

```text
Old Bren pays you the promised 15 gold.
```

Validated results:

```text
reward.payment_candidate -> QuestReducer marks reward paid
currency.gain_candidate -> InventoryReducer adds 15 gold
```

Both should share:

```text
rewardPaymentKey
payerKey
questKey
amount
currencyType
sourceClauseHash
```

## 20.2 Item reward flow

Example:

```text
Bren hands you the silver wolf charm as your reward.
```

Validated results:

```text
reward.payment_candidate -> QuestReducer marks item reward paid/received
item.reward_handoff_candidate -> InventoryReducer adds item
```

## 20.3 XP reward flow

XP reward should remain deferred unless ProgressionReducer exact XP support is active.

If deferred:

```text
reward status can be ready/paid as narrative status
XP mutation deferred/pending
ReleaseCheck warns honestly
```

---

# 21. Operation keys

Required quest/reward operation keys:

```text
quest.rumor.<questKey>.<sourceHash>
quest.offer.<questKey>.<giverKey>.<clauseHash>
quest.accept.<questKey>.<giverKey>.<clauseHash>
quest.objective.progress.<questKey>.<objectiveKey>.<progressHash>
quest.objective.complete.<questKey>.<objectiveKey>.<clauseHash>
quest.ready.<questKey>.<sourceKey>
quest.complete.<questKey>.<sourceKey>
quest.fail.<questKey>.<sourceKey>
quest.abandon.<questKey>.<sourceKey>
quest.reward.promise.<questKey>.<rewardKey>.<sourceHash>
quest.reward.ready.<questKey>.<rewardKey>.<sourceHash>
quest.reward.pay.<questKey>.<payerKey>.<rewardKey>
quest.reward.deny.<questKey>.<payerKey>.<rewardKey>
quest.reward.dispute.<questKey>.<payerKey>.<rewardKey>
```

Inventory/Progression related keys:

```text
currency.gain.<amount>.<currencyType>.<rewardPaymentKey>
item.reward.<itemKey>.<rewardPaymentKey>
xp.reward.<amount>.<rewardPaymentKey> deferred until progression active
```

Rules:

```text
payment keys are durable
objective completion keys are durable or quest-scoped durable
progress keys prevent spam on output replay
promise keys prevent duplicate promised reward records
```

---

# 22. Dirty targets

Quest/reward updates should mark:

```text
quest_log
context
inventory_equipment when currency/item reward split result occurs
player_sheet if progression reward eventually applies
social_memory if actor promise/payment memory later applies
```

Mapping:

```text
quest rumor/offer/accept/progress/complete/fail/abandon -> quest_log, context
reward promise/ready/paid/denied/disputed -> quest_log, context
currency/item payment split -> quest_log, inventory_equipment, context
```

Dirty targets do not write cards directly.

CardSync remains mirror/dry-run unless later live writes are enabled.

---

# 23. `/quests` updates

`/quests` must display quest and reward states honestly.

Required sections:

```text
Active quests
Offered quests
Rumored quests
Ready to turn in
Completed quests
Failed / abandoned quests
Objectives
Promised rewards
Ready rewards
Paid rewards
Denied / disputed rewards
Warnings
```

Display rules:

```text
rumored quests not listed as active
offered quests not listed as accepted
objective progress not listed as complete
completed quest not listed as rewarded unless reward paid
promised reward not listed as paid
ready reward not listed as paid
paid reward shown once
XP reward displayed as deferred if progression inactive
```

Example:

```text
[AIDRPG] Quests

Active:
- Clear the Mill Wolves
  Objective: Defeat or drive off wolves near Old Mill — in progress
  Reward: 15 gold promised by Old Bren — not paid

Ready to turn in:
- None recorded

Paid rewards:
- None recorded
```

---

# 24. `/inventory` reward reference consistency

`/inventory` should not count promised rewards as currency.

If promised reward exists:

```text
Promised rewards are shown in /quests and do not count as paid currency.
```

If reward is paid and InventoryReducer applied currency:

```text
Currency: Gold 15 confirmed
```

If QuestReducer marks paid but InventoryReducer currency event is deferred due missing split support:

```text
Reward marked paid in quest log, currency update pending/deferred due split-result policy.
```

Do not silently lie.

---

# 25. ContextBuilder quest packet

ContextBuilder should include concise quest facts.

Include:

```text
active quest objectives
ready turn-ins
urgent failed/deadline states
promised vs paid reward status when relevant
```

Do not include:

```text
all old completed quests every turn
rumored quest as active
promised reward as paid
full raw quest JSON
old quarantined quest claims
```

Example packet:

```text
Quest: Clear the Mill Wolves is active. Old Bren promised 15 gold, but it has not been paid. Objective: drive off or defeat the wolves near Old Mill.
```

---

# 26. IntegrityChecker quest/reward checks

Add checks:

```text
active quest records have stable keys
objectives have stable keys and statuses
reward promises and payments are separate
paid reward has paymentOperationKey
paid currency reward has matching inventory/currency key or deferred warning
no duplicate paid reward keys
rumored/offered quests not also active unless accepted key exists
completed quest not automatically rewarded without payment key
manual/card/context quest sources not active truth
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad records.

It may not complete quests or pay rewards.

---

# 27. Cleanup quest/reward protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old low-risk quest history entries
old completed quest summaries if archived policy exists
old rejected reward claim audits
quest warnings cap
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
active quests
ready-to-turn-in quests
unpaid promised rewards
paid reward ledger keys
objective evidence needed for audit
failed/abandoned important quest history
```

Cleanup must not convert promised rewards into paid rewards or remove duplicate protection keys.

---

# 28. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC07-specific gates:

```text
PC07-G01 QuestRecord schema exists or equivalent stable quest record exists
PC07-G02 ObjectiveRecord schema exists or equivalent objective structure exists
PC07-G03 RewardRecord schema exists or equivalent reward structure exists
PC07-G04 quest status model separates rumored/offered/active/ready/completed/rewarded/failed/abandoned
PC07-G05 objective status model separates progress/completion/failure
PC07-G06 reward status model separates promised/ready/paid/denied/disputed/duplicate_skipped
PC07-G07 QuestObjectiveSanitizer supports broad objective verbs without auto-completion
PC07-G08 RewardNameSanitizer separates promise/ready/payment/denial/dispute
PC07-G09 CanonValidator rejects rumor-only active quest and promise-as-payment
PC07-G10 QuestReducer rejects unsafe routed quest/reward events
PC07-G11 split-owner reward payment contract exists or payment currency mutation is safely deferred
PC07-G12 reward/currency duplicate keys exist
PC07-G13 /quests displays quest/reward states honestly
PC07-G14 /inventory does not show promised rewards as paid currency
PC07-G15 command/context/card quest/reward text cannot become canon
PC07-G16 cleanup does not delete active quests or reward keys
PC07-G17 economy/reputation/domain remain disabled
PC07-G18 player-breaker quest/reward tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if promised rewards can become currency, command quest output can pay rewards, or duplicate reward payment is possible.

```text
quests.records

quests.rewardLedger

sourceOperationKeys
```

These fields should already be prepared by PC03, but PC07 gives them behavior and display depth.

---



Make displays honest.

Keep it concise and state-accurate.

```text
/playerbreakercheck
/regressioncheck

/inventory
```

## Step 15 — Save accepted quest/reward build

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.7_QUEST_REWARD_DEPTH_ACCEPTED.txt
```

```text
inventory reward reference consistency
quest context packet
quest/reward integrity checks
PC07 ReleaseCheck gates
PC07 regression/player-breaker tests
quest/reward acceptance report
updated planning zip with Production Chunk 07 added
```

It should not produce:

```text
full economy system
full reputation system
full domain/faction automation
manual quest/reward mutation commands
progression XP automation unless separately active
generated quest cards

old card prose quest import
```

# 37. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 07 passes if:

```text
quest statuses are separated
objective statuses are separated
reward statuses are separated
rumor/offer does not become active automatically
objective progress/completion works without reward payment
reward promise/ready/payment separation works
reward/currency duplicates are prevented
QuestReducer rejects unsafe routed quest/reward events
split-owner payment contract exists or payment mutation is safely deferred
/quests displays quest/reward states honestly
/inventory does not show promised rewards as paid currency
command/context/card quest/reward text cannot become canon
cleanup preserves active quests and reward keys
PC07 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC07 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
XP rewards are deferred
reputation rewards are deferred
favor/access rewards are descriptive only
quest generation is not active
economy integration is deferred
some automatic all-objectives-complete behavior is conservative
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
rumor-only quest becomes active
quest offer becomes accepted without acceptance
objective mention completes objective
quest completion pays reward automatically
reward promise gives currency
reward ready gives currency
reward payment duplicates
command/context/card text completes quest or pays reward
/quests displays promised as paid
/inventory displays promised reward as currency
manual /completequest or /payreward mutates state
QuestReducer trusts unsafe routed data
cleanup deletes active quest or reward keys
```

---

# 38. Handoff to Production Chunk 08

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 08 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 08 — Actor Presence, Social Memory, Witnessing, and Relationship Depth
```

Production Chunk 08 may use quest giver, payer, witness, and turn-in actor references, but should not turn private quest interactions into public reputation automatically.

Production Chunk 07 must leave quest/reward state reliable enough that future chunks can ask:

```text
Is this quest rumored, offered, active, ready, completed, failed, abandoned, or rewarded?
Which objectives are active, progressing, or complete?
Was the reward promised, ready, paid, denied, or disputed?
Was currency actually added?
Has this reward already been paid?
```

---

# 39. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 07 is where quest truth stops being a loose story note.

Final rule:

```text
Quests have states.
Objectives have states.
Rewards have states.
Promises are not payment.
Completion is not payment.
Payment is duplicate-safe.
Commands, cards, context, rumors, and vague mentions cannot complete quests or pay rewards.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen actors, witnesses, social memory, and relationship state in Production Chunk 08.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 08 — Actor Presence, Social Memory, Witnessing, and Relationship Depth

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 08 — Actor Presence, Social Memory, Witnessing, and Relationship Depth  
**Production sequence:** 08 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.7 quest/reward-depth accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen actor records, actor presence, scene-only actors, reference-only actors, absent actors, witness memory, direct-informed memory, relationship evidence, and social continuity without false presence, duplicate actors, or unsafe reputation automation.  
**Status:** Core gameplay-depth production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 07 — Quest, Objective, Reward, and Promise/Payment Depth.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 08 exists because actors are where AI Dungeon continuity can either feel alive or become badly corrupted. Old rough attempts showed the same risks repeatedly: duplicate actors from titles/names, absent actors treated as present, reference-only names treated as scene participants, private witness memory becoming global reputation, generic guards becoming permanent major NPCs, command/card/context text creating memories, and actor harm being applied to a statue, painting, title, or training dummy named after someone.

This chunk turns actors into structured, useful, bounded state. The goal is not full reputation, faction, romance, party, summon, or NPC combat automation yet. The goal is reliable actor presence, source-bound memory, and relationship evidence that future systems can safely build on.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Mentioned is not present.
Absent is not present.
Reference-only is not present.
Generic is not automatically persistent.
Witnessed is not global reputation.
Private is not public.
Relationship claim is evidence, not absolute truth.
Actor memory requires presence or direct information.
A training dummy named after Mira is not Mira.
```

Actor truth must be local, source-aware, duplicate-safe, scene-aware, and AI-Dungeon-safe.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 07 made quest givers, reward payers, turn-ins, and quest references safer. Actors come next because they connect to quest givers, reward payers, witnesses, companions, enemies, merchants, trainers, faction contacts, social memory, relationship changes, scene presence, future reputation, future party/summon logic, and world persistence.

If actors are not safe now, later systems will create false witnesses, false public knowledge, duplicate NPCs, and broken relationship state.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Allowed changes:

```text
Actor schema helpers
ActorReducerLite
ActorNameSanitizer
CanonValidator actor/presence/memory rules
ProposalBuilder actor event types
EventScannerLite actor/social hints
CommandRenderers /actors
ContextBuilder actor/social packets
OperationLedger actor/memory keys
DirtyQueue actor/social targets
IntegrityChecker actor checks
ReleaseCheck actor gates
Regression/player-breaker actor tests
```

Allowed additions:

```text
ActorRecord schema depth
SceneActorRecord schema
ActorMemoryRecord schema
RelationshipEvidenceRecord schema
PresenceStatus model
PersistenceClass model
WitnessScope model
ActorRole model
Alias/title handling
Compound actor suppression rules
Generic actor promotion policy
Social memory audit summaries
```

Forbidden in this chunk:

```text
full reputation automation
faction standing automation
romance system
party/summon control system
merchant economy system
NPC combat AI engine
manual /spawnactor or /setrelationship commands
generated actor Story Cards
old card prose actor import
```

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
ActorState helpers
ActorReducerLite
ActorNameSanitizer
CanonValidator actor rules
ProposalBuilder actor proposals
EventScannerLite actor/social hints
Resolver actor target binding
CommandRenderers: /actors and relevant quest-giver references
ContextBuilderLite actor/social packet
OperationLedger actor/memory keys
DirtyQueue actor dirty targets
AuditLogger actor/social lines
InvariantChecker actor checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC08 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
QuestReducer except actor references for giver/payer/turn-in display
InventoryReducer except custody-holder references
AbilityReducer except trainer references only
BodyReducer except NPC injury/death remains conservative
PlaceReducer except current-scene actor references
ProgressionReducer
Reputation/faction/domain systems
Party/summon systems
CardSync live writes
WorldBible import
manual mutation commands
```

---

# 5. Actor state model

Actors bucket should support:

```text
records
sceneActors
present
absent
referenceOnly
memories
relationshipNotes
warnings
notes
```

Recommended shape:

```text
actors: {
  records: {},
  sceneActors: {},
  present: [],
  absent: [],
  referenceOnly: [],
  memories: [],
  relationshipNotes: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

If existing code uses arrays, keep stable actor keys and helper functions. Do not allow multiple active actor records for the same canonical named actor.

---

# 6. ActorRecord schema

```text
ActorRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  normalizedName,
  aliases,
  titles,
  type,
  role,
  importance,
  persistenceClass,
  presenceStatus,
  currentPlaceKey,
  currentSceneKey,
  knownToPlayer,
  relationshipSummary,
  memories,
  relationshipEvidence,
  custodyRefs,
  questRefs,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  lastSeenTurn,
  lastMentionedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
key is stable and family-scoped
key does not contain full raw output
key does not come from command/card/context text
important titles are preserved when identity-relevant
aliases are stored, not automatically separate actors
bad compound/connectors are cleaned without creating fake actors
```

Named/important actors may become persistent. Generic actors normally become scene actors.

Examples:

```text
Mira Vell -> persistent actor
Old Bren -> persistent actor
High Priestess Ilyra -> persistent actor
Veteran Guard -> important scene actor or persistent if repeated/quest-relevant
A guard -> scene actor by default
A merchant -> scene actor by default
Three bandits -> scene group actor by default
```

---

# 7. SceneActorRecord schema

```text
SceneActorRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  normalizedName,
  actorKey,
  groupCount,
  role,
  presenceStatus,
  sceneKey,
  placeKey,
  persistenceClass,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  expiresTurn,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
scene actor may link to persistent ActorRecord if known
scene-only actor does not automatically become permanent
scene actor may expire on scene change through cleanup policy
scene actor presence does not imply global knowledge
```

---

# 8. PresenceStatus model

Allowed statuses:

```text
present
scene_only
nearby
mentioned
reference_only
absent
elsewhere
unknown
left_scene
dead_pending
dead_confirmed
captured
```

Rules:

```text
present means currently in scene or directly interacting
scene_only means present but not durable by default
nearby means nearby, not necessarily interacting
mentioned/reference_only means not present
absent/elsewhere means no witness memory
actor death remains conservative and must not come from rumor/card/context/command text
```

---

# 9. PersistenceClass model

Allowed persistence classes:

```text
player
major
recurring
quest_relevant
scene_only
generic_group
summon_deferred
unknown
```

Generic actors should not become major unless named, repeatedly recurring, connected to a quest/reward/major event, or explicitly promoted by validation.

---

# 10. ActorMemoryRecord schema

```text
ActorMemoryRecord {
  memoryId,
  actorKey,
  memoryType,
  scope,
  confidence,
  summary,
  sourceOperationKey,
  sourceEventId,
  sourceTurn,
  witnessedDirectly,
  informedByActorKey,
  placeKey,
  publicness,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Memory types:

```text
witnessed_action
heard_report
direct_conversation
quest_promise
quest_payment
threat
help_received
harm_received
relationship_evidence
private_secret
rumor
```

Scopes/publicness:

```text
private_to_actor
shared_with_present_group
public_local
public_faction_deferred
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
present witnesses can receive witnessed_action memory
absent actors do not receive scene memory
directly informed actors can receive heard_report/direct_conversation memory
private memory does not become public reputation
rumor memory is rumor, not confirmed truth
```

---

# 11. RelationshipEvidenceRecord schema

```text
RelationshipEvidenceRecord {
  evidenceId,
  actorKey,
  relationType,
  direction,
  strength,
  confidence,
  summary,
  sourceOperationKey,
  sourceEventId,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Relation types:

```text
friendly
hostile
fearful
respectful
suspicious
grateful
angry
loyal
distrustful
romantic_deferred
rival_deferred
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
relationship evidence is not numeric reputation automation
one nice action does not create permanent loyalty by itself
one private insult does not create public hostility
romance remains deferred unless future spec exists
```

---

# 12. ActorNameSanitizer depth

Must handle:

```text
Veteran Guard -> Veteran Guard, not Guard duplicate
High Priestess Ilyra -> High Priestess Ilyra
Old Bren -> Old Bren
Mira Vell -> Mira Vell
Captain Arlen of the North Gate -> Captain Arlen
three bandits -> bandits group with count 3 if supported
ACTOR:AND_MIRA -> ACTOR:MIRA
Mira and Old Bren -> two actors, not one actor named Mira and Old Bren
```

Must not create present actors from:

```text
training dummy named after Mira
statue of Mira
painting of Old Bren
rumor about the High Priestess
book title containing a name
Story Card/context/command text
```

---

# 13. Compound actor suppression

Examples:

```text
The Veteran Guard blocks the gate.
```

Expected:

```text
one actor: Veteran Guard
not Veteran Guard + Guard
```

```text
High Priestess Ilyra raises her staff.
```

Expected:

```text
one actor: High Priestess Ilyra
not High Priestess + Ilyra separately
```

```text
Mira and Old Bren argue.
```

Expected:

```text
two actors: Mira, Old Bren
not one actor named Mira and Old Bren
```

---

# 14. Actor CanonValidator expansion

Accept present actor only when safe story output places actor in the current scene or direct interaction, actor target is bound, and source is not absent/reference-only/object/statue/painting/dummy/title/dream-only/command/context/card/audit/debug.

Accept scene-only actor when a generic actor or group appears in scene and should not be persistent by default.

Accept absent/reference-only actor when source explicitly says actor is absent/elsewhere/not present, or actor is used only as comparison/title/reference/object label.

Accept memory only when actor is present and directly witnesses event, or actor is directly informed. Reject memory when actor is absent, reference-only, only rumored, not informed, or source is command/card/context/audit/debug.

---

# 15. Actor ProposalBuilder event types

```text
actor.present_candidate
actor.scene_only_candidate
actor.nearby_candidate
actor.absent_candidate
actor.reference_only_candidate
actor.elsewhere_candidate
actor.memory_candidate
actor.relationship_evidence_candidate
actor.status_candidate
actor.death_candidate
actor.custody_reference_candidate
actor.quest_reference_candidate
```

Current chunk may commit present actor, scene-only actor, nearby/reference, absent/reference-only note, memory for present/directly informed actor, relationship evidence, quest/custody references. Actor death/status remains conservative and may stay pending unless directly validated.

---

# 16. EventScannerLite actor/social hints

Detect actor/social hints, but detection is not canon.

Allowed scan families:

```text
named actors
titles and roles
generic people/groups
presence verbs
absence verbs
speech/direct information
witnessing verbs
relationship/emotion cues
custody/holding item references
quest giver/payer/turn-in references
```

Must flag absent, elsewhere, referenceOnly, objectReference, statueOrPainting, dummyOrTrainingTarget, quotedSpeechOnly, rumorOrClaim, dreamOrVision, privateNoWitness, commandSource, contextSource, cardSource, auditSource, compoundActorRisk, genericActor.

---

# 17. ActorReducerLite detailed behavior

ActorReducerLite owns:

```text
actors.records
actors.sceneActors
actors.present
actors.absent
actors.referenceOnly
actors.memories
actors.relationshipNotes
actors.warnings
```

Accepted present actor should create/update ActorRecord or SceneActorRecord, set presence/current scene if known, set lastSeenTurn, mark social_memory/current_location/context dirty, and audit compactly.

Accepted generic actor should create/update SceneActorRecord with scene_only/generic_group persistence and not create major persistent actor unless promotion rule accepts it.

Accepted absent/reference-only should record absent/reference-only status or note, not add to present list, not create witness memory, and not apply scene effects.

Accepted memory should verify actor present or directly informed, create ActorMemoryRecord, set scope/private/publicness correctly, not update public reputation, and mark social_memory/context dirty.

Accepted relationship evidence should create RelationshipEvidenceRecord, update relationshipSummary cautiously, not apply numeric reputation/faction automation, and mark social_memory/context dirty.

---

# 18. ActorReducer defensive guards

Reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
absent as present
reference-only as present
statue/painting/dummy as actor presence
training dummy named after actor as actor harm
rumor-only memory as direct memory
private event as public memory
generic actor auto-major without promotion
missing actor key
wrong owner reducer
```

Reducer must not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 19. Witness memory rules

Present witness example:

```text
The Veteran Guard watches you steal the apple.
```

Expected: Veteran Guard gets witnessed_action memory, private/local scope, no global reputation.

Absent actor example:

```text
Mira is not here when you steal the apple.
```

Expected: Mira receives no witness memory.

Directly informed example:

```text
You tell Mira that you stole the apple.
```

Expected: Mira gets heard_report/direct_conversation memory only if present/directly addressed or output confirms contact.

Public group example:

```text
The whole market sees you steal the apple.
```

Expected: scene/public_local memory candidate; reputation/faction numeric automation remains deferred.

---

# 20. Relationship evidence rules

Examples:

```text
Mira smiles warmly after you help her.
Old Bren thanks you for clearing the wolves.
The guard narrows his eyes, suspicious of you.
The bandit swears revenge.
```

Expected: relationship evidence added, relationship summary may update cautiously, no faction/reputation automation.

---

# 21. Actor custody and quest references

Actors may be linked to items and quests as references.

```text
Mira holds the jeweled crown.
Old Bren promises 15 gold.
Captain Arlen gives the gate repair job.
```

ActorReducer may store social/reference memory only. InventoryReducer owns custody truth. QuestReducer owns quest/reward truth. ActorReducer must not add inventory or pay rewards.

---

# 22. Operation keys

```text
actor.present.<actorKey>.<placeOrSceneKey>.<clauseHash>
actor.scene.<actorKey>.<sceneKey>.<clauseHash>
actor.absent.<actorKey>.<clauseHash>
actor.reference.<actorKey>.<referenceKind>.<clauseHash>
actor.memory.<actorKey>.<memoryHash>
actor.relationship.<actorKey>.<relationType>.<sourceHash>
actor.custodyref.<actorKey>.<itemKey>.<sourceHash>
actor.questref.<actorKey>.<questKey>.<sourceHash>
actor.status.<actorKey>.<statusKey>.<clauseHash>
```

Present/scene keys prevent duplicate actors on replay. Memory keys prevent duplicate memory spam. Relationship keys prevent repeated emotional spam. Custody/quest refs do not replace Inventory/Quest owner keys.

---

# 23. Dirty targets

Actor updates should mark social_memory, current_location, context, quest_log when actor reference affects quest display, and inventory_equipment only when paired with InventoryReducer custody truth.

---

# 24. `/actors` updates

Required sections:

```text
Present important actors
Scene-only actors
Known persistent actors
Nearby actors
Absent / elsewhere actors
Reference-only mentions
Recent witness memories
Relationship evidence
Quest / custody references
Warnings
```

Display rules:

```text
absent actors not shown as present
reference-only actors not shown as present
scene-only actors not shown as major persistent unless promoted
private memory not shown as public reputation
relationship evidence not shown as absolute reputation score
```

---

# 25. ContextBuilder actor/social packet

Include present important actors, scene-only actors when scene-relevant, recent witness memories if relevant, important relationship evidence, and quest giver/payer/turn-in actor if relevant. Do not include all known actors every turn, absent actors as present, reference-only actors as present, private memory as public reputation, full raw memory logs, or old quarantined actor claims.

---

# 26. IntegrityChecker actor checks

Checks:

```text
no duplicate actor records with same canonical key
present list only contains present/scene actors
referenceOnly list does not overlap present unless valid transition exists
absent actor not marked present
scene-only actors not promoted without reason
memory records have actorKey and sourceOperationKey
witness memories require present/directly informed source
relationship evidence does not become numeric reputation
actor records do not originate from command/card/context source
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad records. It may not create actors, memories, or relationship changes.

---

# 27. Cleanup actor/social protections

Cleanup may compact low-value scene-only actors after scene expiry policy, duplicate memory warning spam, low-risk relationship evidence summaries, and actor warning caps.

Cleanup must not delete major/recurring/quest-relevant actors, active present actors, important witness memories, quest giver/payer references, custody references needed for inventory display, or relationship evidence needed for future continuity.

---

# 28. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

```text
PC08-G01 ActorRecord schema exists or equivalent stable actor record exists
PC08-G02 SceneActorRecord schema exists or equivalent scene actor structure exists
PC08-G03 ActorMemoryRecord schema exists or equivalent memory structure exists
PC08-G04 RelationshipEvidenceRecord schema exists or equivalent evidence structure exists
PC08-G05 presence model separates present/scene_only/nearby/mentioned/reference_only/absent/elsewhere/unknown
PC08-G06 persistence model separates major/recurring/quest_relevant/scene_only/generic_group
PC08-G07 ActorNameSanitizer preserves titles/names and suppresses compound duplicates
PC08-G08 CanonValidator rejects absent/reference-only/object/dummy/card/context actors as present
PC08-G09 ActorReducer rejects unsafe routed actor/memory events
PC08-G10 witness memory requires presence or direct information
PC08-G11 private memory does not become public reputation
PC08-G12 relationship evidence does not activate reputation automation
PC08-G13 actor custody/quest refs do not mutate inventory/quest truth directly
PC08-G14 /actors displays presence/reference/memory honestly
PC08-G15 command/context/card actor text cannot become canon
PC08-G16 cleanup does not delete important actor/memory truth
PC08-G17 reputation/faction/party/summon systems remain disabled
PC08-G18 player-breaker actor/social tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if absent/reference-only actors can become present, command/card/context actor text can create actors, or private witness memory can become public reputation.

---

# 29. Required tests

```text
PC08-001 Named actor present: Mira Vell steps into tavern -> Mira present persistent record.
PC08-002 Generic guard scene-only: A guard stands by the gate -> scene-only, not major.
PC08-003 Veteran Guard no duplicate Guard.
PC08-004 High Priestess Ilyra preserved as one actor.
PC08-005 Mira and Old Bren split into two actors.
PC08-006 Absent actor not present.
PC08-007 Reference-only actor not present.
PC08-008 Training dummy named Mira does not harm real Mira.
PC08-009 Statue of Old Bren not actor presence.
PC08-010 Present witness receives memory, no global reputation.
PC08-011 Absent actor receives no witness memory.
PC08-012 Directly informed actor memory only if present/addressed/contact confirmed.
PC08-013 Whole market sees theft -> local/public memory candidate, no reputation automation.
PC08-014 Friendly relationship evidence added, not permanent loyalty.
PC08-015 Hostile relationship evidence added, no combat/faction automation.
PC08-016 Quest giver reference does not complete quest.
PC08-017 Custody reference does not grant item.
PC08-018 /actors output is non-canon when fed into Output hook.
PC08-019 Story Card actor prose does not mutate presence/trust.
PC08-020 Context actor prose does not mutate actor memory/reputation.
PC08-021 Duplicate actor presence skipped.
PC08-022 Duplicate memory skipped.
PC08-023 Scene-only actor cleanup preserves major actors/memories.
PC08-024 /actors display separates present/scene-only/reference/absent.
PC08-025 /reputation /faction /setrelationship disabled/no mutation.
PC08-026 /partycontrol /summoncontrol disabled/no actor promotion.
```

Minimum expectation:

```text
26 PC08 tests registered
0 critical PC08 failures
```

---

# 30. Regression placement

Add PC08 tests to:

```text
T2 Parser/Proposal/Validator Safety Tests
T3 Resolver/Sanitizer Tests
T4 Reducer Commit Tests
T6 Player-Breaker Adversarial Tests
T8 Deferred System Disabled Tests
T7 Long-Run Stability Tests
```

# 32. Parser/proposal/validator changes

Allowed: actor presence/absence/reference detection refinement, witness/direct-informed memory detection, relationship evidence detection, compound actor suppression, generic scene-only classification, operation key suggestions.

Forbidden: full reputation/faction parser, party/summon command parser, actor combat AI parser, manual relationship mutation parser, card/context actor import.

---

# 33. Reducer changes

Allowed: ActorReducer present/scene-only/absent/reference-only/memory/relationship evidence behavior, unsafe routed-data rejection, memory logging, dirty targets, duplicate skip results.

Forbidden: public reputation mutation, faction standing mutation, party/summon activation, manual spawn/setrelationship reducer, inventory/quest mutation inside ActorReducer, combat damage automation.

---

# 34. Command/context/card changes

Allowed: /actors display depth, context actor/social packet, cardaudit actor mirror labels if dry-run, releasecheck PC08 gates, playerbreakercheck actor/social cases.

Forbidden: /setrelationship, /spawnactor, /addcompanion, active /reputation, active /faction, live card writes that create actor truth.

# 35. Beginner implementation order

```text
4. Deepen ActorNameSanitizer for names, titles, groups, compounds, connectors, and reference-only names.
```

It should not produce full reputation, full faction standing, party/summon control, manual actor/relationship mutation commands, NPC combat AI, generated actor cards, live CardSync writes, or old card prose actor import.

---

# 37. Pass / warn / fail criteria

PASS if actor presence/reference/absence states are separated, named/generic actors are classified safely, compound duplicates are suppressed, reference-only and absent actors do not become present, witness memory requires presence/direct information, private memory does not become public reputation, relationship evidence remains evidence, ActorReducer rejects unsafe routed data, /actors displays honestly, command/context/card actor text cannot become canon, cleanup preserves important actors/memories, PC08 tests pass, and ReleaseCheck has no PC08 FAIL/BLOCKED gates.

WARN if reputation/faction, party/summon, relationship automation, generic actor promotion, scene-only cleanup, or NPC combat/death remain conservative/deferred.

FAIL if absent/reference-only actors become present, training dummy named after Mira harms Mira, Veteran Guard creates duplicate Guard, command/context/card text creates actor/memory, private witness memory becomes public reputation, generic guard becomes permanent major actor without promotion, relationship evidence mutates faction/reputation automation, ActorReducer trusts unsafe routed data, or cleanup deletes major actor/important memory.

---

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 09 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 09 — Place, Travel, Time, Scene, and Deadline Depth
```

Production Chunk 09 may use actor presence and scene actor state to improve current scene/location display, but should not let location text create false actors or let actor references overwrite location.

---

# 39. Final doctrine

```text
Actors exist in states.
Presence is local.
Memory is source-bound.
Relationships are evidence-based.
Reputation is deferred.
No unsafe text source creates people, witnesses, trust, hatred, or public knowledge.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen places, travel, scenes, time, and deadlines in Production Chunk 09.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 09 — Place, Travel, Time, Scene, and Deadline Depth

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 09 — Place, Travel, Time, Scene, and Deadline Depth  
**Production sequence:** 09 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.8 actor/social-depth accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen current location, current scene, travel intent, visible/nearby references, route/interstitial handling, time passage, time claims, deadlines, and age-safety behavior without weak mention overwrites or fake time/age canon.  
**Status:** Core gameplay-depth production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 08 — Actor Presence, Social Memory, Witnessing, and Relationship Depth.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 09 exists because place and time continuity determine whether AI Dungeon gameplay feels like a real world or a constantly-resetting hallucination.

Old attempts often failed by:

```text
weak place mentions overwriting current location
visible places becoming arrival
travel intent becoming arrival
roads/path/interstitial phrases beating named destinations
context/card/command location text becoming canon
quoted or narrated time skips changing age
long time passage duplicating on output replay
deadlines being forgotten or completed from vague text
scene actors/items not matching current scene after movement
```

This chunk makes location and time persistent, conservative, duplicate-safe, and readable. The goal is not full world simulation yet. The goal is to make current place, current scene, intended travel, known references, time passage, and deadlines safe enough for later world bible, region, faction, encounter, and long-run persistence systems.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Visible is not current location.
Nearby is not arrival.
Travel intent is not arrival.
Road is not destination when a named destination exists.
Route is not scene unless confirmed.
Mentioned place is not current place.
Command location text is not canon.
Context location text is not canon.
Story Card location text is not canon.
Quoted time skip is not time passage.
Dream time skip is not real aging.
Literal time passage is duplicate-sensitive.
Age mutation requires strict policy.
Deadline mention is not completion.
```

Place and time truth must be local, source-aware, duplicate-safe, and conservative.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 08 made actor presence and scene witnesses safer. Places and time come next because they connect to scene actors, scene items, quest objectives, travel and arrival, deadlines, actor memory location, region pressure, creature encounters, world bible references, CardSync current-location mirrors, context prioritization, and long-run persistence.

If place/time is not safe now, later world systems will misfire. A wolf encounter in the market, a chapel scene overwriting a tavern, or a twenty-year quote aging the player can break the whole game.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 09 may change:

```text
Place schema helpers
Time schema helpers
PlaceReducerLite
TimeReducerLite
PlaceNameSanitizer
CanonValidator place/time rules
ProposalBuilder place/time event types
EventScannerLite place/time hints
CommandRenderers /location and /time
ContextBuilder place/time packets
OperationLedger place/time/deadline keys
DirtyQueue place/time targets
Cleanup scene-expiry shell
IntegrityChecker place/time checks
ReleaseCheck place/time gates
Regression/player-breaker place/time tests
```

Production Chunk 09 may add:

```text
PlaceRecord schema depth
SceneRecord schema
TravelIntentRecord schema
PlaceReferenceRecord schema
RouteReferenceRecord schema
TimeAdvanceRecord schema
TimeClaimRecord schema
DeadlineRecord schema
SceneTransitionRecord schema
arrival-strength model
weak-reference model
age policy shell
scene expiry coordination shell
location/time audit summaries
```

Production Chunk 09 may not add:

```text
full world bible import
full region/faction pressure model
full weather/calendar system
full encounter generator
full travel simulation with pathfinding
full settlement/world structure extraction
manual /teleport or /settime commands
live CardSync writes
old card prose place/time import
```

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
PlaceState helpers
TimeState helpers
PlaceReducerLite
TimeReducerLite
PlaceNameSanitizer
CanonValidator place/time rules
ProposalBuilder place/time proposals
EventScannerLite place/time hints
Resolver place target binding
CommandRenderers: /location and /time
```
