```text
OperationLedger place/time/deadline keys
DirtyQueue place/time dirty targets
AuditLogger place/time lines
InvariantChecker place/time checks
CleanupManagerLite scene expiry shell
ReleaseCheckLite PC09 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to ActorReducer, InventoryReducer, QuestReducer, BodyReducer, ProgressionReducer, WorldBible import, region/faction systems, encounter/creature systems, CardSync live writes, or manual mutation commands.

---

# 5. Place state model

Places bucket should support:

```text
currentLocation
currentScene
records
travelIntent
visibleReferences
nearbyReferences
routeReferences
sceneHistory
warnings
notes
```

Recommended shape:

```text
places: {
  currentLocation: null,
  currentScene: null,
  records: {},
  travelIntent: null,
  visibleReferences: [],
  nearbyReferences: [],
  routeReferences: [],
  sceneHistory: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

Do not create multiple conflicting current locations.

---

# 6. PlaceRecord schema

Required place record fields:

```text
PlaceRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  normalizedName,
  type,
  status,
  regionKey,
  parentPlaceKey,
  knownToPlayer,
  currentStrength,
  lastConfirmedTurn,
  firstDiscoveredTurn,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  references,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Place identity rules:

```text
key is stable
key is family-scoped
key does not contain full raw output
key does not contain command/card/context text
key preserves meaningful proper names
key suppresses route/interstitial noise when named destination exists
```

Examples:

```text
The Drunken Huntsman -> drunken_huntsman
Market Lane -> market_lane
Old Mill -> old_mill
Chapel of Ash -> chapel_of_ash
North Gate Road -> north_gate_road only if road is actually the relevant place
```

---

# 7. Place type model

Allowed place types:

```text
room
building
street
road
path
landmark
settlement
region
wilderness
dungeon
interior
exterior
route
threshold
vehicle
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
road/path/route are often interstitial
building/room/street/landmark can be current scene/location if confirmed
region is broader than current scene
settlement is broader than street/building
vehicle can be current location if player is inside/on it
village well is a landmark, not the whole village
market lane is a street/scene, not the whole city
road to chapel is a route, not chapel arrival
chapel visible is a visible reference, not current location
```

---

# 8. SceneRecord schema

Current scene is more immediate than current location.

```text
SceneRecord {
  id,
  key,
  placeKey,
  displayName,
  summary,
  sceneType,
  activeActorKeys,
  sceneItemKeys,
  mood,
  dangerLevel,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  sourceOperationKey,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
scene can update without changing location
location can change and create/reset scene
scene summary must be concise
scene actors/items coordinate through DirtyQueue/Cleanup, not direct deletion by PlaceReducer unless explicit policy exists
```

---

# 9. TravelIntentRecord schema

Travel intent is what the player is trying to do, not where they are.

```text
TravelIntentRecord {
  id,
  targetPlaceKey,
  targetDisplayName,
  originPlaceKey,
  routeHint,
  status,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Statuses:

```text
intended
in_progress
interrupted
arrived
cancelled
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
I go to the chapel -> travel intent
I head toward the chapel -> travel intent
You enter the chapel -> arrival
You arrive at the chapel -> arrival
You can see the chapel -> visible reference, not travel intent unless player action says travel
```

---

# 10. PlaceReferenceRecord schema

Weak references must be tracked separately.

```text
PlaceReferenceRecord {
  id,
  placeKey,
  displayName,
  referenceType,
  strength,
  sourceOperationKey,
  sourceEventId,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Reference types:

```text
visible
nearby
mentioned
rumored
known
route_hint
background
memory
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
weak/medium references do not overwrite current location
visible references can appear in /location but not as current place
known references can support future travel target binding
background references should not spam context
```

---

# 11. Route/interstitial model

Routes and interstitials are common in AI Dungeon prose.

Examples:

```text
after the road
along the path
through the alley
past the gate
across the bridge
at the threshold
near the doorway
```

Rules:

```text
route/interstitial can be scene if the narrative stops there
route/interstitial should not beat a named arrival destination
route/interstitial may be stored as routeReference
route/interstitial should not reset destination if arrival is strong
```

Critical example:

```text
After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.
```

Expected:

```text
current location = The Drunken Huntsman
road = route reference
not current location
```

---

# 12. Arrival strength model

Place CanonValidator should score arrival strength.

```text
none
weak_reference
travel_intent
scene_confirmation
strong_arrival
explicit_location_set
```

Examples:

```text
You see the chapel from the market lane -> weak_reference
I head toward the chapel -> travel_intent
The market lane remains crowded -> scene_confirmation
You enter the chapel -> strong_arrival
You arrive at The Drunken Huntsman -> strong_arrival
```

Rules:

```text
strong_arrival can change currentLocation
scene_confirmation can update scene/current location if it matches current state
travel_intent does not change currentLocation
weak_reference does not change currentLocation
explicit_location_set is reserved for safe startup/structured state only, not commands
```

---

# 13. PlaceNameSanitizer depth

PlaceNameSanitizer must preserve named places and suppress route noise.

Must handle:

```text
The Drunken Huntsman -> The Drunken Huntsman
Chapel of Ash -> Chapel of Ash
market lane -> Market Lane
north gate road -> North Gate Road
Old Mill -> Old Mill
road to the chapel -> Chapel target + route road, depending clause
village well -> Village Well, not whole village
```

Must not create current locations from visible chapel, rumored city, book title place, Story Card place, command output location, context reminder, or route phrase when named destination exists.

---

# 14. Time state model

Time bucket should support:

```text
text
turn
sequence
lastAdvance
acceptedAdvances
pendingClaims
deadlines
agePolicy
warnings
notes
```

Rules:

```text
turn/sequence is script metadata, not necessarily in-world time
text can be loose in MVP
time advances require accepted event
age mutation requires stricter age policy
```

---

# 15. TimeAdvanceRecord schema

```text
TimeAdvanceRecord {
  id,
  key,
  amountText,
  normalizedAmount,
  unit,
  literal,
  affectsAge,
  sourceOperationKey,
  sourceEventId,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Units:

```text
moments
minutes
hours
days
weeks
months
years
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
literal accepted time passage can update lastAdvance/text
nonliteral/quote/dream time passage cannot
same output replay cannot duplicate time advance
years/months time passage may be pending for age mutation unless age policy is active
```

---

# 16. TimeClaimRecord schema

Uncertain time claims should be stored separately from accepted advances.

```text
TimeClaimRecord {
  id,
  claimText,
  claimType,
  confidence,
  sourceOperationKey,
  sourceEventId,
  createdTurn,
  status,
  notes
}
```

Claim types:

```text
quote
rumor
dream
metaphor
hypothetical
planning
uncertain_literal
calendar_reference
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
pending claim is not accepted time advance
pending claim does not age player
pending claim can be shown in /time if useful
```

---

# 17. DeadlineRecord schema

```text
DeadlineRecord {
  id,
  key,
  title,
  relatedQuestKey,
  relatedActorKey,
  relatedPlaceKey,
  dueText,
  dueTurn,
  status,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  completedTurn,
  missedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Deadline statuses:

```text
active
pending
completed
missed
cancelled
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
deadline mention creates pending/active deadline only if clear
deadline completion requires accepted completion event
deadline missed requires accepted time/deadline miss event
deadline does not auto-fail quest unless future policy says so
```

---

# 18. Age policy shell

Age updates are high-risk.

Required policy questions:

```text
is player age known?
is player species/race aging model known?
is time passage literal?
is time passage long enough?
is passage duplicate-safe?
is body/identity lock compatible?
is there a reason to update exact age instead of just recording time passage?
```

Rules:

```text
quoted "twenty years pass" does not age player
dream twenty years pass does not age player
narration practice does not age player
literal exile for twenty years may be accepted time passage
exact player age mutation may remain pending if age model incomplete
```

This chunk may record long literal passage without changing exact age unless age policy is implemented.

---

# 19. Place CanonValidator expansion

Accept travel intent when safe input/output says the player heads/goes/travels toward a destination but has not arrived. Result: update travelIntent, currentLocation unchanged.

Accept strong arrival when safe output directly confirms arrival/entry/entering/reaching destination, target is bound, and it is not command/context/card source, dream/quote only, hypothetical, or merely visible/nearby. Result: update currentLocation/currentScene and clear/mark matching travelIntent.

Accept scene confirmation when safe output confirms current scene/location details and target matches current place or is strong current-scene phrase. Result: update scene/confirm location without overwriting from weak reference.

Accept weak reference when safe source mentions visible/nearby/known/background place with no direct arrival. Result: reference record only.

---

# 20. Time CanonValidator expansion

Accept time advance when safe output directly narrates literal time passage, not quote/dream/metaphor/hypothetical/planning, not command/context/card, and operation key is unique.

Reject or pending when time wording is quoted, storytelling/narration practice, metaphorical, planning intent, hypothetical, prophecy, card/context/command text, or dream/vision.

Examples to reject or keep pending:

```text
You say, "Twenty years pass."
You feel like years pass.
In a dream, a century passes.
If a week passed, the town would change.
[AIDRPG] Time: three days passed.
```

Exact age update requires a stricter policy. Default safe result for long passage is accepted/pending time advance with age update pending/deferred unless age policy is active.

---

# 21. ProposalBuilder event types

Place event types:

```text
place.travel_intent_candidate
place.arrival_candidate
place.current_scene_confirm_candidate
place.visible_reference_candidate
place.nearby_reference_candidate
place.route_reference_candidate
place.known_reference_candidate
place.scene_transition_candidate
place.conflict_candidate
```

Time event types:

```text
time.advance_candidate
time.claim_candidate
time.age_candidate
time.deadline_candidate
time.deadline_complete_candidate
time.deadline_missed_candidate
time.rest_candidate
time.calendar_reference_candidate
```

Current chunk may commit travel intent, strong arrival, scene confirmation, visible/nearby/route references, literal time advance, pending/claim records, deadlines, and age pending/deferred status.

---

# 22. EventScannerLite place/time hints

Place hint families:

```text
enter/arrive/reach/step into
head toward/go to/travel to
see/visible/nearby/in the distance
road/path/bridge/gate/doorway/threshold
current scene confirmation phrases
```

Time hint families:

```text
moments pass
minutes/hours/days/weeks/months/years pass
later
at dawn/dusk/night/morning
deadline/due before/by/sunset/tomorrow
rest/sleep/wait/travel time
```

Must flag weakReference, travelIntentOnly, strongArrival, routeInterstitial, namedDestination, quotedSpeech, dreamOrVision, metaphor, hypothetical, planningOnly, commandSource, contextSource, cardSource, auditSource, duplicateRisk, and ageRisk.

Parser cannot mutate place/time state.

---

# 23. PlaceReducerLite behavior

PlaceReducerLite owns:

```text
places.currentLocation
places.currentScene
places.records
places.travelIntent
places.visibleReferences
places.nearbyReferences
places.routeReferences
places.sceneHistory
places.warnings
```

Travel intent commit:

```text
create/update TravelIntentRecord
status intended or in_progress
currentLocation unchanged
mark current_location/context dirty
audit compactly
```

Strong arrival commit:

```text
create/update PlaceRecord
set currentLocation
create/update currentScene
mark matching travelIntent arrived/clear if applicable
record SceneTransitionRecord
coordinate scene actor/item expiry through DirtyQueue/Cleanup shell
mark current_location/context dirty
audit compactly
```

Scene confirmation commit updates currentScene and confirms currentLocation only if target matches current place or is stronger current-scene phrase.

Weak/visible/nearby/reference commit creates a PlaceReferenceRecord without changing currentLocation.

Route/interstitial commit stores routeReference and does not beat a named destination arrival.

---

# 24. TimeReducerLite behavior

TimeReducerLite owns:

```text
time.text
time.turn
time.sequence
time.lastAdvance
time.acceptedAdvances
time.pendingClaims
time.deadlines
time.agePolicy
time.warnings
```

Time advance commit:

```text
check operation key
create TimeAdvanceRecord
update lastAdvance
update time.text if useful
update deadlines only if explicit deadline policy supports it
mark current_location, quest_log, context dirty
audit compactly
```

Time claim commit creates TimeClaimRecord without updating lastAdvance, age, or deadlines.

Deadline create/complete/miss commits update DeadlineRecord and do not auto-complete/fail quests unless QuestReducer receives a separate accepted event or future policy exists.

Age update default policy: pending/deferred unless agePolicy active and all safety checks pass.

---

# 25. Reducer defensive guards

PlaceReducer must reject/no-op if routed result has command/context/card/audit/debug source, visible/nearby as arrival, travel intent as arrival, route phrase overriding named destination, weak mention as current location, dream/quote-only location change, missing place key, or wrong owner reducer.

TimeReducer must reject/no-op if routed result has command/context/card/audit/debug source, quoted time skip, dream time skip, metaphor, hypothetical/planning only, age update without policy, duplicate time advance, missing operation key for repeat-sensitive advance, or wrong owner reducer.

Reducers must not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 26. Operation keys

Place keys:

```text
place.intent.<placeKey>.<inputHash>
place.arrive.<placeKey>.<clauseHash>
place.scene.confirm.<placeKey>.<clauseHash>
place.reference.visible.<placeKey>.<clauseHash>
place.reference.nearby.<placeKey>.<clauseHash>
place.reference.route.<placeKey>.<clauseHash>
place.transition.<fromPlaceKey>.<toPlaceKey>.<clauseHash>
```

Time/deadline keys:

```text
time.advance.<amountKey>.<clauseHash>
time.claim.<claimType>.<claimHash>
time.age.<amountKey>.<clauseHash>
time.deadline.create.<deadlineKey>.<sourceHash>
time.deadline.complete.<deadlineKey>.<sourceHash>
time.deadline.miss.<deadlineKey>.<sourceHash>
```

Rules:

```text
arrival keys prevent repeated scene transitions on replay
time advance keys prevent repeated time passage
deadline keys prevent duplicate deadlines
age keys are durable/high-risk
```

---

# 27. Dirty targets

Place/time updates should mark:

```text
current_location
context
quest_log when deadlines/objective locations are affected
social_memory when present actor scene association changes
inventory_equipment when scene items need expiry/display refresh
card_registry only through mirror dirty, not direct write
```

---

# 28. Scene actor/item coordination shell

When strong arrival changes scene:

```text
PlaceReducer records scene transition
DirtyQueue marks social_memory/current_location/inventory_equipment/context
CleanupManagerLite may expire scene-only actors/items if policy exists
ActorReducer remains owner of actor records
InventoryReducer remains owner of scene item records
```

PlaceReducer must not directly delete actor or inventory records unless a narrow, tested scene-expiry contract exists.

MVP safe behavior:

```text
mark scene transition
let cleanup/support layer expire scene-only records conservatively
preserve persistent actors/items/history
```

---

# 29. `/location` updates

`/location` must display:

```text
Current location
Current scene
Travel intent
Visible references
Nearby references
Route/interstitial references
Known places
Scene transition history summary
Warnings
```

Display rules:

```text
visible place not shown as current
travel intent not shown as arrived
route not shown as destination when named destination exists
unknown current location shown unknown, not guessed
```

Example:

```text
[AIDRPG] Location

Current location:
- Market Lane

Current scene:
- Crowded market street near the old fountain

Travel intent:
- Chapel of Ash — intended destination, not arrived

Visible references:
- Chapel of Ash — visible from Market Lane

Route references:
- North Gate Road — route/interstitial, not destination
```

---

# 30. `/time` updates

`/time` must display:

```text
Current time text
Turn / sequence marker if used
Last accepted time advance
Pending/nonliteral time claims
Deadlines
Age policy status
Warnings
```

Display rules:

```text
command does not advance time
pending claims not shown as accepted advances
age update status shown separately
deadlines not marked complete/missed unless accepted
```

---

# 31. ContextBuilder place/time packets

Place packet should include current location, current scene, travel intent if active, important visible/nearby references, and scene-relevant actors/items already owned by their systems.

Time packet should include current time text if known, recent accepted time advance, urgent deadlines, and age policy warnings only when relevant.

Do not include full place registry, all known places, weak references as current location, pending time claims as accepted passage, hidden lore locations before discovery, or old quarantined place/time claims.

---

# 32. IntegrityChecker place/time checks

Add checks:

```text
only one currentLocation
currentScene has placeKey or unknown-safe state
travelIntent separate from currentLocation
visible/nearby/route references not marked current
route/interstitial not overriding named destination
arrival history operation keys exist
accepted time advances have operation keys
time claims not in acceptedAdvances
age update not applied without policy
active deadlines have stable keys/statuses
command/card/context source not active place/time truth
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad records. It may not teleport the player or advance time.

---

# 33. Cleanup place/time protections

Cleanup may compact old low-value place references, route reference spam, time claim spam, scene history summaries, and resolved deadline warnings if archive policy exists.

Cleanup must not delete currentLocation, currentScene, active travelIntent, active deadlines, accepted time advances needed for audit, important scene transition history, or known major places.

Cleanup must not convert weak references into current location or erase accepted time passage.

---

# 34. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC09-specific gates:

```text
PC09-G01 PlaceRecord schema exists or equivalent stable place record exists
PC09-G02 SceneRecord schema exists or equivalent current scene structure exists
PC09-G03 TravelIntentRecord schema exists or equivalent travel intent structure exists
PC09-G04 PlaceReferenceRecord schema exists or equivalent visible/nearby/route reference structure exists
PC09-G05 TimeAdvanceRecord schema exists or equivalent accepted time advance structure exists
PC09-G06 TimeClaimRecord schema exists or equivalent pending claim structure exists
PC09-G07 DeadlineRecord schema exists or equivalent deadline structure exists
PC09-G08 PlaceNameSanitizer preserves named destinations and suppresses route noise
PC09-G09 CanonValidator rejects weak visible/nearby reference as current location
PC09-G10 CanonValidator separates travel intent from arrival
PC09-G11 CanonValidator rejects quoted/dream/metaphorical/planning time passage
PC09-G12 PlaceReducer rejects unsafe routed place events
PC09-G13 TimeReducer rejects unsafe routed time/age events
PC09-G14 duplicate arrival/time/deadline keys exist
PC09-G15 /location displays current/travel/reference/route states honestly
PC09-G16 /time displays accepted/pending/deadline/age-policy states honestly
PC09-G17 command/context/card place/time text cannot become canon
PC09-G18 cleanup does not delete current location/time/deadlines
PC09-G19 world bible/region/faction/encounter systems remain deferred
PC09-G20 player-breaker place/time tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if weak place references overwrite current location, travel intent becomes arrival, quoted/dream time changes age, or command/card/context place/time text mutates state.

---

# 35. Required tests

## PC09-001 — Visible chapel no overwrite

Output:

```text
The chapel is visible from the market lane.
```

Expected:

```text
chapel visible reference
currentLocation remains Market Lane if already there
```

## PC09-002 — Nearby place no overwrite

```text
The old mill is nearby beyond the trees.
```

Expected: old mill nearby reference; currentLocation unchanged.

## PC09-003 — Travel intent not arrival

```text
I head toward the chapel.
```

Expected: travelIntent Chapel; currentLocation unchanged.

## PC09-004 — Strong arrival updates location

```text
You enter the chapel.
```

Expected: currentLocation Chapel; matching travelIntent arrived/cleared; currentScene updated.

## PC09-005 — Road loses to named destination

```text
After the road, you enter The Drunken Huntsman.
```

Expected: currentLocation The Drunken Huntsman; road route reference only.

## PC09-006 — Route as scene only when narrative stops there

```text
You stop on the narrow bridge as fog rolls over the river.
```

Expected: bridge may become current scene/location if strong enough; not discarded merely because bridge is route-like.

## PC09-007 — Current scene confirmation

Setup: currentLocation = Market Lane. Output:

```text
The market lane remains crowded and loud.
```

Expected: currentScene confirmed/updated; currentLocation remains Market Lane.

## PC09-008 — Weak mention of distant city no overwrite

```text
Someone mentions the capital far to the east.
```

Expected: capital mentioned/known reference if useful; currentLocation unchanged.

## PC09-009 — Command location non-canon

Run `/location`, feed command output through Output hook. Expected: no location/travel/reference mutation.

## PC09-010 — Story Card location prose non-canon

Story Card text:

```text
The player is currently in the Chapel of Ash.
```

Expected: no currentLocation mutation; quarantine/audit only if surfaced.

## PC09-011 — Context location prose non-canon

Context text:

```text
Current location: The Drunken Huntsman.
```

Expected: no currentLocation mutation.

## PC09-012 — Literal night passes

```text
The night passes uneventfully while you rest.
```

Expected: accepted time advance; lastAdvance updated; no healing unless separate recovery event accepted.

## PC09-013 — Rest intent no time advance

```text
I plan to rest until morning.
```

Expected: intent only; no accepted time advance until output confirms.

## PC09-014 — Quote time skip rejected

```text
You say, "Twenty years pass," while practicing narration.
```

Expected: no accepted time advance; no age update.

## PC09-015 — Dream time skip rejected

```text
In a dream, a century passes before you wake.
```

Expected: no accepted real time advance; no age update.

## PC09-016 — Metaphorical years rejected

```text
The wait feels like years.
```

Expected: no literal time advance; possible metaphor claim only.

## PC09-017 — Literal long passage age cautious

```text
Twenty years pass during your exile.
```

Expected: literal time advance accepted or pending by safety mode; exact age update pending/deferred unless age policy active.

## PC09-018 — Duplicate time advance skipped

Repeat night-passes output. Expected: time advance not duplicated; operation key skip/audit.

## PC09-019 — Deadline creation

```text
Old Bren says the wolves must be cleared before sunset.
```

Expected: deadline active/pending linked if bound; quest not failed/completed automatically.

## PC09-020 — Deadline completion explicit

```text
You return before sunset, meeting Old Bren's deadline.
```

Expected: deadline completed if bound; quest/reward changes only through separate accepted events.

## PC09-021 — Deadline missed explicit

```text
Sunset passes, and you miss Old Bren's deadline.
```

Expected: deadline missed if bound; quest failure only if separate event/policy applies.

## PC09-022 — Command time non-canon

Run `/time`, feed command output through Output hook. Expected: no time/deadline/age mutation.

## PC09-023 — Story Card time prose non-canon

Story Card text:

```text
Ten years have passed since the player entered town.
```

Expected: no accepted time advance; no age update; quarantine/audit only if surfaced.

## PC09-024 — Scene transition marks dirty

Strong arrival occurs. Expected: dirty targets include current_location and context; social_memory/inventory_equipment marked for scene actor/item refresh if applicable.

## PC09-025 — Location display honest

State: currentLocation Market Lane, travelIntent Chapel of Ash, visibleReference Chapel of Ash, routeReference North Gate Road. `/location` should show Market Lane current, Chapel as travel/visible not current, Road as route.

## PC09-026 — Time display honest

State: accepted night passed, pending quoted twenty years, active before-sunset deadline, cautious age policy. `/time` should separate all of these.

## PC09-027 — Cleanup preserves current place/time/deadlines

Cleanup must preserve currentLocation, currentScene, active deadlines, and accepted time advances while compacting only low-value old references.

# 38. Parser/proposal/validator changes

Forbidden: world bible import parser, region/faction pressure parser, encounter generator parser, manual teleport/time mutation parser, and card/context place/time import.

# 39. Reducer changes

Allowed: PlaceReducer travel intent/arrival/scene confirmation/weak reference/route behavior; TimeReducer time advance/time claim/deadline behavior; unsafe routed-data rejection; dirty targets; duplicate skip results.

Forbidden: region/faction mutation, world event simulation, random encounter generation, manual teleport/settime reducer, age update without policy, and quest failure/payment from deadline unless separately validated.

Forbidden:


# 41. Beginner implementation order

1. Copy accepted actor/social build: `WorldLedgerV0.17.8_ACTOR_SOCIAL_DEPTH_ACCEPTED.txt`.
2. Create working file: `WorldLedgerV0.17.9_PLACE_TIME_DEPTH_WORKING.txt`.
3. Add/verify place/time helpers: `ensurePlacesShape`, `ensureTimeShape`, `makePlaceRecord`, `makeSceneRecord`, `makeTravelIntentRecord`, `makePlaceReferenceRecord`, `makeTimeAdvanceRecord`, `makeTimeClaimRecord`, `makeDeadlineRecord`, `normalizeArrivalStrength`, `normalizePlaceType`, `isWeakPlaceReference`, `isStrongArrival`, `isUnsafePlaceSource`, and `isUnsafeTimeSource`.
4. Deepen PlaceNameSanitizer for named destinations, routes, visible/nearby references, and landmark precision.
5. Update place/time CanonValidator rules for travel intent, arrival, scene confirmation, weak references, literal time advance, nonliteral claims, deadlines, and cautious age policy.
6. Update ProposalBuilder to carry place target, event strength, reference type, route/named destination info, time amount/unit/literal flag, deadline target, safety flags, and operation key suggestions.
7. Update PlaceReducerLite for travel intent, strong arrival, scene confirmation, weak references, route references, and scene transitions.
8. Update TimeReducerLite for literal time advance, claims, deadlines, and age pending/deferred policy.
9. Update `/location` and `/time` displays.
10. Update ContextBuilder place/time packets.
11. Update IntegrityChecker place/time checks.
12. Add PC09-G01 through PC09-G20 ReleaseCheck gates.
13. Add PC09-001 through PC09-027 tests.
14. Run `/foundationcheck`, `/releasecheck`, `/integrity`, `/playerbreakercheck`, `/regressioncheck`, `/location`, and `/time`.
15. Save accepted build as `WorldLedgerV0.17.9_PLACE_TIME_DEPTH_ACCEPTED.txt`.

---

# 42. What this chunk produces

Production Chunk 09 should produce:

```text
WorldLedger V0.17.9 place/time-depth accepted script if code changes are made
place/time helper depth
PlaceReducer travel/arrival/reference behavior
TimeReducer time/deadline behavior
PlaceNameSanitizer depth
place/time CanonValidator refinement
/location and /time display improvement
place/time context packets
place/time integrity checks
PC09 ReleaseCheck gates
PC09 regression/player-breaker tests
place/time acceptance report
updated planning zip with Production Chunk 09 added
```

It should not produce full world bible import, full region/faction model, encounter generator, manual teleport/settime commands, calendar/weather engine, age mutation without strict policy, live CardSync writes, generated place cards, or old card prose place/time import.

---

# 43. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 09 passes if:

```text
current location/current scene/travel intent/references/routes are separated
weak visible/nearby mentions do not overwrite location
travel intent does not equal arrival
strong arrival updates location safely
road/interstitial does not beat named destination
literal time advances are duplicate-safe
quoted/dream/metaphorical/planning time does not advance time or age
age updates are cautious/pending unless policy active
deadlines are stateful and do not auto-complete/fail quests without rules
PlaceReducer and TimeReducer reject unsafe routed events
/location and /time display honestly
command/context/card place/time text cannot become canon
cleanup preserves current location/time/deadlines
PC09 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC09 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if exact calendar system is deferred, weather/time-of-day details are loose, age mutation is pending/deferred, region/faction/world pressure systems are deferred, scene-only actor/item expiry is conservative, or long-route travel simulation is not active.

## FAIL

Fail and stop if visible chapel becomes current location, heading toward chapel becomes arrival, road beats The Drunken Huntsman, command/context/card location text changes currentLocation, quoted or dream time skip advances time/age, repeated time passage duplicates, rest intent advances time without output confirmation, deadline mention completes/fails quest automatically, cleanup deletes current location/current scene/active deadline, or PlaceReducer/TimeReducer trusts unsafe routed data.

---

# 44. Handoff to Production Chunk 10

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 10 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 10 — Formula Matrix and Progression Baseline
```

Production Chunk 10 may use time and place context for training, rest, travel, and progression pacing, but it must not turn every time passage into XP or every region into world scaling.

Production Chunk 09 must leave place/time state reliable enough that future chunks can ask:

```text
Where is the player now?
What is the current scene?
Where is the player trying to go?
What places are only visible or nearby?
Did time literally pass?
Was time only mentioned, quoted, dreamed, or planned?
Are deadlines active, completed, or missed?
Can age safely update, or should it stay pending?
```

---

# 45. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 09 is where the world stops sliding under the player’s feet.

Final rule:

```text
A place must be arrived at before it becomes current.
A time skip must be literal before it changes time.
A deadline must be explicit before it matters.
Age must be protected.
Weak references, command text, context text, card prose, dreams, quotes, routes, and plans cannot rewrite reality.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely begin progression and formula baseline work in Production Chunk 10.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 10 — Formula Matrix and Progression Baseline

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 10 — Formula Matrix and Progression Baseline  
**Production sequence:** 10 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.9 place/time-depth accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen XP, level, progression events, formula registry, progression history, stat-baseline placeholders, and world-scaling boundaries without vague XP, duplicate XP, hidden rubber-band scaling, or unsafe stat automation.  
**Status:** Core systems production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 09 — Place, Travel, Time, Scene, and Deadline Depth.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 10 exists because progression is where many RPG systems silently become unfair, inconsistent, or impossible to test.

Old attempts and rough builds often risked:

```text
vague “learning experience” becoming XP
ability training awarding XP automatically
quest reward promises granting XP before payment/completion
repeat outputs duplicating XP/levels
level formulas changing mid-save
stats increasing without owner/rule/test support
world enemies rubber-banding to player level
high-level numbers becoming meaningless
manual /setlevel shortcuts corrupting balance
```

This chunk creates the formula and progression baseline.

It does **not** build the full final stat engine yet.

It makes progression deterministic, source-bound, duplicate-safe, inspectable, and ready for Production Chunk 11.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Vague experience is not XP.
Training is not XP unless an exact XP event says so.
Quest promise is not XP.
Quest completion is not XP unless reward/XP event is accepted.
Level does not change without formula authority.
Stats do not change without owner rules.
World threat does not rubber-band to player level.
Duplicate output cannot duplicate XP.
Formula changes must be versioned.
```

Progression truth must be formula-driven, versioned, repeat-safe, and explainable.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 09 made place/time safer.

Progression comes next because later chunks depend on it:

```text
stats and derived values
body resources and conditions
combat damage/healing
ability reliability and costs
world-threat benchmarks
creature/encounter difficulty
race/form consequences
quest rewards
long-run advancement
```

If progression is not locked before stats and combat, later code will invent numbers ad hoc.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 10 may change:

```text
Progression schema helpers
ProgressionReducerLite
FormulaRegistry / FormulaMatrix shell
CanonValidator progression/XP rules
ProposalBuilder progression event types
EventScannerLite progression/XP hints
CommandRenderers /sheet and progression/status display
ContextBuilder progression packet
OperationLedger XP/level/progression keys
DirtyQueue progression targets
IntegrityChecker progression checks
ReleaseCheck progression gates
Regression/player-breaker progression tests
```

Production Chunk 10 may add:

```text
FormulaProfile schema
FormulaRuleRecord schema
XPEventRecord schema
LevelEventRecord schema
ProgressionHistoryRecord schema
ProgressionMilestoneRecord schema
WorldScalingBoundaryRecord schema
explicit XP validation rules
level calculation boundary
formula versioning
no-rubber-band world rule
progression audit summaries
```

Production Chunk 10 may not add:

```text
full stat/derived value engine
full level 1–1000 final balancing matrix unless Formula Appendix locks it
full combat XP automation
full class/talent tree
ability XP economy
manual /setlevel /givexp commands
reputation/faction progression
world enemy auto-scaling
absorption/evolution stat gain
```

Stats and derived values are Production Chunk 11. Combat is Production Chunk 12. World threat benchmark is Production Chunk 14.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
ProgressionState helpers
ProgressionReducerLite
FormulaRegistry / FormulaMatrix helper
CanonValidator progression rules
ProposalBuilder progression proposals
EventScannerLite XP/progression hints
Resolver progression target binding
CommandRenderers: /sheet, /status if needed, /progression if supported
ContextBuilderLite progression packet
OperationLedger progression/XP/level keys
DirtyQueue progression dirty targets
AuditLogger progression lines
InvariantChecker progression checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC10 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
BodyReducer except resource/stat interactions remain deferred
AbilityReducer except no-direct-XP guard
QuestReducer except XP reward split/deferred contract
InventoryReducer
ActorReducer
PlaceReducer
TimeReducer except time passage does not grant XP automatically
Combat systems
World threat/encounter systems beyond no-rubber-band boundary
manual mutation commands
```

---

# 5. Progression state model

Progression bucket should support:

```text
level
currentXP
totalXP
xpToNext
stats
baseStats
modifiers
derivedSnapshot
statXP
levelHistory
xpHistory
formulaProfile
formulasEnabled
milestones
warnings
notes
```

Recommended shape:

```text
progression: {
  level: 1,
  currentXP: 0,
  totalXP: 0,
  xpToNext: null,
  stats: {},
  baseStats: {},
  modifiers: [],
  derivedSnapshot: {},
  statXP: {},
  levelHistory: [],
  xpHistory: [],
  formulaProfile: null,
  formulasEnabled: false,
  milestones: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

If the current script already tracks progression differently, this chunk should normalize or bridge without deleting valid data.

Do not invent high-level stats if formulas are not active.

---

# 6. FormulaProfile schema

FormulaProfile controls which progression formulas are active.

```text
FormulaProfile {
  profileId,
  profileName,
  version,
  source,
  active,
  locked,
  xpCurveId,
  levelCap,
  statFormulaSetId,
  derivedFormulaSetId,
  worldScalingPolicyId,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
active formula profile must be explicit
locked profile cannot change silently mid-save
formula source must be known: Formula Appendix, setup, or current build default
changing formula profile requires migration/audit
if formula profile missing, XP can be tracked but level-up may be pending/deferred
```

---

# 7. FormulaRuleRecord schema

Formula rules should be inspectable.

```text
FormulaRuleRecord {
  ruleId,
  family,
  name,
  version,
  expressionType,
  expressionText,
  enabled,
  source,
  inputs,
  outputs,
  caps,
  clamps,
  roundingPolicy,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Families:

```text
xp_curve
level
base_stat
derived_stat
resource
condition_modifier
ability_modifier
world_threat_boundary
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
formula rule must declare inputs and outputs
formula rule must not mutate state directly
formula evaluator returns computed result only
reducers decide whether state changes
```

---

# 8. XPEventRecord schema

XP events must be explicit and duplicate-safe.

```text
XPEventRecord {
  id,
  key,
  amount,
  sourceType,
  sourceKey,
  reason,
  relatedQuestKey,
  relatedAbilityKey,
  relatedActorKey,
  relatedPlaceKey,
  operationKey,
  acceptedTurn,
  applied,
  duplicateOf,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Allowed source types:

```text
explicit_story_xp
quest_reward_xp
structured_startup
system_award_later
manual_deferred
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
amount must be numeric and positive
source must be safe
operationKey must exist
vague experience is rejected
training does not grant XP unless exact XP event exists
quest promise does not grant XP
command/card/context text cannot grant XP
```

---

# 9. LevelEventRecord schema

Level changes must be formula-authorized.

```text
LevelEventRecord {
  id,
  key,
  fromLevel,
  toLevel,
  totalXPAtLevel,
  formulaProfileId,
  operationKey,
  acceptedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
level cannot decrease unless a future special spec supports it
level cannot skip unexpectedly unless formula says totalXP crosses multiple thresholds
level-up requires active/locked formula profile or explicit safe policy
level-up does not auto-assign stats unless PC11 supports it
```

---

# 10. ProgressionHistoryRecord schema

```text
ProgressionHistoryRecord {
  historyId,
  eventType,
  summary,
  sourceOperationKey,
  relatedKeys,
  turn,
  notes
}
```

Event types:

```text
xp_gain
level_up
formula_profile_set
formula_profile_warning
xp_rejected
xp_duplicate_skipped
progression_deferred
```

History should be compact and capped/summarized later, but durable XP/level operation keys must remain protected.

---

# 11. ProgressionMilestoneRecord schema

Milestones allow story-relevant progression without messy stat changes.

```text
ProgressionMilestoneRecord {
  id,
  key,
  title,
  family,
  status,
  sourceOperationKey,
  relatedAbilityKey,
  relatedQuestKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Examples:

```text
first confirmed XP gain
first level-up
first stabilized ability
first major quest completion
survived first deadly injury
```

Milestones do not trigger hidden rewards unless future rules say so.

---

# 12. WorldScalingBoundaryRecord schema

This chunk must explicitly prevent rubber-band scaling.

```text
WorldScalingBoundaryRecord {
  id,
  policyName,
  enabled,
  rule,
  worldLevelMode,
  playerScalingAllowed,
  regionThreatAuthority,
  creatureThreatAuthority,
  notes
}
```

Required policy:

```text
No automatic rubber-band scaling.
```

Rules:

```text
player level does not automatically change all enemies
region/creature/world bible threat authority controls later threat modeling
low-level player can encounter high-threat region if story/world supports it
high-level player does not make all rats legendary
world threat benchmark comes later in PC14
```

---

# 13. Formula activation levels

## 13.1 Stage 0 — XP tracked, level pending

Allowed if formula profile missing.

```text
XPEventRecord can be stored
level does not update automatically
ReleaseCheck warns formula profile missing
```

## 13.2 Stage 1 — Simple locked XP curve

Allowed if formula profile exists.

```text
numeric XP events update totalXP/currentXP
level calculated from locked formula/table
xpToNext computed
level history recorded
```

## 13.3 Stage 2 — Stat formulas

Deferred to PC11 unless Formula Appendix and tests are fully integrated.

## 13.4 Stage 3 — World threat formulas

Deferred to PC14.

---

# 14. XP source rules

Accepted XP sources:

```text
safe output explicitly says “You gain 20 XP.”
validated quest reward payment/completion produces exact XP reward and ProgressionReducer is active
structured startup exact XP if before lock and source is safe
future combat reward if Combat Event Model supports it
future system award if owner/rule/test/gate exists
```

Rejected XP sources:

```text
You learn from the experience.
This was a valuable experience.
You feel stronger.
You are more skilled now.
The quest promises 20 XP.
The quest board lists 20 XP.
[AIDRPG] XP: +20.
Context says you gained 20 XP.
Story Card says the player is level 10.
```

Pending/deferred XP sources:

```text
quest promises XP but not paid/completed
teacher says training will make you stronger
long training montage without exact XP
combat victory without XP rule active
```

---

# 15. XP and ability training separation

Ability training and XP are separate systems.

```text
I train Ember Ward all afternoon.
```

Expected:

```text
ability training evidence may update
XP unchanged
```

```text
After training, you gain 10 XP.
```

Expected:

```text
ability evidence may update if ability target exists
XPEventRecord +10 only if safe output and exact amount accepted
```

Rules:

```text
trainingCount does not equal XP
useCount does not equal XP
ability stabilization does not grant XP unless separate accepted XP event exists
```

---

# 16. XP and quest reward separation

Quest reward handling was locked in PC07.

Rules:

```text
XP reward promise does not grant XP
XP reward ready does not grant XP unless policy explicitly says ready applies; default no
XP reward paid/awarded can grant XP only once through ProgressionReducer
quest completion alone does not grant XP unless validated XP reward event exists
```

Preferred split result:

```text
reward.payment_candidate -> QuestReducer marks reward paid
xp.reward_candidate -> ProgressionReducer adds XP if exact and active
```

Both share reward payment/XP key.

---

# 17. XP and combat separation

Combat is not fully active until PC12.

Rules:

```text
defeating enemy does not automatically award XP in PC10
combat XP remains pending/deferred unless exact XP output occurs
future Combat Event Model may emit explicit XP result if spec allows
```

---

# 18. Level formula requirements

If level automation is active, it must be formula-driven.

```text
level is derived from totalXP or currentXP according to locked profile
xpToNext computed from next threshold
levelHistory records level-ups
level-up is duplicate-safe
level-up does not auto-assign stats unless PC11 enables it
```

If formula is not locked:

```text
XP tracked
level-up pending/deferred
ReleaseCheck WARN, not fake PASS as full progression
```

---

# 19. Stat automation boundary

PC10 prepares stat baseline but does not fully implement PC11.

Allowed:

```text
baseStats placeholder
modifiers placeholder
derivedSnapshot placeholder
statXP placeholder
formula rule records for stat family disabled
/stat or /sheet display shows stat automation deferred/limited
```

Forbidden:

```text
random STR/DEX/INT increases from training
HP max increases from level without formula
ability use increasing stats automatically
world threat scaling from stats
manual stat commands
```

---

# 20. Progression CanonValidator expansion

CanonValidator must enforce XP/progression safety.

## 20.1 Accept XP gain when

All are true:

```text
source is safe story output or safe structured startup
local clause explicitly says exact numeric XP gained/awarded/received
target is progression/XP, not vague experience
amount is positive number
not promised/ready/listed/deferred only
not command/context/card/audit/debug source
operation key not duplicate
```

## 20.2 Reject XP gain when

Any are true:

```text
vague experience
feels stronger
training only
quest promise only
quest board listing only
combat victory without XP event
output command/debug/context/card source
negative or nonnumeric amount
manual command
```

## 20.3 Accept level-up when

```text
formula profile active and XP threshold crossed
or safe structured startup explicitly sets level before lock
or future owner/rule/test/gate supports level change
```

## 20.4 Reject level-up when

```text
Story Card says level 10
command says level 10
context says level 10
NPC boasts you are level 10
manual /setlevel in MVP
formula profile missing and no safe structured source
```

---

# 21. Progression ProposalBuilder event types

Add or verify event types:

```text
progression.xp_gain_candidate
progression.xp_reward_candidate
progression.xp_rejected_candidate
progression.level_up_candidate
progression.level_set_candidate
progression.formula_profile_candidate
progression.stat_gain_deferred_candidate
progression.milestone_candidate
progression.world_scaling_boundary_candidate
```

Current chunk may commit safe:

```text
exact XP gain
XP reward if exact and split result active
formula profile set/lock if source is safe
level-up if formula active
milestone records
world scaling boundary record
```

Stat gains remain deferred to PC11 unless explicitly scoped.

---

# 22. EventScannerLite progression hints

EventScannerLite may detect progression hints, but detection is not canon.

Allowed scan families:

```text
XP gained/awarded/received
level up/reach level
experience points
reward XP
formula profile/setup progression
feel stronger/valuable experience as rejected/vague hints
```

Must flag:

```text
exactNumericXP
vagueExperience
promiseOnly
readyOnly
trainingOnly
combatOnly
commandSource
contextSource
cardSource
auditSource
manualMutation
negativeAmount
nonnumericAmount
duplicateRisk
formulaRequired
```

Parser cannot mutate progression.

---

# 23. ProgressionReducerLite detailed behavior

ProgressionReducerLite owns:

```text
progression.level
progression.currentXP
progression.totalXP
progression.xpToNext
progression.levelHistory
progression.xpHistory
progression.formulaProfile
progression.milestones
progression.warnings
```

## 23.1 XP gain commit

Accepted XP gain should:

```text
1. Confirm validation accepted.
2. Confirm safe source and exact positive amount.
3. Confirm operation key.
4. If duplicate, return skipped_duplicate.
5. Add XPEventRecord.
6. Increase totalXP/currentXP according to formula profile.
7. Add operation key.
8. Check level-up only if formula profile active.
9. Mark progression/player_sheet/context dirty.
10. Audit compactly.
```

## 23.2 Level-up commit

If formula profile active and threshold crossed:

```text
calculate new level
create LevelEventRecord
update level/xpToNext
record levelHistory
mark player_sheet/progression/context dirty
```

Do not assign stats automatically in PC10.

## 23.3 Formula profile commit

Accepted formula profile setup should:

```text
set formulaProfile
set formulasEnabled according to profile
lock profile if safe
record FormulaRuleRecords or profile reference
write audit/migration note
```

Do not silently change locked profile.

## 23.4 Milestone commit

Accepted milestone should:

```text
create ProgressionMilestoneRecord
not grant stats/XP unless separate accepted event exists
```

---

# 24. ProgressionReducer defensive guards

ProgressionReducer must reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
vague experience
training-only XP
quest promise XP
quest ready XP unless policy active
combat-only XP without XP event
manual mutation command
negative XP
nonnumeric XP
level set without formula/startup authority
stat gain without PC11 support
world scaling mutation
missing operation key
wrong owner reducer
```

Reducer must not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 25. Operation keys

Required progression operation keys:

```text
xp.gain.<amount>.<sourceKey>.<clauseHash>
xp.reward.<amount>.<questKey>.<rewardPaymentKey>
xp.startup.<amount>.<startupKey>
level.up.<fromLevel>.<toLevel>.<totalXP>.<formulaProfileId>
level.set.startup.<level>.<startupKey>
formula.profile.set.<profileId>.<version>.<sourceKey>
formula.profile.lock.<profileId>.<version>
progression.milestone.<milestoneKey>.<sourceHash>
world.scaling.boundary.<policyId>.<version>
```

Rules:

```text
XP and level keys are durable
formula profile keys are durable
recentKeys must not delete durable progression keys
same XP output replay cannot duplicate XP
same level threshold cannot add duplicate level history
```

---

Progression updates should mark:

```text
player_sheet
progression
context
quest_log if XP reward is tied to quest
body_status only later if stat/resource formulas affect body in PC11
```

```text
level
current XP

XP to next if formula active

warnings/deferred notes
```

Display rules:

```text
if formula profile missing, say level automation pending/deferred
if XP is tracked but level not calculated, say so
if stats are deferred, say stat automation deferred to PC11
if world scaling is disabled, say no automatic rubber-band scaling
```

# 29. ContextBuilder progression packet

ContextBuilder should include concise progression facts.

Include:

```text
level if known
important XP/level milestones
formula/deferred warning only if relevant
no-rubber-band reminder only if world/encounter context needs it
```

Do not include pending XP claims as accepted XP, stat placeholders as real stats, or world-scaling rules as enemy stats.

---

# 30. IntegrityChecker progression checks

Add checks:

```text
XP totals are numeric and nonnegative
level is positive integer
level matches formula profile if active
XPEventRecords have operation keys
LevelEventRecords have formula profile IDs
no duplicate XP event keys
no duplicate level-up history for same threshold
formula profile is explicit if formulasEnabled true
locked formula profile not silently changed
stat placeholders not treated as active stats
world scaling boundary exists and disables rubber-band scaling
command/card/context source not active progression truth
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad records.

It may not grant XP, levels, or stats.

---

# 31. Cleanup progression protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old low-value rejected XP audits
old duplicate XP warning spam
old verbose formula debug logs
progression warnings cap
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
XPEventRecords needed for audit
LevelEventRecords
formula profile records
formula profile operation keys
XP/level durable keys
progression milestones
world scaling boundary policy
```

---

# 32. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC10-specific gates:

```text
PC10-G01 FormulaProfile schema exists or equivalent profile structure exists
PC10-G02 FormulaRuleRecord schema exists or equivalent formula registry exists
PC10-G03 XPEventRecord schema exists or equivalent XP event structure exists
PC10-G04 LevelEventRecord schema exists or equivalent level history structure exists
PC10-G05 ProgressionHistoryRecord or compact progression audit exists
PC10-G06 WorldScalingBoundaryRecord exists and disables rubber-band scaling
PC10-G07 CanonValidator rejects vague/training/promise/card/context/command XP
PC10-G08 exact numeric XP gains are accepted only from safe sources
PC10-G09 ProgressionReducer rejects unsafe routed progression events
PC10-G10 XP/level duplicate operation keys exist and are durable
PC10-G11 level-up requires active formula profile or safe structured startup source
PC10-G12 stat automation remains deferred to PC11 unless fully specified
PC10-G13 quest XP reward follows split/deferred reward contract
PC10-G14 ability training does not award XP directly
PC10-G15 combat victory does not award XP without exact XP event/spec
PC10-G16 /sheet or /progression displays formula/progression status honestly
PC10-G17 command/context/card progression text cannot become canon
PC10-G18 cleanup does not delete XP/level/formula durable records
PC10-G19 world threat/encounter systems remain deferred and not rubber-banded
PC10-G20 player-breaker progression tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if vague XP, command/card/context XP, duplicate XP, manual level setting, or rubber-band scaling is possible.

---

# 33. Required tests

## PC10-001 — Exact XP gain accepted

Output:

```text
You gain 20 XP.
```

Expected:

```text
XPEventRecord amount 20
totalXP/currentXP update if active
operation key added
```

## PC10-002 — Vague experience rejected

```text
You learn from the experience.
```

Expected: no XP gain.

## PC10-003 — Feel stronger no stat/XP

```text
You feel stronger after the climb.
```

Expected: no XP and no stat increase.

## PC10-004 — Ability training no XP

```text
I train Ember Ward all afternoon.
```

Expected: ability training may update; XP unchanged.

## PC10-005 — Training plus exact XP

```text
After training Ember Ward, you gain 10 XP.
```

Expected: ability evidence if bound; XP +10 only once from exact XP clause.

## PC10-006 — Quest XP promise no XP

```text
Old Bren promises 20 XP if you clear the wolves.
```

Expected: XP reward promise/deferred; XP unchanged.

## PC10-007 — Quest board XP no XP

```text
The quest board lists a reward of 20 XP.
```

Expected: listed/promise reward only; XP unchanged.

## PC10-008 — Paid XP reward once

```text
Old Bren awards you the promised 20 XP.
```

Expected: XP +20 only if quest XP reward split/progression result accepted; operation key added.

## PC10-009 — Paid XP duplicate skipped

Repeat paid XP reward output.

Expected: no second XP gain.

## PC10-010 — Combat victory no XP by itself

```text
You defeat the wolf.
```

Expected: no XP unless exact XP event also appears.

## PC10-011 — Combat victory with exact XP

```text
You defeat the wolf and gain 5 XP.
```

Expected: XP +5 from exact XP clause only; no combat automation beyond this chunk.

## PC10-012 — Command progression non-canon

Run `/sheet` or `/progression`, then feed command output through Output hook.

Expected: no XP/level/stat mutation from command text.

## PC10-013 — Story Card level prose non-canon

```text
The player is level 10 with 5000 XP.
```

Expected: no level/XP mutation; quarantine/audit only if surfaced.

## PC10-014 — Context XP prose non-canon

```text
The player gained 20 XP last turn.
```

Expected: no XP mutation from context text.

## PC10-015 — Manual setlevel disabled

Run:

```text
/setlevel 999
/givexp 1000
```

Expected: disabled/safe unknown command; no mutation.

## PC10-016 — Level formula missing warning

Setup: XP exists, formulaProfile missing or inactive.

Expected: XP tracked; level-up pending/deferred; ReleaseCheck WARN, not fake full PASS.

## PC10-017 — Level-up with locked formula

Setup: formula profile active and XP threshold crossed.

Expected: level updates according to formula; LevelEventRecord created; no stat auto-allocation.

## PC10-018 — Duplicate level-up skipped

Repeat same XP/threshold event.

Expected: no duplicate level history.

## PC10-019 — Formula profile cannot silently change

Setup: locked formulaProfile = basic_curve_v1; incoming profile = different_curve.

Expected: warning/block/migration-needed; no silent formula replacement.

## PC10-020 — Negative XP rejected

```text
You gain -20 XP.
```

Expected: rejected/quarantined; no XP decrease unless future loss spec exists.

## PC10-021 — Nonnumeric XP rejected

```text
You gain many XP.
```

Expected: no XP gain.

## PC10-022 — World does not rubber-band

Setup: player level increases; threat profile exists.

Expected: threat not automatically rescaled to player level.

## PC10-023 — Low-level player can face high threat later

Expected: system does not force fair fight scaling; region/creature threat authority deferred to PC14.

## PC10-024 — Progression display honest

State: level 1, totalXP 20, formulaProfile inactive, stat automation deferred, world scaling disabled.

Expected: display says exactly that.

## PC10-025 — Cleanup preserves progression keys

Setup: XPEventRecord, LevelEventRecord, formula profile key, world scaling boundary.

Expected: durable progression records/keys preserved.

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.9_PLACE_TIME_DEPTH_ACCEPTED.txt
```

Create working file:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.10_PROGRESSION_BASELINE_WORKING.txt
```

```text
ensureProgressionShape
makeFormulaProfile

makeLevelEventRecord
makeProgressionHistoryRecord
makeProgressionMilestoneRecord
makeWorldScalingBoundaryRecord
normalizeXPAmount

```

## Step 3 — Add FormulaRegistry / FormulaMatrix shell

Start small:

```text
registry structure
profile lookup
formula active/locked checks
level threshold helper if formula active
no-rubber-band policy record
```

Do not implement all final stat math here.

## Step 4 — Update progression CanonValidator rules

Add strict accept/reject/pending logic for exact XP, vague experience, quest XP promise/ready/payment, training-only XP, combat-only XP, level-up authority, formula profile changes, and world scaling boundary.

## Step 5 — Update ProposalBuilder progression support

Ensure proposals carry XP amount, source type, related quest/ability/actor/place keys, formula profile id if relevant, safety flags, and operation key suggestion.

## Step 6 — Update ProgressionReducerLite

Implement safe commits for exact XP gain, XP reward if split result active, formula profile set/lock, level-up if formula active, milestone, and world scaling boundary.

## Step 7 — Update `/sheet` and optional `/progression`

Make progression display honest.

## Step 8 — Update ContextBuilder progression packet

Keep it concise and state-accurate.

## Step 9 — Update IntegrityChecker progression checks

Add XP totals, formula profile, duplicate XP/level, deferred stats, and no-rubber-band checks.

## Step 10 — Add PC10 ReleaseCheck gates

Add PC10-G01 through PC10-G20.

## Step 11 — Add PC10 tests

Add PC10-001 through PC10-025.

## Step 12 — Run baseline checks

Run:

```text
/foundationcheck
/releasecheck
/integrity
/playerbreakercheck
/regressioncheck
/sheet
/progression if available
```

## Step 13 — Save accepted progression build

Recommended output:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.10_PROGRESSION_BASELINE_ACCEPTED.txt
```


# 41. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 10 passes if:

```text
formula profile/registry baseline exists
exact XP gains are accepted from safe sources only
vague/training/promise/card/context/command XP is rejected or deferred
XP and level operations are duplicate-safe
level-up requires formula authority
stat automation remains deferred to PC11
quest XP reward uses split/deferred reward contract
ability training does not award XP directly
combat victory does not award XP without exact XP event/spec
world scaling boundary disables rubber-band scaling
/sheet or /progression displays status honestly
command/context/card progression text cannot become canon
cleanup preserves XP/level/formula durable records
PC10 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC10 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
formula profile exists but final XP curve is conservative
level automation is pending because formula profile is missing
stats are deferred to PC11
combat XP is deferred to PC12
world threat scaling is deferred to PC14
XP reward integration is conservative/deferred if split-result support is incomplete
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
vague experience grants XP
training grants XP by default
quest promise grants XP
command/context/card text grants XP or level
XP duplicates on replay
level changes without formula/startup authority
manual /setlevel or /givexp mutates state
stat increases happen without PC11 rules
world enemies automatically scale to player level
cleanup deletes XP/level/formula durable records
ProgressionReducer trusts unsafe routed data
```

---

# 42. Handoff to Production Chunk 11

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 11 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 11 — Stats, Derived Values, Conditions, and Invariant Checker
```

Production Chunk 11 may now safely build:

```text
base stats
modifiers
derived values
resource formulas
condition modifiers
stat invariants
safe stat changes
```

Production Chunk 10 must leave progression reliable enough that PC11 can ask:

```text
What level is the player?
How much XP is confirmed?
Which formula profile is active?
Are formulas locked?
Can stats safely derive from level/base/modifiers?
Are XP and level keys duplicate-safe?
Is world scaling explicitly not rubber-banded?
```

---

# 43. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 10 is where progression stops being vibes and starts being a controlled system.

Final rule:

```text
XP must be exact.
Levels must be formula-authorized.
Stats must wait for stat rules.
World threat must not rubber-band.
Every progression change must have source, key, formula status, and audit.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen stats, derived values, conditions, and invariants in Production Chunk 11.
