# AIDRPG Production Chunk 11 — Stats, Derived Values, Conditions, and Invariant Checker

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 11 — Stats, Derived Values, Conditions, and Invariant Checker  
**Production sequence:** 11 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.10 progression-baseline accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen base stats, stat modifiers, derived values, resource formulas, condition modifiers, invariant checking, stat audit, and safe repair/quarantine behavior without vague stat gains, hidden recalculation drift, impossible values, or cleanup deleting truth.  
**Status:** Core systems production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 10 — Formula Matrix and Progression Baseline.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 11 is the math-safety wall between the progression baseline and the combat system. It makes stats useful while preventing the classic failure pattern where vague narration, cleanup, or a stale formula silently rewrites the character.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Base stats are truth.
Derived stats are calculated snapshots.
Modifiers need source, owner, duration, status, and operation keys.
Conditions may affect stats, but cleanup cannot heal them.
Level does not grant stats unless a stat rule exists.
Vague strength is not STR.
Feeling faster is not DEX.
Training is not a stat increase by default.
Impossible values must warn, repair structurally, or quarantine.
No derived value becomes permanent truth without formula authority.
```

Stats must be deterministic, versioned, explainable, and ready for AI Dungeon gameplay, where output text can be messy and repeated.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 10 locked exact XP, formula profile authority, duplicate-safe level events, and no-rubber-band world scaling. Stats come next because future systems depend on them:

```text
combat damage/healing
body resources
ability costs and reliability
conditions and injuries
race/form consequences
creature threat comparison
world threat benchmarks
equipment modifiers
long-run balance
```

If stats and invariants are not safe before combat, combat will be built on unstable math and patch-loop risk.

---

# 3. Allowed scope

Production Chunk 11 may change:

```text
Progression stat helpers
Stat schema helpers
Modifier helpers
DerivedValueCalculator shell
ConditionModifierBridge
InvariantChecker
Body resource invariant checks
CanonValidator stat/progression modifier rules
ProposalBuilder stat/modifier event types
EventScannerLite stat/condition modifier hints
/sheet, /status, optional /stats
ContextBuilder stat/body packet
OperationLedger stat/modifier/resource keys
DirtyQueue stat/body targets
Cleanup protected paths
ReleaseCheck stat/invariant gates
Regression/player-breaker stat tests
```

It may not add:

```text
full combat damage engine
full equipment stat bonus system
full class/talent tree
race/lineage power automation
absorption/evolution stat gains
manual /setstat or /addstat commands
world enemy auto-scaling
full final level 1–1000 balancing unless the formula authority is locked and tested
```

---

# 4. Stat authority model

Every stat value must have an authority level:

```text
unknown
setup_confirmed
formula_derived
validated_story_change
temporary_modifier
condition_modifier
equipment_modifier_deferred
race_form_modifier_deferred
manual_deferred
invalid_quarantined
```

Rules:

```text
unknown does not become 10 by default unless the active profile says so
setup_confirmed can initialize base stats before lock
formula_derived calculates snapshots but does not overwrite base stats
validated_story_change requires strict validation and ReleaseCheck coverage
temporary_modifier must have source/duration/status
condition_modifier must link to an active condition/injury
manual_deferred is blocked/display only, not active mutation
```

---

# 5. StatBlock schema

Required shape:

```text
StatBlock {
  baseStats,
  modifiers,
  derivedValues,
  resources,
  formulaProfileId,
  lastCalculatedTurn,
  lastValidTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Recommended storage:

```text
progression.statBlock
```

or a clearly bridged equivalent across:

```text
progression.baseStats
progression.modifiers
progression.derivedSnapshot
body.resources
```

Do not create a second unrelated stat root.

---

# 6. BaseStatRecord schema

Required fields:

```text
BaseStatRecord {
  key,
  displayName,
  value,
  known,
  min,
  max,
  source,
  sourceOperationKey,
  locked,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Possible stat keys if the active formula profile supports them:

```text
strength
dexterity
constitution
intelligence
wisdom
charisma
willpower
perception
agility
endurance
```

The final active stat list must come from FormulaProfile / Formula Appendix authority. If no stat profile is active, base stats should stay unknown/deferred instead of being guessed.

---

# 7. StatModifierRecord schema

Required fields:

```text
StatModifierRecord {
  id,
  key,
  targetStatKey,
  modifierType,
  value,
  stackingPolicy,
  sourceFamily,
  sourceKey,
  sourceOperationKey,
  status,
  durationType,
  expiresTurn,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Modifier types:

```text
add
multiply
set_min
set_max
cap
floor
advantage_label
disadvantage_label
unknown
```

Source families:

```text
condition
injury
ability_deferred
equipment_deferred
race_form_deferred
quest_deferred
manual_deferred
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
condition modifiers require active condition/injury
expired modifiers do not affect derived values
duplicate modifiers require operation keys and stacking policy
equipment, ability, race, and form modifiers remain deferred unless their owner systems are active
```

---

# 8. DerivedValueRecord schema

Derived values are calculated outputs, not independent truth.

Required fields:

```text
DerivedValueRecord {
  key,
  displayName,
  value,
  known,
  formulaRuleId,
  formulaProfileId,
  inputsHash,
  calculatedTurn,
  stale,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Examples:

```text
maxHP
maxMP
maxEP
armorClass_deferred
carryCapacity_deferred
initiative_deferred
attackBonus_deferred
defense_deferred
```

Rules:

```text
derived values recalculate from base stats, active modifiers, and formula rules
derivedSnapshot can be stored for display/performance
stale derived values must be marked stale, not trusted
cleanup cannot delete formula inputs and leave a snapshot as truth
```

---

# 9. ResourceFormulaRecord schema

Body resources connect body and stats.

Required fields:

```text
ResourceFormulaRecord {
  resourceKey,
  formulaRuleId,
  formulaProfileId,
  currentPolicy,
  maxPolicy,
  clampPolicy,
  source,
  enabled,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
maxHP/maxMP/maxEP may derive from stat formulas only if formula active
current resource does not auto-fill to new max unless a policy says so
structural clamp is not healing
current cannot exceed max when both are known
current cannot go below 0 when known
```

---

# 10. ConditionModifierRecord schema

Conditions and injuries may apply modifiers.

Required fields:

```text
ConditionModifierRecord {
  id,
  conditionKey,
  injuryKey,
  targetStatKey,
  targetDerivedKey,
  effectLabel,
  modifierType,
  value,
  severityScale,
  active,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Examples:

```text
fatigued -> minor endurance/agility disadvantage label
exhausted -> major endurance/strength/agility disadvantage label
bleeding -> endurance / HP-risk label
sprained ankle -> movement/agility disadvantage label
blinded -> perception/attack labels deferred to combat
```

Unknown severity should use cautious labels, not guessed math.

---

# 11. StatChangeRecord schema

Direct stat changes must be rare and audited.

Required fields:

```text
StatChangeRecord {
  id,
  statKey,
  fromValue,
  toValue,
  delta,
  changeType,
  sourceType,
  sourceOperationKey,
  acceptedTurn,
  formulaProfileId,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Allowed change types:

```text
setup_initialize
formula_level_up
validated_story_change
migration_repair
quarantine_rejected
manual_deferred
unknown
```

Default direct story stat changes should be pending/deferred unless a strict stat-change policy is active.

---

# 12. InvariantViolationRecord and RepairActionRecord schemas

Invariant problems must be recorded clearly.

```text
InvariantViolationRecord {
  id,
  family,
  severity,
  path,
  expected,
  actualPreview,
  action,
  sourceOperationKey,
  detectedTurn,
  resolvedTurn,
  notes
}
```

```text
RepairActionRecord {
  id,
  violationId,
  actionType,
  path,
  beforePreview,
  afterPreview,
  source,
  safe,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Allowed actions:

```text
report_only
repair_structural
clamp_structural
quarantine_payload
block_release
```

Forbidden repairs:

```text
award stats
award XP
heal player
remove active condition
remove stable ability
remove active inventory
silently rewrite formula profile
```

---

# 13. Formula and stat activation levels

## Stage 0 — Unknown stats

```text
baseStats empty/unknown
progression tracks XP
combat/stat math deferred
```

## Stage 1 — Setup-confirmed base stats

```text
structured startup can set base stats before lock
values must pass invariant checks
```

## Stage 2 — Derived values active

```text
formula profile has enabled derived rules
derivedSnapshot recalculates from base stats/modifiers
```

## Stage 3 — Level-up stat changes

```text
formula profile supports stat progression
level-up emits stat changes through owner rules
```

Stage 3 is allowed only if formulas and tests are fully defined. Otherwise it must remain WARN/deferred.

---

# 14. CanonValidator stat/modifier rules

Accept setup stat initialization only when:

```text
structured startup source before lock
stat profile active or setup schema explicitly supports stats
stat value numeric and within allowed range
operation key present
```

Reject stat change when any are true:

```text
vague stronger/faster/smarter phrasing
training-only claim
ability use claim
dream/quote/rumor/prophecy
command/context/card/audit/debug source
manual command
impossible negative/nonnumeric value
formula profile missing for formula-derived change
```

Accept modifier only when:

```text
active condition/injury/status exists or is accepted in same batch
modifier mapping rule exists
source is safe
operation key present
```

Reject modifier when:

```text
condition is only rumored/quoted/dreamed
source is command/context/card/audit/debug
equipment/race/ability modifier tries to activate before its owner chunk
```

---

# 15. DerivedValueCalculator behavior

DerivedValueCalculator should:

```text
read base stats
read active modifiers
read formula rules
calculate derived values
write derivedSnapshot only through owner reducer/checker path
mark stale if inputs change
never mutate base stats
never mutate active conditions
never award resources as healing
```

If formula missing:

```text
derived value remains unknown/deferred
ReleaseCheck WARN if feature claimed active
```

---

# 16. InvariantChecker behavior

InvariantChecker should run after reducer batches and through diagnostic commands.

It should check:

```text
required stat structures exist
base stat values valid
resource current/max valid
modifiers have source/status/duration
condition modifiers link to active conditions
expired modifiers inactive
derived values match inputs if active
level/formula profile consistency
no duplicate active modifiers without stacking policy
no command/card/context source as stat truth
cleanup protected paths intact
```

Allowed checker actions:

```text
report warning
clamp structural resource overflow
quarantine malformed modifier/stat record
mark derivedSnapshot stale
block ReleaseCheck
```

---

# 17. ProgressionReducer stat-safe hooks

ProgressionReducer may interact with stats only through explicit stat rules.

Allowed:

```text
on level-up, check stat formula profile
if stat formula active, emit stat change records according to formula
if inactive, add level history only and stat progression pending/warn
```

Forbidden:

```text
level-up automatically gives random stat points
level-up heals to full by default
level-up changes HP max without resource formula
level-up changes world scaling
```

---

# 18. Body/resource integration

Rules:

```text
maxHP/maxMP/maxEP may derive from stats/formulas
currentHP/currentMP/currentEP does not auto-fill to new max unless policy active
structural clamp from above max is not healing
if max decreases below current, current clamps down structurally with audit
if max increases, current remains same unless heal/recovery/fill policy is accepted
```

This prevents false healing from formula recalculation.

---

# 19. Equipment/ability/race modifier boundaries

Equipment modifiers:

```text
iron sword equipped does not add attack bonus yet
armor does not change AC unless equipment formula spec is active
```

Ability modifiers:

```text
ability cost/cooldown labels remain descriptive
ability use does not change stats unless a future ability effect spec validates it
```

Race/form modifiers:

```text
race/form consequences are mostly PC13
wolf form may produce constraint labels if validated, but not full stat formula unless rule active
```

---

# 20. Operation keys

Required keys:

```text
stat.setup.<statKey>.<startupKey>
stat.change.<statKey>.<deltaOrValue>.<sourceHash>
stat.modifier.add.<modifierKey>.<sourceHash>
stat.modifier.expire.<modifierKey>.<sourceHash>
derived.recalc.<formulaProfileId>.<inputsHash>
resource.max.recalc.<resourceKey>.<inputsHash>
resource.clamp.<resourceKey>.<sourceHash>
invariant.check.<turnBucket>.<schemaVersion>
invariant.violation.<family>.<pathHash>.<actualHash>
repair.structural.<pathHash>.<sourceHash>
quarantine.stat.<pathHash>.<sourceHash>
```

Stat change keys are durable. Derived recalculation keys may be cache-like. Repair/quarantine keys should prevent repeated spam.

---

# 21. Dirty targets

Stat/resource updates should mark:

```text
player_sheet
body_status
progression
context
```

Mapping:

```text
base stat change -> player_sheet, progression, context
modifier change -> player_sheet, body_status if condition-related, context
derived recalculation -> player_sheet, body_status if resource-related, context
resource clamp -> body_status, player_sheet, context
invariant warning -> stateaudit/releasecheck only unless gameplay-relevant
```

---

# 22. Command and context updates

`/sheet` should show:

```text
level/XP summary
base stats if known
formula profile
derived values if active
stat automation warnings/deferred notes
world scaling boundary summary
```

`/status` should show:

```text
resources current/max/known
active conditions
condition modifiers if active
injuries
death boundary
resource formula warnings
```

Optional `/stats` should show:

```text
base stats
active modifiers
derived values
formula profile
last invariant result
```

ContextBuilder should include only relevant stat/body facts, not raw stat JSON or unknown stats as fake values.

---

# 23. Cleanup protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old low-risk invariant logs
old duplicate stale-derived warnings
old repair spam after summary
old inactive modifier history after archive policy
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
base stats
active modifiers
active condition sources
derived formula rules
formula profile
XP/level records
active body resources
unresolved invariant violations
quarantine records
repair action records needed for audit
```

Cleanup must not heal, level, assign stats, or change formulas.

---

# 24. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC11-specific gates:

```text
PC11-G01 StatBlock schema exists or equivalent stat structure exists
PC11-G02 BaseStatRecord schema exists or equivalent base stat record exists if stats active
PC11-G03 StatModifierRecord schema exists or equivalent modifier structure exists
PC11-G04 DerivedValueRecord schema exists or equivalent derived snapshot structure exists
PC11-G05 ResourceFormulaRecord schema exists or equivalent resource formula structure exists
PC11-G06 ConditionModifierRecord schema exists or equivalent condition modifier bridge exists
PC11-G07 InvariantViolationRecord and RepairActionRecord exist or equivalent audit structures exist
PC11-G08 CanonValidator rejects vague/training/command/card/context stat gains
PC11-G09 DerivedValueCalculator does not mutate base stats
PC11-G10 ProgressionReducer does not auto-assign stats without formula authority
PC11-G11 resource max/current recalculation does not heal by default
PC11-G12 condition modifiers link to active conditions and deactivate on resolution
PC11-G13 InvariantChecker detects impossible stat/resource/modifier states
PC11-G14 cleanup preserves base stats, active modifiers, active conditions, formula profiles, and unresolved violations
PC11-G15 command/context/card stat text cannot become canon
PC11-G16 equipment/ability/race/form stat modifiers remain deferred unless owner rules active
PC11-G17 world scaling remains no-rubber-band
PC11-G18 optional /stats or /sheet+/status displays stat state honestly
PC11-G19 durable stat/modifier/resource/repair keys exist
PC11-G20 player-breaker stat/invariant tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if vague stat gains apply, derived values overwrite base stats, cleanup heals/removes active modifiers, or resource recalculation heals the player silently.

---

```text
PC11-001 Unknown stats not defaulted silently
PC11-002 Structured setup stats accepted with operation keys

PC11-004 Training no stat gain by default
PC11-005 Command stat text non-canon
PC11-006 Story Card stat prose non-canon
PC11-007 Context stat prose non-canon
PC11-008 Level-up without stat formula does not assign stats
PC11-009 Level-up with stat formula applies only formula-defined changes
PC11-010 Derived value recalculates from inputs
PC11-011 Derived value does not overwrite base stat
PC11-012 Resource max increase does not heal
PC11-013 Resource max decrease clamps structurally
PC11-014 Resource current cannot exceed max
PC11-015 Negative resource rejected/quarantined or structurally clamped
PC11-016 Condition modifier active only with active condition
PC11-017 Resolved condition deactivates modifier
PC11-018 Cleanup does not heal condition modifier
PC11-019 Duplicate modifier skipped without stacking policy
PC11-020 Equipment modifier deferred
PC11-021 Ability modifier deferred
PC11-022 Race/form modifier deferred unless rule active
PC11-023 Invalid stat record quarantined
PC11-024 Formula profile cannot silently change stats
PC11-025 World scaling remains disabled
PC11-026 Stats display honest
PC11-027 Integrity command actionable and non-mutating
```

Minimum expectation:

```text
27 PC11 tests registered
0 critical PC11 failures
```

Regression tiers:

```text
T1 State/Runtime/Command Foundation Tests
T2 Parser/Proposal/Validator Safety Tests
T4 Reducer Commit Tests
T6 Player-Breaker Adversarial Tests
T7 Long-Run Stability Tests
T8 Deferred System Disabled Tests
```

```text

/integrity
/playerbreakercheck
/regressioncheck
/sheet
/status

```

14. Save accepted build:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.11_STATS_INVARIANTS_ACCEPTED.txt
```

---

# 27. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

```text
stat structures exist and are source-aware
base stats and derived values are separated
modifiers have source/status/duration/keys
condition modifiers link to active conditions
derived values recalculate from formula inputs only
resource max/current recalculation does not heal by default
level-up does not assign stats without formula authority
vague/training/command/card/context stat gains are rejected
InvariantChecker detects impossible values and records actions
cleanup preserves active stat/body/formula truth
stats display honestly
PC11 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC11 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

```text
stats remain unknown because no profile is active
stat formulas are partially deferred
combat derived values are deferred
race/form/equipment modifiers are deferred
derived snapshots are display-only until combat formulas activate
```

## FAIL

```text
unknown stats default silently
vague narration increases stats
training increases stats by default
command/context/card text mutates stats
level-up assigns stats without formula authority
resource max increase heals current HP/MP/EP by default
derived values overwrite base stats
condition cleanup removes active penalties
modifiers duplicate without stacking policy
world enemies scale from player stats
InvariantChecker repairs gameplay truth by awarding/healing/deleting active state
```

---

# 28. Handoff to Production Chunk 12

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 12 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 12 — Combat Event Model and Damage/Healing Dispute Resolver
```

Production Chunk 12 may now safely use:

```text
known resources
active conditions
injuries
stat labels/modifiers
derived values if active
duplicate-safe operation keys
invariant checks
```

Production Chunk 11 must leave stat/body math reliable enough that PC12 can ask:

```text
What HP/MP/EP values are known?
Which modifiers are active?
Are derived combat values known, stale, or deferred?
Can damage/healing safely change resources?
Will duplicate combat output apply twice?
Will cleanup accidentally heal or remove penalties?
```

---

# 29. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 11 is where numbers stop being vibes.

Final rule:

```text
Stats are source-bound.
Derived values are calculated.
Modifiers are owned.
Resources are clamped safely.
Conditions are not cleanup noise.
Invariants catch corruption before it spreads.
No unsafe text source, formula drift, or convenience repair can rewrite the character.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely build combat event and damage/healing resolution in Production Chunk 12.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 12 — Combat Event Model and Damage/Healing Dispute Resolver

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 12 — Combat Event Model and Damage/Healing Dispute Resolver  
**Production sequence:** 12 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.11 stats/invariants accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Add a conservative combat event model, attack/result separation, damage/healing dispute resolution, duplicate-safe combat operation keys, body/resource integration, combat display support, and combat regression gates without activating full advanced combat automation.  
**Status:** Core systems production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 11 — Stats, Derived Values, Conditions, and Invariant Checker.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 12 exists because combat is where AI Dungeon scripts can break the fastest.

Old and rough builds often risked:

```text
player input attack automatically damaging enemies
model narration applying damage twice
miss/block/graze text still applying full damage
healing from item names without validated use
healing from /status or context text
armor/equipment bonuses applying without formulas
ability names becoming damage rules
NPC death from rumor/threat/metaphor
player death from dramatic wording
combat victory granting XP automatically
duplicate combat output repeating damage, healing, loot, XP, and death
```

This chunk creates combat as a structured event system. It does **not** build the full advanced combat engine. It does **not** attempt full tactical AI, encounter balancing, party combat, warfare, absorption, or creature-specific advanced logic. It creates the safe baseline needed before those systems can exist.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
An attack attempt is not a hit.
A hit is not damage until validated.
Damage is not death until the death boundary validates it.
Healing is not recovery unless a healing/recovery event validates it.
A miss is not damage.
A block is not full damage.
A graze is not full damage unless validated.
Input intent cannot force outcome.
Output contradiction beats input prediction.
Duplicate output cannot repeat damage or healing.
Combat victory is not XP unless exact XP is separately validated.
```

Combat truth must be proposal-based, outcome-aware, duplicate-safe, and integrated with Body/Stats without bypassing them.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 11 made stats, resources, conditions, derived values, and invariants safer.

Combat comes now because it uses:

```text
body resources
injuries
conditions
stat/derived labels
inventory/equipment records
ability status/evidence
actor presence
place/scene context
XP/progression boundaries
operation ledgers
invariant checks
```

If combat is built before these layers, it becomes a pile of special cases. This chunk makes combat an event model that can later be expanded into advanced combat, creature ecology, party/summon handling, and warfare.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 12 may change:

```text
Combat schema helpers
CombatEventReducer / CombatReducerLite shell
CombatDisputeResolver
DamageResolver shell
HealingResolver shell
CanonValidator combat/damage/healing rules
ProposalBuilder combat event types
EventScannerLite combat hints
CommandRenderers /status, optional /combat
ContextBuilder combat/body packet
OperationLedger combat/damage/healing/death keys
DirtyQueue combat/body/social targets
IntegrityChecker combat checks
ReleaseCheck combat gates
Regression/player-breaker combat tests
```

Production Chunk 12 may add:

```text
CombatEventRecord schema
CombatantRecord / CombatantSnapshot schema
AttackAttemptRecord schema
DefenseResultRecord schema
DamageRecord schema
HealingRecord schema
CombatDisputeRecord schema
CombatOutcomeRecord schema
CombatSceneState schema
combat result status model
damage/healing split-owner contract with BodyReducer
combat audit summaries
```

Production Chunk 12 may not add:

```text
full advanced combat automation
full enemy stat generation
full initiative/turn-order engine unless shell-only
full equipment stat bonus engine
full spell damage formula engine
full creature ecology combat rules
party/summon control
warfare/group combat
absorption/evolution on kill
automatic combat XP
manual /damage /heal /kill commands
```

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
CombatState helpers
CombatReducerLite / CombatEventReducer
CombatDisputeResolver
DamageResolver shell
HealingResolver shell
BodyReducer integration boundary
ActorReducer reference boundary
Inventory/equipment read-only references
Ability read-only references
Stats/derived read-only references
CanonValidator combat rules
ProposalBuilder combat proposals
EventScannerLite combat hints
CommandRenderers: /status, optional /combat
ContextBuilderLite combat/body packet
OperationLedger combat keys
DirtyQueue combat/body dirty targets
AuditLogger combat lines
InvariantChecker combat checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC12 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
ProgressionReducer except no automatic combat XP
InventoryReducer except item use/hit references remain separate owner events
AbilityReducer except ability use evidence remains separate owner event
ActorReducer except actor status/death remains conservative and owner-routed
PlaceReducer except scene danger labels only
World threat/creature ecology systems
Advanced combat systems
Manual mutation commands
CardSync live writes
```

---

# 5. Combat state model

Combat should have a bounded state area.

Recommended shape:

```text
combat: {
  active: false,
  sceneState: null,
  events: [],
  recentAttempts: [],
  recentOutcomes: [],
  disputes: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

If no dedicated combat root exists, records may live in audits initially, but PC12 should prepare a stable `combat` bucket or equivalent. Combat state is not a full turn engine yet. It is an event ledger and dispute model.

---

```text
combat scene state is context/support, not automatic damage
```

---

# 7. Combat CanonValidator expansion

CanonValidator must enforce combat safety.

## 7.1 Accept attack attempt when

```text
safe input/output includes attack/swing/shoot/cast/use-hostile intent
target is bound enough
source is not command/context/card/audit/debug
```

Result:

```text
attack_attempt only
no damage yet unless output also confirms hit/damage
```

## 7.2 Accept miss/block/dodge when

```text
safe output directly says miss/dodge/parry/block/deflect/no effect
attacker/defender target can be bound
```

Result:

```text
combat outcome records miss/block
damage blocked/rejected unless separate accepted damage clause exists
```

## 7.3 Accept hit when

```text
safe output directly confirms hit/strike/connects/lands
not metaphor/quote/dream
not command/context/card source
```

Result:

```text
hit confirmed
no numeric damage unless damage clause validates
injury/severity may be proposed if direct physical consequence exists
```

## 7.4 Accept damage when

```text
safe output directly confirms wound, HP loss, injury, burn, bleeding, etc.
target is bound
not denied/blocked/missed/no effect
operation key not duplicate
```

## 7.5 Accept healing when

```text
safe output directly confirms HP restored, wound closes, bleeding stops, poison fades, etc.
target is bound
not failed/no effect/attempt only
operation key not duplicate
```

---

# 8. Combat rejection rules

Reject or defer when any are true:

```text
input-only attack tries to deal damage
miss/dodge/parry with no damage clause
blocked attack with no damage clause
dream/vision combat
quote says someone is hit
threat/intimidation only
metaphor only
training description not actual combat
command/context/card/audit/debug source
ambiguous target
ambiguous amount when numeric damage required
duplicate output
manual damage/heal command
```

Examples:

```text
I stab the goblin. -> attempt only
You miss the goblin. -> miss, no damage
The goblin blocks your sword. -> block, no damage unless separate injury says otherwise
Mira says, "The goblin is dead." -> no confirmed death
[AIDRPG] Status: HP -5 -> no damage
```

---

# 9. Damage/healing owner contract

Combat should not bypass BodyReducer.

Preferred owner contract:

```text
CombatReducer records combat event/outcome.
DamageResolver creates validated body.damage_candidate or actor.damage_candidate.
BodyReducer owns player HP, injuries, conditions, and death boundary.
ActorReducer owns actor presence/status memory; NPC body depth remains conservative unless NPC body system exists.
ProgressionReducer owns XP and must not auto-award combat XP.
InventoryReducer owns item consumption, not CombatReducer.
AbilityReducer owns ability evidence, not CombatReducer.
```

If a target owner system is not ready:

```text
record combat outcome and defer effect safely
```

Do not apply damage/healing by writing random fields.

---

# 10. DamageResolver behavior

DamageResolver should:

```text
read validated combat outcome
read target type
read amount/severity/body part if known
create DamageRecord
route player body effects to BodyReducer
route NPC damage conservatively to actor/status pending unless NPC body owner exists
apply no effect if miss/block/no-effect wins dispute
add operation key
mark dirty targets
```

DamageResolver should not:

```text
invent numeric damage from dramatic prose
kill target from damage unless death boundary validates
apply armor/equipment math unless rules active
apply ability damage formulas unless rules active
award XP/loot
```

---

# 11. HealingResolver behavior

HealingResolver should:

```text
read validated healing/recovery outcome
read target type
read resource/condition/injury target if known
create HealingRecord
route player body effects to BodyReducer
route NPC healing conservatively if owner exists
skip if failed/no effect/attempt only
add operation key
mark dirty targets
```

HealingResolver should not:

```text
heal from item name alone
consume item automatically unless InventoryReducer receives validated consume/use event
revive dead target unless revival policy active
clear all conditions broadly
heal above max
```

---

# 12. Dispute resolver behavior

CombatDisputeResolver should run before reducers commit combat consequences.

Priority rules:

```text
1. Command/context/card/debug source blocks effect.
2. Duplicate output blocks repeated effect.
3. Explicit miss/dodge/parry/no effect blocks damage.
4. Explicit block/armor blocks or defers damage unless damage clause says partial/graze.
5. Explicit hit permits hit outcome but not automatic numeric damage.
6. Explicit wound/injury/HP loss permits damage candidate.
7. Explicit healing success permits healing candidate.
8. Explicit healing failure blocks healing.
9. Death requires death-boundary validation.
10. Ambiguous target blocks damage/healing until resolved.
```

This makes combat robust against AI Dungeon contradictions.

---

# 13. Input/output dispute examples

## 13.1 Input claims damage, output says miss

Input:

```text
I slash the goblin across the chest.
```

Output:

```text
The goblin ducks under your blade.
```

Expected:

```text
attack attempt recorded
miss/dodge outcome recorded
no damage
```

## 13.2 Input attempts healing, output says no effect

Input:

```text
I drink the potion to heal my wounds.
```

Output:

```text
The potion is stale and has no effect.
```

Expected:

```text
item use/consume may be separately validated by InventoryReducer
no healing
failed healing outcome recorded
```

## 13.3 Output confirms wound

Output:

```text
The goblin's spear cuts your side, and blood spills down your armor.
```

Expected:

```text
damage/injury candidate accepted
BodyReducer records injury/bleeding if validated
```

---

Add or verify event types:

```text
combat.attack_attempt
combat.defense_result_candidate
combat.hit_candidate
combat.miss_candidate
combat.block_candidate
combat.graze_candidate
combat.damage_candidate
combat.healing_candidate
combat.healing_failed_candidate
combat.retreat_candidate
combat.capture_candidate
combat.victory_candidate
combat.defeat_candidate
combat.death_candidate
combat.dispute_candidate
body.damage_candidate
body.healing_candidate
body.injury_from_combat_candidate
body.condition_from_combat_candidate
```

Current chunk may commit safe:

```text
attack attempt
miss/block/hit/graze outcome
damage/healing records
player body damage/healing routed to BodyReducer
conservative actor/NPC status records
combat dispute records
combat scene active/resolved labels
```

---

# 15. EventScannerLite combat hints

EventScannerLite may detect combat hints, but detection is not canon.

Hint families:

```text
attack verbs: attack, slash, stab, shoot, strike, punch, kick, bite
hit verbs: hits, lands, connects, cuts, pierces, smashes
miss/block verbs: misses, dodges, blocks, parries, deflects, no effect
injury verbs: bleeds, breaks, burns, wounds, fractures, poisons
healing verbs: heals, restores, closes, stops bleeding, cures, stabilizes
combat outcome verbs: flees, surrenders, captures, defeats, dies
```

Must flag:

```text
inputIntentOnly
missed
blocked
noEffect
partialOrGraze
healingFailed
dreamOrVision
quotedSpeech
metaphor
threatOnly
trainingOnly
commandSource
contextSource
cardSource
auditSource
duplicateRisk
ambiguousTarget
ambiguousAmount
deathRisk
```

Parser cannot mutate combat/body state.

---

# 16. CombatReducerLite detailed behavior

CombatReducerLite owns:

```text
combat.active
combat.sceneState
combat.events
combat.recentAttempts
combat.recentOutcomes
combat.disputes
combat.warnings
```

## 16.1 Attack attempt commit

Accepted attack attempt should:

```text
create AttackAttemptRecord
create CombatEventRecord eventType attack_attempt
status intent_only or pending
not apply damage
mark combat/context dirty
audit compactly
```

## 16.2 Defense/miss/block commit

Accepted result should:

```text
update related attempt if found
create DefenseResultRecord
create CombatOutcomeRecord
block damage unless separate accepted damage exists
mark combat/context dirty
```

## 16.3 Hit commit

Accepted hit should:

```text
update related attempt if found
create hit outcome
not apply numeric damage unless damage candidate accepted
```

## 16.4 Damage/healing commit

Accepted damage/healing should:

```text
create DamageRecord or HealingRecord
route body effects through owner reducer contract
mark combat/body/context dirty
add operation key after successful owner-routing or safe defer
```

## 16.5 Dispute commit

Accepted dispute should:

```text
create CombatDisputeRecord
store resolvedResult
block/defer unsafe effects
mark combat/context dirty
```

---

# 17. CombatReducer defensive guards

CombatReducer must reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
input-only damage
miss/block/no-effect damage
healing failed but healing applied
duplicate damage/healing/death
ambiguous target
death without death-boundary validation
manual damage/heal/kill command
wrong owner reducer
```

CombatReducer must not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 18. Operation keys

Required combat operation keys:

```text
combat.attempt.<attackerKey>.<defenderKey>.<method>.<inputHash>
combat.result.<attackerKey>.<defenderKey>.<resultType>.<clauseHash>
combat.hit.<attackerKey>.<defenderKey>.<clauseHash>
combat.damage.<targetKey>.<damageKey>.<clauseHash>
combat.healing.<targetKey>.<healingKey>.<clauseHash>
combat.dispute.<disputeType>.<clauseHash>
combat.retreat.<combatantKey>.<clauseHash>
combat.capture.<targetKey>.<clauseHash>
combat.victory.<winnerKey>.<loserKey>.<clauseHash>
combat.death.pending.<targetKey>.<clauseHash>
```

Body/owner linked keys:

```text
body.damage.<targetKey>.<damageKey>.<sourceCombatKey>
body.healing.<targetKey>.<healingKey>.<sourceCombatKey>
body.injury.combat.<injuryKey>.<sourceCombatKey>
body.deathboundary.<newStatus>.<sourceCombatKey>
```

Rules:

```text
damage/healing/death keys are durable
attempt keys can be recent unless needed for dispute binding
same output replay cannot repeat damage/healing/death
```

---

# 19. Dirty targets

Combat updates should mark:

```text
combat
body_status
social_memory
current_location
context
progression only if exact XP event separately accepted
inventory_equipment only if item use/consumption separately accepted
```

Mapping:

```text
attack attempt -> combat, context
hit/miss/block/graze -> combat, context
player damage/healing -> combat, body_status, context
actor damage/status -> combat, social_memory, context
retreat/capture/victory -> combat, social_memory, current_location, context
```

Dirty targets do not write cards directly.

---

# 20. `/status` and optional `/combat` updates

## 20.1 `/status`

Should show combat-relevant body state:

```text
current HP/MP/EP if known
active injuries
active combat conditions
death boundary
recent combat damage/healing if relevant
```

It must not show combat attempt as confirmed damage.

## 20.2 Optional `/combat`

If command budget permits, add:

```text
/combat
```

Should show:

```text
combat active/resolved status
recent attempts
recent confirmed outcomes
damage/healing records
blocked/deferred effects
disputes
warnings
```

If not, include combat summaries inside:

```text
/status
/debuglast
/stateaudit
```

All command output starts with `[AIDRPG]` and remains non-canon.

---

# 21. ContextBuilder combat packet

ContextBuilder should include concise combat facts.

Include:

```text
active combat/threat status
confirmed recent hit/miss/block only if relevant
active injuries/conditions through body packet
important enemy/actor status if known
```

Do not include:

```text
input attack attempts as confirmed hits
blocked/rejected damage as applied damage
full combat event ledger
formula debug text
exact enemy stats if unknown
pending death as confirmed death
```

Example:

```text
Combat: Your last sword swing was blocked; no damage was confirmed. You have a bleeding cut on your side from the goblin's spear.
```

---

# 22. IntegrityChecker combat checks

Add checks:

```text
damage/healing records have operation keys
applied damage has owner-routed body/actor effect or safe defer note
miss/block/no-effect outcomes do not have applied damage unless separate damage record exists
healing failure does not have applied healing
death pending/confirmed follows death boundary rules
duplicate damage/healing keys not repeated
combat events do not originate from command/card/context source
combat victory does not auto-create XP/loot
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad combat records. It may not apply damage, healing, loot, XP, or death.

---

# 23. Cleanup combat protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old low-value attack attempts
old duplicate combat warning spam
old resolved dispute summaries
old non-applied rejected outcomes
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
applied damage/healing records needed for audit
active injuries/conditions
death-boundary records
durable combat/body operation keys
important combat outcome history
unresolved disputes
```

Cleanup must not heal, damage, kill, revive, award XP, or create loot.

---

# 24. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC12-specific gates:

```text
PC12-G01 CombatEventRecord schema exists or equivalent combat event structure exists
PC12-G02 AttackAttemptRecord schema exists or equivalent attempt structure exists
PC12-G03 DamageRecord schema exists or equivalent damage structure exists
PC12-G04 HealingRecord schema exists or equivalent healing structure exists
PC12-G05 CombatDisputeRecord schema exists or equivalent dispute structure exists
PC12-G06 CanonValidator separates attempt/hit/miss/block/graze/damage/healing/death
PC12-G07 input-only attacks do not apply damage
PC12-G08 miss/block/no-effect prevents damage unless separate accepted damage exists
PC12-G09 healing failure prevents healing
PC12-G10 CombatReducer rejects unsafe routed combat events
PC12-G11 damage/healing routes through BodyReducer/owner contract
PC12-G12 death remains protected by death-boundary validation
PC12-G13 duplicate damage/healing/death keys exist and are durable
PC12-G14 command/context/card combat text cannot become canon
PC12-G15 combat victory does not auto-award XP/loot
PC12-G16 equipment/ability/stat advanced combat formulas remain deferred unless owner rules active
PC12-G17 /status or /combat displays combat state honestly
PC12-G18 cleanup preserves active combat/body truth and durable keys
PC12-G19 advanced combat/party/warfare/absorption systems remain disabled
PC12-G20 player-breaker combat tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if input attacks apply damage directly, repeated output duplicates damage/healing, command/context/card combat text mutates state, or combat victory awards XP/loot automatically.

# 31. Beginner implementation order

When implementing this chunk in code, follow this order.

## Step 1 — Copy accepted stats/invariant build

Start from:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.11_STATS_INVARIANTS_ACCEPTED.txt
```

Create working file:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.12_COMBAT_EVENTS_WORKING.txt
```

## Step 2 — Add/verify combat helper functions

Create helpers for:

```text
ensureCombatShape
makeCombatSceneState
makeCombatantSnapshot
makeCombatEventRecord
makeAttackAttemptRecord
makeDefenseResultRecord
makeDamageRecord
makeHealingRecord
makeCombatDisputeRecord
makeCombatOutcomeRecord
isUnsafeCombatSource
isInputIntentOnly
isDamageBlockedByResult
```

## Step 3 — Add CombatDisputeResolver

Implement the priority rules before reducers apply consequences.

## Step 4 — Update combat CanonValidator rules

Add strict accept/reject/pending logic for:

```text
attack attempt
hit/miss/block/graze
damage
healing
healing failure
victory/defeat
capture/retreat
death pending/confirmed
```

## Step 5 — Update ProposalBuilder combat support

Ensure proposals carry:

```text
attacker
defender
method
result type
damage/healing data
target body part if known
source/localClause/safety flags
operation key suggestion
```

## Step 6 — Update CombatReducerLite

Implement safe commits for:

```text
attack attempt
miss/block/hit/graze outcome
damage/healing record
dispute record
combat scene state
```

## Step 7 — Wire damage/healing to owner reducers

Route player body effects to BodyReducer. Defer unsafe NPC body effects unless owner exists.

## Step 8 — Update `/status` and optional `/combat`

Make combat state display honest.

## Step 9 — Update ContextBuilder combat packet

Keep it concise and state-accurate.

## Step 10 — Update IntegrityChecker combat checks

Add applied damage/healing, duplicate, dispute, and unsafe-source checks.

## Step 11 — Add PC12 ReleaseCheck gates

Add PC12-G01 through PC12-G20.

## Step 12 — Add PC12 tests

Add PC12-001 through PC12-028.

## Step 13 — Run baseline checks

Run:

```text
/foundationcheck
/releasecheck
/integrity
/playerbreakercheck
/regressioncheck
/status
/combat if available
```

## Step 14 — Save accepted combat-event build

Recommended output:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.12_COMBAT_EVENTS_ACCEPTED.txt
```

# 33. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 12 passes if:

```text
attack attempts and confirmed outcomes are separated
input-only attacks do not apply damage
miss/block/no-effect prevents damage unless separate damage validates
healing failure prevents healing
damage/healing routes through owner reducer contract
death remains protected by death-boundary rules
duplicate damage/healing/death keys work
combat victory does not auto-award XP/loot
/status or /combat displays honestly
command/context/card combat text cannot become canon
cleanup preserves active combat/body truth
PC12 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC12 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
advanced combat formulas are deferred
equipment/ability combat math is deferred
NPC body depth is conservative
initiative/turn order is shell-only or absent
creature-specific combat logic is deferred
party/warfare/absorption systems are disabled
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
input attack directly damages target
miss/block still applies damage
healing failure still heals
damage/healing duplicates on replay
command/context/card text damages/heals/kills
combat victory grants XP or loot automatically
BodyReducer death boundary is bypassed
CombatReducer writes random body fields directly
manual damage/heal/kill commands mutate state
cleanup deletes active injury/combat keys
CombatReducer trusts unsafe routed data
```

---

# 34. Handoff to Production Chunk 13

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 13 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 13 — Body Form, Race Logic, Transformation Consequence, and Non-Humanoid Constraints
```

Production Chunk 13 may now use combat/body evidence to reason about form constraints, non-humanoid bodies, and transformation consequences, but should not use combat as proof of race/form powers without validation.

Production Chunk 12 must leave combat reliable enough that future chunks can ask:

```text
Was there only an attempt?
Was there a miss, block, graze, or hit?
Was damage actually validated?
Was healing actually validated?
Was death only pending or confirmed?
Was this effect already applied?
Which owner reducer owns the resulting body/actor/progression/inventory change?
```

---

# 35. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 12 is where combat stops being outcome hallucination.

Final rule:

```text
Combat is an event pipeline, not a shortcut.
Attempts, outcomes, damage, healing, death, loot, and XP are separate.
Every consequence must be validated, owner-routed, duplicate-safe, and honest in display.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen race/form/transformation consequences in Production Chunk 13.

================================================================================================
END 40_Production_Roadmap/12_AIDRPG_Production_Chunk_12_Combat_Event_Model_and_Damage_Healing_Dispute_Resolver.txt
================================================================================================

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 13 — Body Form, Race Logic, Transformation Consequence, and Non-Humanoid Constraints

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 13 — Body Form, Race Logic, Transformation Consequence, and Non-Humanoid Constraints  
**Production sequence:** 13 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.12 combat-event accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen race/species identity, current form, transformation events, body traits, form capabilities, non-humanoid constraints, form consequences, and form-aware display/context without identity drift, fake transformations, or unsafe power grants.  
**Status:** Core systems production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 12 — Combat Event Model and Damage/Healing Dispute Resolver.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 13 is where form finally starts to matter.

This is the chunk that lets the game support things like wolf form, winged form, scaled body, ghostly partial form, large monster body, tiny familiar body, water-breathing form, clawed hands, no hands, tail balance, horns, fur, shell, nonhuman speech limits, and nonhuman tool limits.

But it must do this without repeating the old failure loops:

```text
current form overwriting true identity
illusion becoming body truth
disguise becoming species
rumor becoming lineage
dream transformation becoming real
race label granting full powers
non-humanoid body still using every human tool normally
cleanup deleting active form consequences
combat damage proving a race power without validation
card/context text changing body truth
```

This chunk should feel fun in gameplay because forms and bodies create real consequences.

It should also be strict because identity/body corruption can break every future system.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
True race/species is not current form.
Current form is not disguise.
Disguise is not illusion.
Illusion is not body truth.
Temporary transformation is not permanent identity.
Rumor is not lineage.
Dream form is not real form.
Cosmetic label is not physical capability.
Physical form must create physical constraints.
Non-humanoid bodies must not behave like default humans.
Race/lineage labels do not grant powers without validated rules.
```

Form should be playable, visible, and consequential.

But every form truth must have source, duration, owner, rollback behavior, and tests.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 12 created safe combat event handling.

Body form comes next because future systems depend on it:

```text
equipment compatibility
inventory carrying/use constraints
combat reach, bite, claws, wings, size, posture
ability use and spellcasting limitations
social reaction and witness memory
creature ecology
race/species world rules
transformation powers
absorption/evolution later
world bible race/creature references later
encounter threat logic
party/summon forms later
```

If form/race logic is not safe now, later world and combat systems will keep patching around identity drift.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 13 may change:

```text
Player identity/body form helpers
Body form schema helpers
BodyReducer form/trait/constraint behavior
FormReducer or FormLogic helper if separated
RaceSpecies registry shell
Transformation event validator
CanonValidator form/race/transformation rules
ProposalBuilder form event types
EventScannerLite form/race hints
CommandRenderers /sheet, /status, optional /form
ContextBuilder form/body packet
OperationLedger form/transformation keys
DirtyQueue form/body/social/inventory targets
IntegrityChecker form/race/body checks
ReleaseCheck form/race gates
Regression/player-breaker form tests
```

Production Chunk 13 may add:

```text
RaceSpeciesRecord schema
FormRecord schema
TransformationEventRecord schema
BodyTraitRecord schema
BodyConstraintRecord schema
FormCapabilityRecord schema
FormConsequenceRecord schema
FormDurationRecord schema
FormAccessProfile schema
FormSourceRecord schema
SocialVisibilityRecord shell
EquipmentCompatibility shell
form rollback/expiry model
form-aware command/context summaries
```

Production Chunk 13 may not add:

```text
full world bible import
full race power automation
full lineage/divine power system
absorption/evolution system
full equipment stat compatibility engine
full reputation/social reaction automation
full creature ecology rules
manual /setrace /setform /transform commands
live CardSync writes
generated form/race cards
old card prose form import
```

World Bible import/reference layer is Production Chunk 15.

Creature ecology is Production Chunk 17.

Absorption/evolution is Production Chunk 24.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
PlayerState helpers
BodyState helpers
FormLogic helper
BodyReducer form/trait/constraint path
CanonValidator form/race rules
ProposalBuilder form proposals
EventScannerLite form/race hints
Resolver form/race target binding
CommandRenderers: /sheet, /status, optional /form
ContextBuilderLite form/body packet
OperationLedger form keys
DirtyQueue form/body/social/inventory dirty targets
AuditLogger form lines
InvariantChecker form checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC13 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
InventoryReducer except form-related dirty/display warnings
Equipment stat formulas
Combat damage formulas
Ability power grants
Actor reputation automation
World Bible full import
Creature ecology
Absorption/evolution
Manual mutation commands
CardSync live writes
```

# 5. Identity/form authority model

Every race/form/body trait must have authority.

Authority levels:

```text
unknown
setup_confirmed
validated_story_transformation
validated_temporary_form
validated_disguise
validated_illusion
validated_body_trait
candidate_trait
rumor_only
dream_only
card_context_command_blocked
world_bible_reference_later
deferred_manual_override
invalid_quarantined
```

Rules:

```text
setup_confirmed can set true race/species before startup lock
validated_story_transformation can set current form but not necessarily true race/species
validated_disguise changes presentation, not body truth
validated_illusion changes appearance/perception, not physical constraints unless illusion rules later support it
candidate_trait displays as candidate only
world_bible_reference_later can guide rules later, not mutate player state now
```

---

Race/species records describe true body identity, not temporary form.

```text
  powerDefaultsDeferred,

  updatedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
```

Categories:

```text

humanoid
beast
draconic
undead
construct
elemental
spirit
celestial_deferred
infernal_deferred
fae_deferred
plant
ooze
aberration_deferred
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
true race/species must not be inferred from current form alone
unknown race/species must remain unknown
race/species does not grant powers unless rule owner exists
race/species body defaults can prepare constraints but must not override validated current form without policy
```



---

# 17. Race and lineage power boundary

Race/species/lineage can be fun, but must not grant powers by label alone.

Examples that must be controlled:

```text
angel lineage
demon blood
dragonborn ancestry
storm-touched blood
vampire curse
fae-marked child
construct body
undead nature
```

Rules:

```text
confirmed lineage may display as identity/body/lore fact
candidate lineage remains candidate
lineage powers are deferred unless ability/form/race rule validates them
race/species body constraints may apply if physical and validated
cosmic/divine/infernal powers wait for future mini-spec/world bible/divine chunks
```

This prevents:

```text
"You are part dragon" -> automatic breath weapon
"You have angel blood" -> automatic wings/healing
"You are undead" -> automatic immortality
```

---

# 18. Transformation fun rules

Forms should matter in ways that are fun and readable.

Allowed consequences in this chunk:

```text
wolf form: no human hands, altered speech, quadruped movement, scent-tracking label, bite/claw method label
winged form: visible wings, possible glide/flight deferred, armor/clothing warning
fish/amphibious form: water-breathing capability if validated, land movement warning if appropriate
large beast form: size access warning, armor/tool compatibility warning, intimidation visibility label
tiny familiar form: small tool/use warning, stealth/size label, carrying capacity warning
ghostly form: incorporeal/corporeal rules deferred unless validated, social alarm label
construct form: pain/bleeding logic deferred to creature/body chunks unless fully validated
undead form: death/bleeding/fatigue rules deferred unless race/body rule active
```

Not allowed automatically:

```text
free stat bonuses
free combat damage formulas
free spell powers
free social reputation shifts
free quest completion
free equipment transformation
free immortality
free flight mechanics without rules
```

---

# 19. Form CanonValidator expansion

CanonValidator must enforce form safety.

## 19.1 Accept physical transformation when

All are true:

```text
safe story output directly states physical body transformation
source is not command/context/card/audit/debug
not dream/vision-only
not quoted claim only
not disguise/illusion/cosmetic wording
target form is bound
physicality is clear enough
operation key not duplicate
```

Examples:

```text
Your bones crack and your body becomes a wolf.
Scales spread across your arms, hard and real beneath your fingers.
Your hands twist into claws, changing how you grip the sword.
```

## 19.2 Accept disguise/presentation when

```text
safe source states clothing/mask/makeup/behavior changes appearance
physical body does not change
```

Result:

```text
disguise/presentation active
no body constraints unless outfit itself affects movement/equipment later
```

## 19.3 Accept illusion when

```text
safe source states magical/perceptual appearance change
body remains physically same unless text directly says otherwise
```

Result:

```text
illusion active
social/perception label
no physical capabilities/constraints by default
```

## 19.4 Reject transformation when

Any are true:

```text
dream/vision only
rumor/prophecy only
quote says transformation happened
metaphor/simile only
pretend/roleplay only
command/context/card/audit/debug source
candidate/uncertain wording only
```

---

```text
form.physical_transformation_candidate

race.lineage_candidate

form.equipment_compatibility_candidate
```

```text
physical current form change
partial transformation
reversion/expiry
validated disguise
validated illusion
body trait/constraint/capability labels
form consequences
candidate race/lineage traits
social visibility shell
```

---

# 21. EventScannerLite form/race hints

EventScannerLite may detect form/race hints, but detection is not canon.

Hint families:

```text
transform/become/turn into/shift/morph
sprout/grow/change/crack/twist
fur/scales/claws/fangs/wings/tail/horns/gills
mask/disguise/appears as/looks like
illusion/glamour/seeming
lineage/blood/ancestry/race/species
revert/return to normal/change back
```

Must flag:

```text
physicalTransformation
disguiseOnly
illusionOnly
cosmeticOnly
candidateOnly
rumorOrClaim
quotedSpeech
dreamOrVision
metaphorOrSimile
pretendOnly
commandSource
contextSource
cardSource
auditSource
duplicateRisk
identityOverwriteRisk
powerGrantRisk
```

Parser cannot mutate race/form directly.

---

# 22. BodyReducer/FormLogic behavior

BodyReducer or FormLogic owns:

```text
player.currentForm
player.trueRaceSpecies references
player.lineage/candidate traits
body.bodyTraits
body.formConstraints
body.formCapabilities
body.formConsequences
body.formHistory
```

## 22.1 Physical transformation commit

Accepted transformation should:

```text
create TransformationEventRecord
create/update FormRecord
set currentForm to active physical form
add validated body traits
add derived constraints/capability labels
create duration record if known
mark player_sheet, body_status, context dirty
mark inventory_equipment/social_memory dirty if constraints/visibility changed
audit compactly
```

## 22.2 Partial transformation commit

Accepted partial change should:

```text
add body traits and constraints without replacing whole currentForm unless appropriate
preserve base form reference
record affected body parts
```

## 22.3 Reversion commit

Accepted reversion should:

```text
set active form inactive/reverted
restore previous/base form if known
end constraints/capabilities sourced from reverted form
preserve transformation history
mark dirty targets
```

## 22.4 Disguise/illusion commit

Accepted disguise/illusion should:

```text
create FormRecord type disguise or illusion
set presentation/illusion fields
not change physical currentForm
not add physical constraints unless explicitly physical
```

## 22.5 Race/species commit

True race/species change is protected.

Allowed only from:

```text
structured startup before lock
explicit validated permanent species transformation with strict policy
future migration/override spec
```

Default:

```text
store candidate/pending lineage/species traits
do not overwrite trueRaceSpecies
```

---

# 23. Form defensive guards

BodyReducer/FormLogic must reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
dream/vision-only
quote-only
rumor-only
metaphor/simile only
disguise trying to overwrite body truth
illusion trying to create physical capability
candidate trait trying to become confirmed
race/lineage label trying to grant powers
current form trying to overwrite true race/species
missing form key
wrong owner reducer
```

Reducer must not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 24. Non-humanoid constraint behavior

Non-humanoid constraints should affect display/context and future validation.

## 24.1 Hands and tool use

If form has no human hands:

```text
/sheet and /status show limited/no human hands
/inventory may warn equipped humanoid weapons/tools are awkward/deferred
context reminds model not to assume normal tool use
```

Do not delete items or unequip automatically unless future equipment policy supports it.

## 24.2 Speech

If form cannot speak normally:

```text
context should say speech is altered/limited
actor/social systems may later react if witnessed
commands remain available out-of-story
```

## 24.3 Movement

If quadruped, winged, aquatic, tiny, or huge:

```text
context should include movement constraints
place/travel validators may later use them
```

## 24.4 Combat method labels

Claws/bite/tail may become combat method labels.

```text
bite/claw can be attack method
no automatic damage formula
combat still validates attempts/hits/damage
```

---

# 25. Operation keys

Required form/race operation keys:

```text
form.transform.<fromFormKey>.<toFormKey>.<clauseHash>
form.partial.<traitKey>.<bodyPart>.<clauseHash>
form.revert.<formKey>.<clauseHash>
form.disguise.<presentationKey>.<clauseHash>
form.illusion.<illusionKey>.<clauseHash>
form.trait.add.<traitKey>.<sourceFormKey>.<clauseHash>
form.trait.remove.<traitKey>.<sourceFormKey>.<clauseHash>
form.constraint.add.<constraintKey>.<sourceFormKey>.<clauseHash>
form.capability.add.<capabilityKey>.<sourceFormKey>.<clauseHash>
form.consequence.<consequenceType>.<sourceFormKey>.<clauseHash>
race.true.set.<raceKey>.<startupOrStrictSourceKey>
race.lineage.candidate.<lineageKey>.<sourceHash>
race.trait.candidate.<traitKey>.<sourceHash>
```

Rules:

```text
physical transformation keys are durable
reversion keys are durable
trait/constraint keys prevent duplicate consequences
candidate race/lineage keys do not confirm truth
cleanup must preserve durable transformation history
```

---

# 26. Dirty targets

Form/race updates should mark:

```text
player_sheet
body_status
context
inventory_equipment
social_memory
current_location if size/movement affects scene access
combat if active form affects combat method labels
```

Mapping:

```text
physical transformation -> player_sheet, body_status, context, inventory_equipment, social_memory
trait/constraint/capability -> body_status, context, inventory_equipment if relevant
reversion -> player_sheet, body_status, context, inventory_equipment, social_memory
disguise/illusion -> player_sheet, context, social_memory if visible
candidate race/lineage -> player_sheet, context only if important
```

Dirty targets do not write cards directly.

---

# 27. `/sheet`, `/status`, and optional `/form` updates

## 27.1 `/sheet`

Must show:

```text
true race/species
lineage confirmed/candidate
current physical form
disguise/presentation
illusion
candidate traits
form duration/status
unknowns
```

Display rules:

```text
current form separate from true race/species
candidate lineage separate from confirmed lineage
disguise/illusion separate from physical form
unknown stays unknown
```

## 27.2 `/status`

Must show:

```text
active body traits
active form constraints
active capabilities/labels
injuries/conditions that interact with form
form-related warnings
```

## 27.3 Optional `/form`

If command budget permits, add:

```text
/form
```

Should show:

```text
current form
physicality
duration
traits
constraints
capabilities
consequences
reversion target
unsafe/deferred power notes
```

All command output starts with `[AIDRPG]` and remains non-canon.

---

# 28. ContextBuilder form/body packet

Context should help AI Dungeon narrate forms correctly.

Include:

```text
current physical form
important body traits
important constraints
speech/tool/movement limitations
obvious visible traits if present actors can witness them
candidate traits only if clearly labeled
```

Do not include:

```text
candidate lineage as confirmed truth
illusion as physical body
race powers as active without rules
full raw form history
hidden world bible race lore before PC15/knowledge gating
```

Example packet:

```text
Form: You are physically in wolf form. You have no human hands, move on four legs, and cannot speak normally. Bite/claw are possible combat methods, but damage still requires combat validation.
```

---

# 29. IntegrityChecker form/race checks

Add checks:

```text
true race/species not overwritten by currentForm
currentForm has valid FormRecord
physical form constraints link to active form/traits
disguise/illusion not marked physical unless validated
candidate traits not in confirmed trait list
expired forms do not leave active constraints
reverted forms preserve history
race/lineage powers not active without owner rules
command/card/context source not active form truth
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad records.

It may not transform the player, confirm lineage, grant powers, or erase active form consequences.

---

# 30. Cleanup form/race protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old rejected form claims
old low-risk candidate trait spam
old expired form warning logs after summary
old duplicate constraint audit spam
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
active current form
true race/species
active body traits
active constraints/capabilities
active form duration records
transformation history needed for reversion/audit
candidate lineage evidence still relevant
quarantine records
```

Cleanup must not revert transformation, heal body traits, confirm lineage, or remove active constraints.

---

# 31. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC13-specific gates:

```text
PC13-G01 RaceSpeciesRecord schema exists or equivalent race/species structure exists
PC13-G02 FormRecord schema exists or equivalent current form structure exists
PC13-G03 TransformationEventRecord schema exists or equivalent transformation event structure exists
PC13-G04 BodyTraitRecord and BodyConstraintRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC13-G05 FormCapabilityRecord and FormConsequenceRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC13-G06 true race/species, current physical form, disguise, illusion, and candidate traits are separated
PC13-G07 CanonValidator rejects dream/quote/rumor/metaphor/command/card/context transformations
PC13-G08 BodyReducer/FormLogic rejects unsafe routed form/race events
PC13-G09 physical transformations create traits/constraints/capability labels, not free powers
PC13-G10 disguise/illusion do not overwrite physical body truth
PC13-G11 race/lineage labels do not grant powers without owner rules
PC13-G12 non-humanoid constraints are displayed/contextualized
PC13-G13 reversion/expiry deactivates form-sourced constraints without deleting history
PC13-G14 operation keys exist for transformation/reversion/traits/constraints
PC13-G15 /sheet and /status or /form display form state honestly
PC13-G16 command/context/card form/race text cannot become canon
PC13-G17 cleanup preserves active form, traits, constraints, true race/species, and history
PC13-G18 equipment/ability/combat/reputation/world-bible power systems remain deferred unless owner rules active
PC13-G19 player-breaker form/race tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if current form overwrites true race/species, dream/quote/card/context transformation becomes real, or race/lineage labels grant powers automatically.

# 40. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 13 passes if:

```text
true race/species, current form, disguise, illusion, and candidate traits are separated
physical transformations create form records, traits, constraints, and consequences
disguise/illusion do not overwrite body truth
race/lineage labels do not grant powers automatically
non-humanoid constraints are visible and context-aware
reversion/expiry deactivates form-sourced constraints without deleting history
operation keys prevent duplicate transformations/traits/constraints
/sheet /status /form display honestly
command/context/card form text cannot become canon
cleanup preserves active form truth and history
PC13 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC13 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
race power mechanics are deferred
flight/wall-crawl/incorporeal mechanics are deferred
equipment compatibility is warning/context-only
social reaction is visibility/evidence-only
world bible race rules are deferred to PC15+
creature ecology body logic is deferred to PC17
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
current form overwrites true race/species
unknown race defaults to human without source
dream/quote/rumor/metaphor transforms player
card/context/command text changes form
race/lineage label grants powers automatically
disguise/illusion creates physical constraints incorrectly
non-humanoid body behaves as default human with no warning/context
reversion deletes history or leaves active constraints behind
duplicate transformation spams traits/constraints
cleanup reverts form or deletes active form truth
BodyReducer/FormLogic trusts unsafe routed data
```

---

# 41. Handoff to Production Chunk 14

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 14 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 14 — Threat Benchmark, Encounter Logic, and World Scaling Boundaries
```

Production Chunk 14 may use form/body constraints to compare threat and encounter logic, but it must not rubber-band enemies to the player.

Production Chunk 13 must leave form/race state reliable enough that PC14 can ask:

```text
What is the player's true race/species?
What is the player's current physical form?
What body traits are active?
What constraints affect movement, speech, tools, equipment, and combat method labels?
Which traits are only candidate or illusory?
Can this form reasonably fight, flee, carry, speak, or pass through a place?
```

---

# 42. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 13 is where transformation becomes playable instead of cosmetic.

Final rule:

```text
Forms should be fun because they change what the body can do.
Forms should be safe because they never overwrite identity, grant powers, or mutate reality from unsafe text.
A wolf form should feel like a wolf body.
An illusion should feel like an illusion.
A rumor should stay a rumor.
A transformation should have consequences, duration, and rollback.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen threat benchmarks, encounter logic, and world scaling boundaries in Production Chunk 14.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 14 — Threat Benchmark, Encounter Logic, and World Scaling Boundaries

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 14 — Threat Benchmark, Encounter Logic, and World Scaling Boundaries  
**Production sequence:** 14 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.13 form/race/constraints accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Add threat benchmark records, encounter state, region/creature danger labels, fairness boundaries, no-rubber-band scaling enforcement, encounter proposal safety, and world-scaling display rules without turning every monster mention into combat or scaling the world to the player.  
**Status:** Core systems production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 13 — Body Form, Race Logic, Transformation Consequence, and Non-Humanoid Constraints.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 14 exists because threat and encounter logic is where an RPG script either starts feeling like a living world or becomes fake difficulty math.

Old and rough builds risked:

```text
all enemies scaling to the player automatically
low-level areas becoming impossible because the player leveled up
high-threat regions being softened because the player is weak
monster references becoming immediate combat
rumors spawning enemies
Story Card creature lore becoming active encounter state
context reminders creating fights
current location danger being overwritten by random creature text
form/race traits being treated as instant power scaling
quest danger becoming completed combat
combat victory generating XP/loot automatically
duplicate encounter outputs creating duplicate enemies
```

This chunk locks the threat/encounter boundary. The goal is not a full creature ecology or encounter generator yet. The goal is to define how threat is measured, how encounter state begins and ends, how the world refuses rubber-band scaling, and how AI Dungeon gameplay receives useful danger context without uncontrolled automation.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Threat is not player-scaled by default.
A wolf remains a wolf unless world facts change it.
A dragon remains dangerous even if the player is low level.
A rat does not become legendary because the player is high level.
A creature mention is not an encounter.
A rumor is not an encounter.
A visible enemy is not combat until engagement validates it.
A dangerous region is not an automatic fight.
Encounter victory is not XP or loot unless separate owner events validate it.
Difficulty labels guide context; they do not mutate state by themselves.
```

Threat should be fair because it is honest, not because it secretly bends around the player.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 13 made body form and non-humanoid constraints meaningful. Threat benchmarks come next because they use:

```text
player level and XP boundaries from PC10
stats and resource safety from PC11
combat event model from PC12
form/body constraints from PC13
actor presence from PC08
place/current scene from PC09
quest objective/reward boundaries from PC07
inventory/equipment truth from PC05
ability status from PC06
```

If threat is not defined before world bible and creature ecology chunks, those later chunks will invent inconsistent danger rules. This chunk makes future world systems safer.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 14 may change:

```text
Threat schema helpers
Encounter schema helpers
ThreatBenchmark helper
EncounterState helper
WorldScalingBoundary enforcement
CanonValidator threat/encounter rules
ProposalBuilder threat/encounter event types
EventScannerLite encounter hints
CommandRenderers /location, optional /encounter or /threat
ContextBuilder threat/encounter packet
OperationLedger threat/encounter keys
DirtyQueue threat/combat/location/context targets
IntegrityChecker threat/encounter checks
ReleaseCheck threat/encounter gates
Regression/player-breaker threat tests
```

Production Chunk 14 may add:

```text
ThreatBenchmarkRecord schema
ThreatSourceRecord schema
EncounterRecord schema
EncounterParticipantRecord schema
EncounterStateRecord schema
EncounterTriggerRecord schema
EncounterResolutionRecord schema
RegionThreatRecord shell
CreatureThreatRecord shell
HazardThreatRecord shell
DangerContextRecord schema
FairnessBoundaryRecord schema
NoRubberBandPolicyRecord schema
EncounterAuditRecord schema
```

Production Chunk 14 may not add:

```text
full creature ecology database
full world bible import
full random encounter generator
full region/faction pressure simulation
full tactical combat balancing
full NPC stat generation
full loot table or XP table automation
automatic encounter scaling
manual /spawnencounter or /setthreat commands
live CardSync writes
generated encounter cards
old card prose encounter import
```

World Bible import/reference layer is Production Chunk 15. Creature ecology is Production Chunk 17. Advanced combat/warfare remains later.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
ThreatState helpers
EncounterState helpers

InvariantChecker threat/encounter checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC14 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
CombatReducer except encounter link references
ProgressionReducer except no automatic XP scaling
InventoryReducer except no automatic loot scaling
ActorReducer except encounter participant references
PlaceReducer except danger context display
WorldBible import
Creature ecology
Faction/region pressure systems
Manual mutation commands
CardSync live writes
```

---

# 5. Threat and encounter state model

Threat state should be structured but not overactive.

Recommended shape:

```text
threat: {
  benchmarks: {},
  sources: {},
  regionThreats: {},
  creatureThreats: {},
  hazardThreats: {},
  dangerContexts: {},
  fairnessBoundaries: {},
  noRubberBandPolicy: null,
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

Encounter state should be separate:

```text
encounters: {
  activeEncounterKey: null,
  records: {},
  participants: {},
  triggers: [],
  resolutions: [],
  recentEncounters: [],
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

Do not hide threat data inside combat logs only. Threat can exist without combat. Encounter can exist before combat. Combat can happen inside an encounter, but they are not the same thing.

---

# 6. ThreatBenchmarkRecord schema

Threat benchmark records classify danger without scaling it automatically.

Required shape:

```text
ThreatBenchmarkRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  threatFamily,
  threatTier,
  relativeToPlayerLabel,
  absoluteDangerLabel,
  sourceAuthority,
  sourceOperationKey,
  relatedCreatureKey,
  relatedRegionKey,
  relatedPlaceKey,
  relatedQuestKey,
  formulaProfileId,
  scalingPolicyKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Threat families:

```text
creature
actor
hazard
region
location
quest
faction_deferred
divine_deferred
environment
unknown
```

Threat tiers:

```text
trivial
low
moderate
serious
deadly
overwhelming
legendary
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
absoluteDangerLabel describes the world fact
relativeToPlayerLabel is display/context only
relative labels do not mutate creature/region/player stats
threat tier must not change just because player levels up unless source authority changes
```

```text
ThreatSourceRecord {

  notes
```
