```text
region_record_later
quest_warning
actor_warning
rumor
card_context_blocked
command_blocked
unknown
```

Authority levels:

```text
confirmed
probable
rumored
candidate
blocked
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
rumored threat is not confirmed threat
blocked card/context/command source cannot create active threat truth
world bible reference later can guide future records but does not import yet
```

# 8. NoRubberBandPolicyRecord schema

This record enforces the central fairness rule.

Required shape:

```text
NoRubberBandPolicyRecord {
  id,
  policyName,
  enabled,
  playerScalingAllowed,
  automaticEnemyScalingAllowed,
  automaticRegionScalingAllowed,
  automaticLootScalingAllowed,
  automaticXPScalingAllowed,
  authority,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Required values for MVP:

```text
playerScalingAllowed = false
automaticEnemyScalingAllowed = false
automaticRegionScalingAllowed = false
automaticLootScalingAllowed = false
automaticXPScalingAllowed = false
```

Rules:

```text
player level may influence relative danger display
player level may not rewrite enemy level
player level may not rewrite region danger
player level may not create loot/XP tables
encounter fairness means honest warning, not hidden scaling
```

---

# 9. FairnessBoundaryRecord schema

Fairness boundaries explain what the system should and should not do.

Required shape:

```text
FairnessBoundaryRecord {
  id,
  key,
  boundaryType,
  rule,
  active,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Boundary types:

```text
no_rubber_band_scaling
no_auto_encounter_from_mention
no_auto_xp_from_victory
no_auto_loot_from_victory
no_auto_combat_from_visible_enemy
respect_region_danger
respect_form_constraints
respect_escape_possibility
warn_overwhelming_threat
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
boundaries guide validation and context
boundaries do not themselves mutate gameplay state
```


# 16. Threat benchmark calculation policy

Threat comparison may use player facts, but only for display.

Allowed inputs:

```text
player level if known
player resource/body state if relevant
active severe conditions
current form constraints
known stable abilities
owned/equipped relevant items as references only
place/region danger label
creature/hazard threat label
quest warning label
```

Allowed outputs:

```text
relative danger label
warning text
context note
ReleaseCheck/audit note
```

Forbidden outputs:

```text
enemy stat changes
region level changes
automatic XP changes
automatic loot changes
automatic combat result changes
automatic quest failure/completion
```

Use labels instead of fake precise math unless formulas are explicit:

```text
safe
manageable
risky
dangerous
deadly
overwhelming
unknown
```

Important example:

```text
A wolf may be moderate objective threat.
If the player is exhausted, bleeding, and in a no-hands form, relative danger may be dangerous.
The wolf did not scale; the player's situation changed.
```

---

# 17. Encounter activation policy

An encounter can activate when source validates direct scene pressure.

Accepted activation examples:

```text
The wolf steps from the brush and growls at you.
Bandits surround you in the alley.
The bridge cracks beneath your feet.
The dragon lands in the square, scattering the crowd.
Mira draws her blade and blocks your path.
```

Rejected activation examples:

```text
People say wolves haunt the mill.
A book describes dragons.
The quest board warns of bandits.
[AIDRPG] Encounter: wolf active.
Story Card: wolves attack travelers here.
Context: a dragon is nearby.
```

Candidate-only examples:

```text
You hear howling in the distance.
Tracks suggest wolves passed recently.
The air smells of smoke and scales.
```

---

# 18. Encounter boundaries with other systems

## 18.1 Encounter and combat separation

```text
encounter candidate can exist without combat
active encounter can be social/hazard/creature sighting
combat starts only when direct engagement/attack/threat validates
combat victory can resolve encounter
encounter resolution does not create XP/loot by itself
```

## 18.2 Encounter and quest separation

```text
quest objective mentioning wolves does not spawn wolves
quest warning of dragon does not spawn dragon
encounter with quest target can update quest only through QuestReducer objective validation
encounter resolution does not auto-complete quest unless objective completion validates
```

## 18.3 Encounter and progression separation

```text
encounter activation does not grant XP
encounter survival does not grant XP unless exact XP validates
encounter victory does not grant XP unless exact XP validates
threat tier does not auto-calculate XP
player level does not scale XP table
```

## 18.4 Encounter and inventory separation

```text
encounter resolution does not create loot
creature death does not automatically add loot
loot drop creates available scene item only if InventoryReducer validates it
owned loot requires acquisition event
```

## 18.5 Encounter and form constraints

Form constraints should make encounters feel different.

Examples:

```text
wolf form with no hands in a locked-door encounter -> tool-use warning
winged form in cramped tunnel -> movement/space warning
large beast form in narrow alley -> access/movement warning
tiny form against large predator -> danger warning
water-breathing form in flood hazard -> relative danger may improve
exhausted human body facing wolves -> relative danger worsens
```

Rules:

```text
form constraints affect danger context/relative warnings
form constraints do not auto-win or auto-lose encounters
combat/quest/place owner systems still validate outcomes
```

---

# 19. Threat CanonValidator expansion

CanonValidator must enforce encounter and threat safety.

## 19.1 Accept threat benchmark when

```text
safe output or structured source directly establishes danger level/fact
source is not command/context/card/audit/debug
threat target is bound
not rumor unless stored as rumored/candidate
not player-scaling formula mutation
```

## 19.2 Accept encounter candidate when

```text
safe output shows signs/tracks/distant sound/possible danger
not direct engagement yet
```

## 19.3 Accept active encounter when

```text
safe output places threat in current scene with direct pressure
participant target bound enough
not rumor/book/card/context/command source
```

## 19.4 Accept encounter resolution when

```text
safe output directly resolves encounter by escape, defeat, negotiation, capture, surrender, or disengagement
encounter target binds to active/candidate encounter
not duplicate
```

## 19.5 Reject encounter when

Any are true:

```text
rumor-only
book/lore-only
quest objective-only
Story Card source
context source
command output
hypothetical
dream/vision only
weak mention with no current scene pressure
```

---


# 21. EventScannerLite threat/encounter hints

EventScannerLite may detect encounter hints, but detection is not canon.

Hint families:

```text
creature appears / steps out / charges / stalks
ambush / surround / block path
hazard activates / bridge cracks / gas fills room
tracks / howls / distant roar
region warning / deadly forest / cursed swamp
boss-like presence / dragon lands / giant blocks gate
escape / flee / negotiate / surrender / defeat
```

Must flag:

```text
rumorOnly
loreOnly
questTextOnly
visibleOnly
candidateOnly
activePressure
directEngagement
cardSource
contextSource
commandSource
auditSource
dreamOrVision
hypothetical
scalingRisk
xpLootRisk
duplicateRisk
ambiguousThreat
```

Parser cannot mutate threat or encounter state.

# 22. ThreatBenchmark and EncounterState helper behavior

ThreatBenchmark helper should:

```text
ensure no-rubber-band policy exists
create/update objective threat benchmark records
calculate relative danger labels only for display/context
read player level/body/form state conservatively
read region/creature/hazard threat labels if known
never mutate player/enemy/world stats
never award XP/loot
never start combat by itself
```

EncounterState helper should:

```text
create candidate encounters from signs/distant threats
activate encounters from direct scene pressure
link encounter participants without inventing full stats
link combat scene when combat validates
resolve encounters from direct output
preserve encounter history
mark dirty targets
```

It should not:

```text
spawn random enemies
scale participant stats
award XP/loot
complete quests
kill participants
teleport threats into scene from lore/card/context
```

---

# 23. Encounter defensive guards

Encounter/Threat reducers must reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
rumor/lore-only as active encounter
quest objective as spawned encounter
weak distant sign as combat
visible threat as combat without engagement
encounter victory as XP/loot
player level scaling enemy stats
duplicate encounter activation
ambiguous threat target
wrong owner reducer
```

Reducers must not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 24. Operation keys

Required threat operation keys:

```text
threat.benchmark.<threatKey>.<sourceHash>
threat.source.<sourceType>.<targetKey>.<sourceHash>
threat.region.<regionKey>.<sourceHash>
threat.creature.<creatureKey>.<sourceHash>
threat.hazard.<hazardKey>.<sourceHash>
threat.dangerContext.<placeOrEncounterKey>.<sourceHash>
threat.noRubberBand.<policyVersion>
threat.fairnessBoundary.<boundaryType>.<policyVersion>
```

Required encounter operation keys:

```text
encounter.candidate.<encounterKey>.<sourceHash>
encounter.activate.<encounterKey>.<placeKey>.<sourceHash>
encounter.participant.<encounterKey>.<participantKey>.<sourceHash>
encounter.trigger.<encounterKey>.<triggerType>.<sourceHash>
encounter.resolve.<encounterKey>.<resolutionType>.<sourceHash>
encounter.linkCombat.<encounterKey>.<combatKey>
encounter.linkQuest.<encounterKey>.<questKey>
```

Rules:

```text
encounter activation/resolution keys are durable enough to prevent replay duplication
threat policy keys are durable
relative danger recalculation keys may be recent/cache-like
cleanup must preserve no-rubber-band policy and active encounter keys
```

---

# 25. Dirty targets

Threat/encounter updates should mark:

```text
current_location
context
combat if encounter links to combat
quest_log if encounter links to quest objective
social_memory if actors witness/respond
body_status if danger context depends on severe body/form state
```

Mapping:

```text
threat benchmark -> current_location, context
encounter candidate -> current_location, context
active encounter -> current_location, context, combat if engaged
encounter resolution -> current_location, context, combat/social_memory as relevant
danger context -> current_location, context
```

Dirty targets do not write cards directly.

---

## 26.1 `/location`

Should include danger context when relevant:

```text
Current danger context
Visible/nearby threats
Encounter status if active
Region/creature/hazard warning if known
```

## 26.2 Optional `/threat`

If command budget permits:

```text
/threat
```

Should show:

```text
objective threat labels
relative danger labels
known region/creature/hazard threats
no-rubber-band policy status
warning/deferred notes
```

## 26.3 Optional `/encounter`

If command budget permits:

```text
/encounter
```

Should show:

```text
active encounter
participants
trigger
threat benchmark
combat link if any
resolution status
XP/loot warning: not automatic
```

If commands are not added, include summaries in:

```text
/location
/stateaudit
/debuglast
```

All output starts with `[AIDRPG]` and remains non-canon.

---

# 27. ContextBuilder threat/encounter packet

ContextBuilder should include concise danger facts.

Include:

```text
active encounter status
objective threat warning
relative danger label if useful
known escape/avoidance possibility if supported
form/body constraint warnings
no automatic scaling reminder only if relevant
```

Do not include:

```text
all threat benchmarks every turn
rumors as active encounters
lore/card/context threats as active
enemy stats invented from player level
loot/XP promises from threat tier
```

Example packet:

```text
Threat: A wolf is actively blocking the path. Objective threat is moderate, but you are exhausted and in wolf form with no human hands, making the situation dangerous. The wolf has not scaled to your level; danger comes from the scene and your condition.
```

---

# 28. IntegrityChecker threat/encounter checks

Add checks:

```text
no-rubber-band policy exists and is enabled
player level does not mutate threat tier
active encounter has sourceOperationKey
active encounter source is not command/card/context
rumored threat not active encounter
quest objective not spawned encounter
visible threat not combat unless engagement validates
encounter resolution does not imply XP/loot
threat benchmark has authority/source/confidence
relative danger labels not stored as objective enemy stats
active encounter participants have stable keys
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad records.

It may not spawn encounters, scale enemies, grant XP, or create loot.

---

# 29. Cleanup threat/encounter protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old low-value candidate encounter signs
old rejected rumor encounter audits
old duplicate danger context spam
old resolved encounter summaries after archive policy
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
active encounters
no-rubber-band policy
fairness boundaries
active danger context
important threat benchmarks
encounter resolution history needed for audit
operation keys preventing duplicate encounter activation/resolution
```

Cleanup must not resolve encounters, scale threats, award rewards, or remove danger warnings without validated scene change/resolution.

---

# 30. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC14-specific gates:

```text
PC14-G01 ThreatBenchmarkRecord schema exists or equivalent threat benchmark structure exists
PC14-G02 EncounterRecord schema exists or equivalent encounter structure exists
PC14-G03 EncounterParticipantRecord and EncounterTriggerRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC14-G04 EncounterResolutionRecord exists or equivalent resolution structure exists
PC14-G05 NoRubberBandPolicyRecord exists and disables automatic enemy/region/loot/XP scaling
PC14-G06 FairnessBoundaryRecord exists or equivalent boundary rules exist
PC14-G07 RegionThreatRecord, CreatureThreatRecord, and HazardThreatRecord shells exist or equivalents exist
PC14-G08 CanonValidator rejects rumor/lore/card/context/command threats as active encounters
PC14-G09 CanonValidator separates visible/candidate threat from active engagement/combat
PC14-G10 ThreatBenchmark helper calculates relative danger without mutating enemy/world stats
PC14-G11 EncounterState helper rejects unsafe routed encounter events
PC14-G12 encounter resolution does not auto-award XP/loot/quest completion
PC14-G13 duplicate encounter activation/resolution keys exist
PC14-G14 form/body constraints can influence danger context without auto-win/auto-loss
PC14-G15 /location or /threat /encounter displays danger state honestly
PC14-G16 command/context/card threat text cannot become canon
PC14-G17 cleanup preserves active encounters, policies, boundaries, and operation keys
PC14-G18 world bible/creature ecology/random encounter systems remain deferred
PC14-G19 combat/progression/inventory owner boundaries remain intact
PC14-G20 player-breaker threat/encounter tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if player level scales enemies, rumor spawns encounters, card/context text activates threats, or encounter victory grants XP/loot automatically.

Expected:

```text
resolution shown
XP/loot not shown unless owner events exist
```

---

## PC14-025 — Cleanup preserves active encounter and policy

Setup:

```text
active encounter
no-rubber-band policy
fairness boundaries
operation keys
```

Run cleanup.

Expected:

```text
active encounter/policy/boundaries/keys preserved
only old low-risk candidate signs compacted
```

```text
/location /threat /encounter display improvements
threat/encounter context packet
threat/encounter integrity checks

threat/encounter acceptance report
updated planning zip with Production Chunk 14 added
```

It should not produce:

```text
full world bible import

full enemy stat generation

old card prose encounter import
```

# 39. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 14 passes if:

```text
threat benchmark and encounter structures exist
no-rubber-band policy is active and enforced
player level does not mutate enemy/region threat
rumor/lore/card/context/command threats do not activate encounters
visible/candidate threat is separated from active engagement/combat
encounter resolution does not auto-award XP/loot/quest completion
form/body constraints can inform danger context without auto-win/auto-loss
operation keys prevent duplicate encounter activation/resolution
/location or /threat /encounter displays honestly
command/context/card threat text cannot become canon
cleanup preserves active encounters, policies, boundaries, and keys
PC14 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC14 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
creature ecology is shell-only
world bible threat import is deferred
random encounters are disabled
relative danger labels are approximate/display-only
region/faction pressure is deferred
creature stat details are unknown
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
player level scales enemy or region threat
rumor spawns active encounter
quest objective spawns encounter
Story Card/context/command creates encounter
visible distant threat becomes combat automatically
encounter victory grants XP or loot automatically
relative danger overwrites objective threat tier
duplicate encounter activation creates duplicate enemies
cleanup deletes active encounter or no-rubber-band policy
Threat/Encounter helper trusts unsafe routed data
```

---

# 40. Handoff to Production Chunk 15

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 15 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 15 — World Bible Import, Lore Authority, and Knowledge Boundaries
```

Production Chunk 15 may use threat benchmarks and encounter boundaries to safely import world facts, region danger, creature references, and lore authority.

Production Chunk 14 must leave threat/encounter state reliable enough that PC15 can ask:

```text
Is this danger a confirmed world fact, rumor, lore reference, or active encounter?
Does this creature/region threat come from a trusted source?
Can this world bible fact guide context without spawning combat?
Is player level prevented from scaling the world?
Are XP, loot, quest, and combat still owned by their own systems?
```

---

# 41. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 14 is where difficulty stops being fake scaling.

Final rule:

```text
The world does not bend around the player by default.
Threat is a fact, encounter is a state, combat is an event, reward is owner-routed, and danger context is guidance.
A fair system warns honestly instead of secretly scaling reality.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely build World Bible import and lore authority boundaries in Production Chunk 15.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 15 — World Bible Import, Lore Authority, and Knowledge Boundaries

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 15 — World Bible Import, Lore Authority, and Knowledge Boundaries  
**Production sequence:** 15 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.14 threat/encounter-boundaries accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Add world bible/lore import boundaries, source authority tiers, static-world-fact records, player-knowledge records, lore conflict handling, Story Card reference safety, context-lore safety, and discovery rules without letting lore text directly mutate gameplay state.  
**Status:** Core systems production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 14 — Threat Benchmark, Encounter Logic, and World Scaling Boundaries.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 15 exists because AI Dungeon worlds need lore, but lore is dangerous if the script treats every card, memory, or context line as live state.

This chunk is where the system learns the difference between:

```text
world fact
story card reference
current scene truth
player-known truth
rumor
myth
quest hook
creature ecology reference
region danger note
historical lore
hidden lore
active gameplay event
```

Old and rough builds risked:

```text
Story Card says player owns item -> item appears in inventory
Story Card says player is in city -> location changes
World lore mentions dragon -> encounter spawns
World lore says vampires are immortal -> player death logic changes
Context says quest completed -> quest completes
Lore says empire hates player -> reputation changes
Card says player is level 10 -> progression changes
Hidden lore appears in context before discovery
Conflicting lore silently overwrites accepted facts
```

This chunk prevents those failures.

World Bible and Story Card material can guide context and classification.

It cannot directly create active player state, items, quests, actors, combat, XP, rewards, forms, or encounters unless a dedicated owner system validates an event through normal gameplay flow.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Lore is not live state.
World fact is not player knowledge.
Hidden lore is not known lore.
Story Card text is not gameplay mutation.
Context text is not gameplay mutation.
Rumor is not fact.
Myth is not proof.
History is not current scene.
Region danger is not an encounter.
Creature lore is not combat.
Lore authority guides validation; it does not bypass validation.
```

World lore should make the game richer, not corrupt the ledger.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 14 locked threat benchmarks and encounter boundaries.

World Bible import comes next because later chunks depend on reliable lore authority:

```text
region structure
faction facts
creature ecology
species/race lore
divine/cosmic rules
settlement names
historical facts
quest context
world-specific threat labels
knowledge gates
card/context budgeting
long-run memory stability
```

If lore authority is not safe before region/faction/creature chunks, those chunks will keep asking:

```text
Can this card be trusted?
Is this lore known to the player?
Did this lore spawn a live event?
Which source wins when lore conflicts?
```

Chunk 15 answers those questions.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 15 may change:

```text
WorldBible schema helpers
LoreAuthority helper
LoreImportManager
LoreClaim parser shell
KnowledgeBoundary helper
PlayerKnowledge helper
CanonValidator lore/import rules
ProposalBuilder lore event types
EventScannerLite lore hints
CommandRenderers /world, /lore, /knowledge if supported
ContextBuilder world/lore packet
CardSyncLite dry-run/world-card read boundary
OperationLedger lore/import/knowledge keys
DirtyQueue world/lore/context targets
IntegrityChecker lore checks
ReleaseCheck lore gates
Regression/player-breaker lore tests
```

Production Chunk 15 may add:

```text
WorldBibleRecord schema
LoreSourceRecord schema
LoreClaimRecord schema
LoreAuthorityRecord schema
LoreConflictRecord schema
LoreImportSessionRecord schema
PlayerKnowledgeRecord schema
KnowledgeBoundaryRecord schema
DiscoveryRecord schema
HiddenLoreRecord schema
WorldRegionLoreRecord shell
FactionLoreRecord shell
CreatureLoreRecord shell
SpeciesLoreRecord shell
HistoryLoreRecord shell
LoreContextPacket rules
StoryCardLoreReference boundary
```

Production Chunk 15 may not add:

```text
full region simulation
full faction/reputation system
full creature ecology engine
full divine/cosmic system
full random event generator
active quest generation from lore
active encounter spawning from lore
manual /importascanon mutation commands
live card writes that create gameplay truth
old card prose gameplay import
```

---

# 4. Canonical state path

World/lore data must live under the canonical WorldLedger root.

Recommended path:

```text
state.worldledger.worldBible
```

Recommended shape:

```text
worldBible: {
  records: {},
  sources: {},
  claims: {},
  authorities: {},
  conflicts: {},
  imports: {},
  playerKnowledge: {},
  hiddenLore: {},
  discoveryRecords: {},
  regionLore: {},
  factionLore: {},
  creatureLore: {},
  speciesLore: {},
  historyLore: {},
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

Do not create competing roots such as:

```text
state.worldBible
state.loreTruth
state.storyCardsCanon
state.worldStateFromCards
```

---

# 5. Required schemas

## 5.1 WorldBibleRecord

```text
WorldBibleRecord {
  id,
  key,
  title,
  category,
  status,
  authorityKey,
  sourceKeys,
  claimKeys,
  visibility,
  playerKnowledgeStatus,
  currentUsePolicy,
  relatedRegionKeys,
  relatedFactionKeys,
  relatedCreatureKeys,
  relatedSpeciesKeys,
  relatedPlaceKeys,
  relatedActorKeys,
  relatedQuestKeys,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Categories:

```text
region, place, faction, species, creature, history, religion, divine_deferred,
cosmic_deferred, law, culture, magic_rule, economy_deferred, quest_lore,
character_lore, unknown
```

Statuses:

```text
confirmed_lore, probable_lore, rumored_lore, mythic_lore, hidden_lore,
contradicted, rejected, blocked_source, unknown
```

## 5.2 LoreSourceRecord

```text
LoreSourceRecord {
  id,
  key,
  sourceType,
  sourceName,
  sourcePointer,
  authorityTier,
  trustedForCategories,
  importMode,
  safeForGameplayMutation,
  createdTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Source types:

```text
structured_startup, scenario_setup, world_bible_file, story_card,
memory_context, model_output, player_input, quest_log, command_output,
debug_output, unknown
```

Authority tiers:

```text
authoritative_setup, trusted_world_bible, supporting_story_card,
runtime_observation, player_claim, npc_claim, rumor, blocked_command,
blocked_context, blocked_card_runtime, unknown
```

Default safety rule:

```text
Story Cards, memory/context, command output, and debug output have safeForGameplayMutation = false.
```

## 5.3 LoreClaimRecord

```text
LoreClaimRecord {
  id,

}
```

## 5.4 LoreAuthorityRecord

```text
LoreAuthorityRecord {
  id,
  key,
  category,
  authorityOrder,
  conflictPolicy,
  defaultVisibility,
  defaultMutationPolicy,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Recommended authority order:

```text
1. structured startup / scenario setup
2. trusted world bible file
3. accepted runtime story events
4. quest/actor/place records from owner reducers
5. supporting story cards as reference-only lore
6. NPC claims / rumors
7. player speculation
8. command/context/debug text blocked
```

## 5.5 LoreConflictRecord

```text
LoreConflictRecord {
  id,

  claimBKey,
  conflictType,
  resolutionStatus,
  chosenClaimKey,
  reason,
```

Conflicts must never silently overwrite current state.

```text
LoreImportSessionRecord {
```

Imports must be idempotent and duplicate-safe.

```text
PlayerKnowledgeRecord {
```

Knowledge statuses:

```text
unknown_to_player, suspected, rumored, known, confirmed_by_experience,
misinformed, forgotten_deferred, unknown
```

## 5.8 KnowledgeBoundaryRecord

```text
KnowledgeBoundaryRecord {
  id,
  key,
  boundaryType,
  rule,
  active,
  affectedCategories,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Required boundaries:

```text
hidden_lore_not_in_context
world_fact_not_player_knowledge
rumor_not_fact
myth_not_fact
card_lore_not_gameplay_state
context_not_gameplay_state
owner_state_beats_lore_for_current_state
```

```text
DiscoveryRecord {
```


Allowed context use:

```text
none, foreshadowing_only, validator_only, full_after_reveal, unknown
```

# 6. Region/Faction/Creature/Species/History lore shells

Chunk 15 creates reference shells only.

```text
WorldRegionLoreRecord {
  id, key, displayName, parentRegionKey, threatTierReference, knownSettlements,
  climateTags, cultureTags, sourceAuthority, playerKnowledgeStatus, notes
}

FactionLoreRecord {
  id, key, displayName, publicDescription, hiddenDescriptionKey, knownRelations,
  sourceAuthority, playerKnowledgeStatus, notes
}

CreatureLoreRecord {
  id, key, displayName, family, threatTierReference, knownTraits,
  rumoredTraits, sourceAuthority, playerKnowledgeStatus, notes
}

SpeciesLoreRecord {
  id, key, displayName, bodyDefaults, culturalNotes, powerRulesDeferred,
  sourceAuthority, playerKnowledgeStatus, notes
}

HistoryLoreRecord {
  id, key, title, era, summary, sourceAuthority, playerKnowledgeStatus, notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
shell records do not activate region/faction/creature systems
powerRulesDeferred must not grant powers
threatTierReference must not spawn encounter
hiddenDescriptionKey must not leak into normal context
```

---

# 7. Story Card lore boundary

AI Dungeon Story Cards are important, but unsafe as live-state mutation sources.

Default Story Card behavior:

```text
read as supporting lore/reference
never directly mutate player inventory
never directly mutate current location
never directly mutate active quest state
never directly mutate actor presence
never directly mutate body/race/form
never directly mutate XP/level/stats
never directly spawn encounter/combat
```

Allowed Story Card uses:

```text
support world lore
support region/faction/species/creature descriptions
support names/aliases/reference matching
support context summaries after knowledge-boundary filtering
support validator warnings about contradictions
support future card mirror dry-run output
```

Forbidden Story Card uses:

```text
Player owns Crown of Dawn -> inventory grant
Player is level 10 -> level set
Current location is Chapel -> teleport/location overwrite
Dragon attacks here -> encounter spawn
Mira trusts player -> relationship mutation
Player is dragon -> form/race mutation
Quest completed -> quest completion
```

---

# 8. Context lore boundary

Context is output to the AI model.

It must never be re-imported as fresh gameplay truth.

Rules:

```text
ContextBuilder text is non-canon source for mutation
context summaries can guide generation
context summaries cannot be parsed as new events
context must label hidden/rumor/candidate facts carefully
context must not leak hidden lore
```

Critical rule:

```text
Anything generated by /context, /status, /sheet, /lore, /world, /knowledge,
/debug, /releasecheck, or CardSync mirror output must be source-blocked from gameplay mutation.
```

---

# 9. Lore CanonValidator expansion

CanonValidator must enforce lore safety.

## 9.1 Accept lore claim when

```text
source is allowed for lore import
claim category is recognized
claim does not attempt active gameplay mutation
claim has authority tier assigned
operation key not duplicate
```

Result:

```text
LoreClaimRecord and possible WorldBibleRecord
no owner-system mutation
```

## 9.2 Accept player knowledge update when

```text
safe runtime event shows player discovered/read/heard/witnessed lore
knowledge target is bound
not command/context/card source unless card is an in-world object being read through gameplay output
operation key not duplicate
```

Result:

```text
PlayerKnowledgeRecord update
no unrelated gameplay mutation
```

## 9.3 Reject gameplay mutation from lore when

```text
Story Card says active state
context says active state
command output says active state
lore claim tries to grant item/XP/ability/form/quest/combat result
hidden lore tries to enter player-known context
rumor tries to become confirmed fact
```

---

# 10. ProposalBuilder and EventScanner lore support

Add event types:

```text
lore.claim_candidate
lore.import_candidate
lore.source_candidate
lore.authority_candidate
lore.conflict_candidate
lore.player_knowledge_candidate
lore.discovery_candidate
lore.hidden_candidate
lore.reference_link_candidate
lore.blocked_gameplay_mutation_candidate
world.region_lore_candidate
world.faction_lore_candidate
world.creature_lore_candidate
world.species_lore_candidate
world.history_lore_candidate
```

EventScannerLite may detect lore hints, but detection is not canon.

Must flag:

```text
storyCardSource
contextSource
commandSource
debugSource
hiddenLore
rumorOrMyth
playerKnownClaim
activeMutationAttempt
ownerSystemClaim
conflictRisk
spoilerRisk
duplicateRisk
```

Parser cannot mutate owner gameplay systems from lore scan.

---

# 11. Manager behavior

## 11.1 LoreImportManager

Should:

```text
create import session
normalize source records
extract lore claims conservatively
assign authority tier
assign visibility/player knowledge status
create WorldBibleRecords only for accepted lore claims
create conflict records when needed
block active gameplay mutation attempts
record operation keys
mark lore/context dirty
```

Must not:

```text
add items
complete quests
spawn actors
move current location
change player identity/form
award XP/levels/stats
start combat/encounters
change reputation/faction standing
```

## 11.2 LoreAuthorityManager

Should:

```text
rank sources by authority
resolve conflicts or mark unresolved
preserve runtime current state against lower-authority lore
separate world fact from player knowledge
separate public lore from hidden lore
expose warnings to /lore and /integrity
```

## 11.3 KnowledgeBoundaryManager

Should:

```text
filter lore before context insertion
exclude hidden lore unless revealed
label rumor/myth/candidate lore
prevent player-unknown facts from being written as player-known
prevent context summaries from becoming mutation sources
```

---

# 12. CardSyncLite integration boundary

This chunk does not activate generated card writes.

Allowed:

```text
read existing cards as possible lore references if source mode allows
record card source as supporting_story_card
quarantine/block active gameplay claims from cards
produce dry-run card audit/mirror notes
```

Forbidden:

```text
write new generated lore cards
rewrite protected cards from lore import
treat card prose as owner-state truth
sync card edits into gameplay state automatically
```

CardSync remains safe/dry-run unless later package activates protected writes with tests.

---

# 13. Operation keys

Required lore operation keys:

```text
lore.source.<sourceKey>.<sourceHash>
lore.import.<importMode>.<sourceSetHash>
lore.claim.<category>.<claimHash>
lore.record.<recordKey>.<claimHash>
lore.authority.<category>.<policyVersion>
lore.conflict.<claimAKey>.<claimBKey>.<conflictHash>
lore.knowledge.<loreRecordKey>.<discoveryHash>
lore.discovery.<discoveryType>.<sourceHash>
lore.hidden.<loreRecordKey>.<sourceHash>
lore.blockedMutation.<ownerSystem>.<claimHash>
```

Rules:

```text
import/claim keys prevent duplicate lore spam
blocked mutation keys prevent repeated warning spam
authority keys are durable
knowledge/discovery keys are durable enough for continuity
cleanup must preserve conflicts and hidden lore controls
```

---

# 14. Dirty targets

Lore updates should mark:

```text
world_lore
context
stateaudit
releasecheck if authority/conflict changed
current_location only as display/reference dirty, not current state mutation
quest_log only as lore reference dirty, not quest mutation
```

Dirty targets do not write cards directly.

---

# 15. Commands and context

If command budget permits, add:

```text
/world
/lore
/knowledge
```

If not, fold summaries into:

```text
/stateaudit
/integrity
/location
/actors
```

## /world

Shows broad known world facts:

```text
known regions
known factions
known species/creature lore
known history
hidden lore count without spoilers
conflicts/warnings summary
```

## /lore

Shows lore with authority:

```text
confirmed lore
rumored/mythic lore
hidden/restricted lore count
conflicts
blocked gameplay mutation claims
```

## /knowledge

Shows what the player knows:

```text
known to player
suspected/rumored by player
confirmed by experience
misinformation if tracked
unknown/hidden not revealed
```

All output starts with `[AIDRPG]` and remains non-canon.

## ContextBuilder lore packet

Include:

```text
player-known relevant lore
confirmed world facts needed for current scene
rumored lore clearly labeled as rumor
creature/region/faction lore relevant to current encounter/place/quest
```

Do not include:

```text
hidden lore before discovery
blocked card/context gameplay claims
full world bible dump
lore unrelated to current scene
rumor as confirmed fact
card lore as current state
player-unknown secrets as player-known
```

---

# 16. IntegrityChecker lore checks

Add checks:

```text
worldBible bucket exists under canonical root
lore sources have authority tiers
Story Card/context/command sources cannot mutate owner state
hidden lore not present in normal context packet
player knowledge records separate from world facts
conflicts are recorded, not silently overwritten
lore claims have operation keys
blocked mutation attempts recorded/quarantined
owner current state beats lower-authority lore for active state
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad lore records.

It may not import lore into gameplay owner state.

---

# 17. Cleanup lore protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old duplicate blocked mutation warnings
old rejected lore scan spam
old low-value rumor duplicates
old import debug traces after summary
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
confirmed lore records
authority records
unresolved conflicts
hidden lore controls
player knowledge/discovery records
blocked mutation records needed for audit
source records used by active lore
```

Cleanup must not reveal hidden lore, confirm rumors, or turn lore into active gameplay state.

---

# 18. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC15-specific gates:

```text
PC15-G01 WorldBibleRecord schema exists or equivalent lore record structure exists
PC15-G02 LoreSourceRecord schema exists with authority tiers
PC15-G03 LoreClaimRecord schema exists or equivalent claim structure exists
PC15-G04 LoreAuthorityRecord exists and defines source precedence
PC15-G05 LoreConflictRecord exists or equivalent conflict handling exists
PC15-G06 PlayerKnowledgeRecord and KnowledgeBoundaryRecord exist or equivalents exist
PC15-G07 DiscoveryRecord and HiddenLoreRecord exist or equivalents exist
PC15-G08 Story Card lore boundary prevents active gameplay mutation
PC15-G09 Context lore boundary prevents re-import as gameplay truth
PC15-G10 LoreImportManager is idempotent and duplicate-safe
PC15-G11 LoreAuthorityManager preserves owner current state against lower-authority lore
PC15-G12 hidden lore does not enter normal context before reveal
PC15-G13 rumor/myth lore remains labeled and does not become confirmed fact
PC15-G14 lore cannot spawn encounters, grant items, complete quests, set XP/stats, or change forms
PC15-G15 /world /lore /knowledge or equivalent displays authority/knowledge honestly
PC15-G16 command/context/card lore text cannot become owner canon
PC15-G17 cleanup preserves authority/conflict/hidden/knowledge records
PC15-G18 region/faction/creature/divine advanced systems remain deferred
PC15-G19 player-breaker lore/import tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if Story Card/context lore can mutate owner state, hidden lore leaks into normal context, or lore conflicts silently overwrite current gameplay truth.

---

# 22. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 15 passes if:

```text
world bible/lore state structure exists
source authority tiers exist
Story Card and context lore cannot mutate owner state
world facts and player knowledge are separated
hidden lore is filtered from normal context
rumors/myths remain labeled
conflicts are recorded instead of silent overwrites
lore import is idempotent
owner current state beats lower-authority lore for active state
/lore /world /knowledge or equivalents display honestly
cleanup preserves authority/conflict/hidden/knowledge records
PC15 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC15 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
region/faction/creature lore shells are not yet full systems
manual file review is still conservative
some lore claims remain unresolved conflicts
hidden lore reveal conditions are simple
commands are folded into /stateaudit instead of separate /lore commands
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
Story Card grants item/XP/ability/form/location/quest/combat state
context text mutates gameplay state
hidden lore appears in normal context before discovery
rumor becomes confirmed fact without authority
lore conflict silently overwrites current state
owner reducer current state loses to lower-authority card lore
lore import duplicates records uncontrollably
cleanup deletes authority/conflict/hidden knowledge controls
manual import-as-canon command mutates state
LoreImportManager trusts unsafe routed data
```

---

# 23. Handoff to Production Chunk 16

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 16 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 16 — Context Builder Budget, Memory Priority, and Card Sync Safety
```

Production Chunk 16 may use lore authority and player-knowledge boundaries to make context packets smarter and safer.

Production Chunk 15 must leave lore state reliable enough that PC16 can ask:

```text
Which lore is confirmed?
Which lore is only rumored or mythic?
Which lore is hidden from the player?
Which lore is relevant to current scene?
Which card/context claims are blocked from mutation?
Which source wins if claims conflict?
Can this lore be shown to the model without spoiling or corrupting gameplay?
```

---

# 24. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 15 is where world lore becomes useful without becoming dangerous.

Final rule:

```text
World lore can guide the game, but it cannot bypass the game.
Story Cards are references, not reducers.
Context is memory, not evidence.
The player only knows what they have discovered, heard, read, or been told through valid gameplay.
Hidden truth stays hidden until revealed.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen Context Builder budgeting, memory priority, and Card Sync safety in Production Chunk 16.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 16 — Context Builder Budget, Memory Priority, and Card Sync Safety

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 16 — Context Builder Budget, Memory Priority, and Card Sync Safety  
**Production sequence:** 16 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.15 world-bible/lore-boundaries accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Deepen context packet construction, token/character budgeting, memory priority, source blocking, stale-context prevention, safe summaries, dry-run Card Sync, protected card boundaries, and context/card non-canon safety without losing gameplay-critical state or re-importing generated summaries as truth.  
**Status:** Core infrastructure production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 15 — World Bible Import, Lore Authority, and Knowledge Boundaries.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 16 exists because AI Dungeon scripting succeeds or fails on context. A perfect ledger still fails if the prompt sends stale facts, hidden lore, false ownership, unpaid rewards as paid rewards, candidates as confirmed powers, or card mirror text as canon. This chunk makes context and card handling safe, prioritized, compact, source-aware, and useful for real gameplay.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Context is guidance, not evidence.
Command output is guidance, not evidence.
Card mirror output is guidance, not evidence.
Generated summaries are guidance, not evidence.
Stale context must never beat current ledger truth.
Hidden lore must not leak into normal context.
Candidate state must not be worded as confirmed state.
Dry-run Card Sync is not live Card Sync.
Card writes must be protected, gated, audited, and reversible before activation.
```

Context should help the model remember the right things. It must not become another source of false canon.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 15 separated world fact, player knowledge, hidden lore, Story Card lore, and context non-canon behavior. Context and Card Sync safety come next because every previous system needs a reliable way to talk to AI Dungeon:

```text
player identity and body
inventory/equipment
abilities
quests/rewards
actors/social memory
place/time/deadlines
progression/stats
combat state
form/race constraints
threat/encounter danger
world lore/player knowledge
```

If context is not budgeted and prioritized now, later systems will add too much information and break coherence.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 16 may change:

```text
ContextBuilderLite / ContextBuilder
ContextPacket helpers
MemoryPriority helpers
ContextBudgetManager
ContextSourceBlocker
ContextStalenessChecker
ContextCompression/Summary helper
CardSyncLite dry-run behavior
CardAudit renderer
CommandRenderers /context, /memory, /cardaudit, /stateaudit
DirtyQueue target handling
OperationLedger context/card keys
IntegrityChecker context/card checks
ReleaseCheck context/card gates
Regression/player-breaker context tests
```

Production Chunk 16 may add:

```text
ContextPacketRecord schema
ContextSectionRecord schema
MemoryPriorityRecord schema
ContextBudgetRecord schema
ContextSourceRecord schema
ContextBlockRecord schema
ContextStalenessRecord schema
ContextSummaryRecord schema
CardMirrorRecord schema
CardSyncPlanRecord schema
ProtectedCardRecord schema
CardWriteProposalRecord schema
CardSyncAuditRecord schema
ContextNonCanonPolicyRecord schema
```

Production Chunk 16 may not add:

```text
live card writes by default
unprotected Story Card rewrites
automatic card creation from gameplay output
card text as gameplay truth
context text as gameplay truth
hidden lore injection into normal context
large raw-state dumps every turn
manual /writecard live mutation command
```

Live Card Sync, if ever enabled, must be separately feature-flagged, ReleaseCheck-gated, and protected. Default in this chunk is dry-run / mirror / audit only.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
ContextBuilderLite
ContextBudgetManager
MemoryPriorityManager
ContextSourceBlocker
ContextStalenessChecker
ContextCompression helper
DirtyQueue
CardSyncLite dry-run/mirror path
CardAudit renderer
CommandRenderers: /context, /memory, /cardaudit, /stateaudit
OperationLedger context/card keys
AuditLogger context/card lines
InvariantChecker context/card checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC16 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to owner reducers. ContextBuilder reads owner state; it must not mutate owner state.

---

# 5. Context state model

Recommended shape:

```text
contextSystem: {
  packets: {},
  sections: {},
  priorities: {},
  budgets: {},
  summaries: {},
  sourceBlocks: {},
  staleness: {},
  cardMirrors: {},
  cardSyncPlans: {},
  protectedCards: {},
  cardWriteProposals: {},
  cardSyncAudits: {},
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

Context state is support state. It is not gameplay canon.

---

# 6. ContextPacketRecord schema

```text
ContextPacketRecord {
  id,
  key,
  packetType,
  sections,
  totalBudget,
  usedBudget,
  overflowPolicy,
  sourceOperationKey,
  generatedTurn,
  stale,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Packet types:

```text
main_context
memory_context
author_note_context
story_card_mirror_context
debug_context
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
main_context should be compact and current
memory_context should be stable and high-priority
debug_context must be blocked as mutation source
story_card_mirror_context must be dry-run/non-canon unless live writes separately enabled
```

---

# 7. ContextSectionRecord schema

```text
ContextSectionRecord {
  id,
  key,
  sectionType,
  title,
  text,
  priority,
  budgetCost,
  sourceFamily,
  sourceKeys,
  freshnessTurn,
  confidence,
  playerKnownOnly,
  hiddenLoreFiltered,
  stale,
  included,
  omittedReason,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Section types:

```text
player_identity
body_status
current_form
inventory_equipment
abilities
quests
actors_social
location_time
combat
threat_encounter
progression_stats
world_lore
player_knowledge
rules_boundaries
warnings
unknown
```

Critical sections should survive budget pressure. Blocked/debug sections must not enter normal context. Hidden lore must be filtered before text is built.

---

# 8. MemoryPriorityRecord schema

```text
MemoryPriorityRecord {
  id,
  key,
  targetFamily,
  targetKey,
  priority,
  reason,
  decayPolicy,
  lastRelevantTurn,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Target families:

```text
player
body
form
inventory
ability
quest
actor
place
time
combat
threat
lore
knowledge
system_warning
unknown
```

Critical identity/body/current scene facts should not decay away. Old low-relevance facts can be summarized or omitted.

---

# 9. ContextBudgetRecord schema

```text
ContextBudgetRecord {
  id,
  key,
  budgetType,
  maxChars,
  maxTokensApprox,
  reservedCriticalBudget,
  usedChars,
  overflowPolicy,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Overflow policies:

```text
omit_low_priority
summarize_low_priority
compress_sections
warn_and_truncate
block_debug
unknown
```

Budget manager should never drop critical warnings silently. Low-priority lore and resolved history are omitted before current identity, body, form, location, combat, active quest, or deadline facts.

---

# 10. ContextSourceRecord schema

```text
ContextSourceRecord {
  id,
  key,
  sourceFamily,
  sourceKey,
  ownerSystem,
  canonStatus,
  safeForContext,
  safeForMutation,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Canon statuses:

```text
owner_canon
reference_only
candidate
rumor
hidden
blocked
summary_generated
command_output
debug_output
unknown
```

Owner canon may be summarized into context. Generated summaries, command output, debug output, and card mirror output are not safe for mutation.

---

# 11. ContextBlockRecord schema

```text
ContextBlockRecord {
  id,
  key,
  blockReason,
  sourceFamily,
  sourceKey,
  textPreview,
  targetPacketType,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Block reasons:

```text
hidden_lore
unsafe_source
debug_only
stale_superseded
candidate_would_look_confirmed
too_low_priority
over_budget
card_mutation_risk
context_reimport_risk
unknown
```

Blocked content may appear in /stateaudit or /debug, not normal context, and must not be parsed as gameplay event.

---

# 12. ContextStalenessRecord schema

```text
ContextStalenessRecord {
  id,
  key,
  staleFamily,
  staleKey,
  staleTextPreview,
  currentSourceKey,
  detectedTurn,
  action,
  sourceOperationKey,
  notes
}
```

Actions:

```text
omit_stale
replace_with_current
mark_warning
archive_summary
block_release_if_critical
unknown
```

Examples:

```text
old currentLocation says Chapel while PlaceReducer says Market Lane
old inventory summary says Crown owned while InventoryReducer says Mira custody
old ability summary says Moonblade stable while AbilityReducer says hint only
```

Current owner reducer state beats old context summary.

---

# 13. ContextSummaryRecord schema

```text
ContextSummaryRecord {
  id,
  key,
  summaryFamily,
  text,
  sourceKeys,
  generatedBy,
  safeForContext,
  safeForMutation,

  notes
}
```

GeneratedBy:

```text
deterministic_renderer
safe_compressor
manual_deferred
model_generated_blocked
unknown
```

Summaries must preserve uncertainty labels. They must not promote candidate, rumor, visible, offered, unpaid, pending, or deferred states into confirmed truth.

---

# 14. ContextNonCanonPolicyRecord schema

```text
ContextNonCanonPolicyRecord {
  id,
  key,
  policyName,
  active,
  blockedSourceFamilies,
  allowedUse,
  mutationAllowed,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Required blocked source families:

```text
context_output
command_output
debug_output
releasecheck_output
stateaudit_output
cardaudit_output
card_mirror_output
model_summary_output
```

Required:

```text
allowedUse = guidance_display_only
mutationAllowed = false
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if this policy is missing or inactive.

---

# 15. CardMirrorRecord schema

```text
CardMirrorRecord {
  id,
  key,
  mirrorType,
  targetCardKey,
  title,
  text,
  sourceKeys,
  protected,
  dryRunOnly,
  safeForMutation,
  createdTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Required defaults:

```text
dryRunOnly = true
safeForMutation = false
protected = true unless explicitly safe unprotected draft
```

Card mirrors can be shown in /cardaudit. They are not written by default and cannot be parsed back into gameplay state.

---

# 16. CardSyncPlanRecord schema

```text
CardSyncPlanRecord {
  id,
  key,
  planType,
  targetCardKey,
  action,
  sourceKeys,
  beforePreview,
  afterPreview,
  riskLevel,
  requiresConfirmation,
  liveWriteAllowed,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Required default:

```text
liveWriteAllowed = false
requiresConfirmation = true
```

Planning is allowed. Live writing is disabled unless future gated policy enables it.

---

# 17. ProtectedCardRecord schema

```text
ProtectedCardRecord {
  id,
  key,
  cardKey,
  title,
  protectionLevel,
  ownerFamily,
  allowedWriteModes,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Protection levels:

```text
locked
protected
manual_review_required
dry_run_only
unprotected_draft
unknown
```

Existing user-authored cards should default protected/manual_review_required. Generated mirrors default dry_run_only.

---

# 18. CardWriteProposalRecord schema

```text
CardWriteProposalRecord {
  id,
  key,
  targetCardKey,
  proposalType,
  proposedText,
  sourceKeys,
  riskFlags,
  status,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Statuses:

```text
dry_run
blocked
manual_review_required
approved_later_disabled
written_later_disabled
rejected
unknown
```

Proposal status is dry_run or blocked in PC16. No actual write in this chunk.

---

# 19. CardSyncAuditRecord schema

```text
CardSyncAuditRecord {
  id,
  key,
  auditType,
  targetCardKey,
  actionTaken,
  liveWriteOccurred,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  summary,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Required default:

```text
liveWriteOccurred = false
```

---

# 20. Context priority order

Recommended critical order:

```text
1. system safety boundaries / source-blocking warnings when needed
2. player identity and true/current form/body constraints
3. current location/current scene/time/deadlines
4. active combat/damage/death-boundary critical status
5. active encounter/threat warning
6. active quest objective and unpaid/paid reward truth
7. active injuries/conditions/resources
8. immediately present actors/witnesses
9. owned/equipped critical inventory and visible/custody distinctions
10. stable abilities and important candidates/failures
11. relevant player-known lore
12. recent important history
13. low-priority background lore
14. debug/audit omitted from normal context
```

Identity/form/current scene should not be pushed out by background lore.

---

# 21. Section wording safety rules

Required wording examples:

```text
Candidate ability: Ember Ward is being trained, not stable.
Visible item: A silver ring is visible nearby, not owned.
Custody: Mira holds the Crown of Dawn; it is not yours.
Reward: Old Bren promised 15 gold; it has not been paid.
Quest: Clear the Mill Wolves is active; objective incomplete.
Form: You are physically in wolf form; true race remains Human.
Lore: Local rumor says wolves fear silver bells; unconfirmed.
Hidden lore: omitted.
```

Forbidden unless truly confirmed:

```text
You know Ember Ward.
You have the silver ring.
You own the Crown.
Old Bren paid 15 gold.
The wolves fear silver bells.
You are a wolf species now.
The hidden bell controls the wolves.
```

---

# 22. ContextBuilder behavior

ContextBuilder should:

```text
read owner state only
request section renderers
filter by source/knowledge boundaries
score priority
fit sections to budget
compress low-priority safe sections
label uncertainty
omit hidden/blocked/debug content
emit ContextPacketRecord
mark output as non-canon source
```

ContextBuilder should not mutate owner state, infer missing owner state, promote candidate facts, reveal hidden lore, write cards live, or create memories as gameplay events.

---

# 23. ContextBudgetManager behavior

Budget manager should:

```text
reserve critical budget
calculate approximate char/token cost
include critical sections first
summarize or omit low-priority sections
record omitted reasons
warn when critical sections are too large
never fill budget with hidden/unsafe content
```

Fallback order:

```text
omit debug
omit stale low-priority summaries
omit irrelevant lore
compress old history
compress actors/inventory to current scene essentials
preserve current identity/body/location/quest/combat warnings
```

---

# 24. MemoryPriorityManager behavior

MemoryPriorityManager should promote active/current/critical facts and lower old/resolved/background facts. It must keep protected identity, form, active quest, deadline, danger, and severe body facts visible when relevant.

It must not prioritize background lore over current state, keep resolved wounds as active injuries, keep old locations after movement, or hide active deadlines because lore is long.

---

# 25. ContextSourceBlocker behavior

Blocked sources:

```text
/context output
/sheet output
/status output
/inventory output
/abilities output
/quests output
/actors output
/location output
/time output
/progression output
/stats output
/combat output
/threat output
/encounter output
/world output
/lore output
/knowledge output
/stateaudit output
/releasecheck output
/cardaudit output
card mirror output
debug output
model-generated summaries
```

These outputs can be shown to the user and can guide the model, but they must be tagged source-blocked if they return through Output hook.

---

# 26. ContextStalenessChecker behavior

Staleness checks:

```text
old current location vs PlaceReducer current location
old form vs Body/Form current form
old inventory ownership vs InventoryReducer access state
old ability stable/candidate state vs AbilityReducer
old quest reward payment vs QuestReducer/InventoryReducer
old actor presence vs ActorReducer
old combat damage vs BodyReducer
old lore visibility vs KnowledgeBoundaryManager
```

Actions include replace, omit, warn, archive, or block ReleaseCheck if stale critical context can emit.

---

# 27. CardSyncLite behavior

CardSyncLite in PC16 is dry-run only.

Allowed:

```text
build card mirror records
build card sync plans
show /cardaudit output
mark protected cards
mark unsafe claims
suggest manual review text
```

Forbidden:

```text
live create card
live update card
live delete card
overwrite user-authored card
unprotect card automatically
parse card mirror as gameplay event
```

Default user-facing wording:

```text
[AIDRPG] Card Sync is dry-run only. No Story Cards were written.
```

---

# 28. Card safety risk flags

Risk flags:

```text
owner_state_claim
hidden_lore_leak
candidate_promoted
current_location_claim
inventory_ownership_claim
quest_completion_claim
reward_payment_claim
ability_mastery_claim
race_form_claim
combat_damage_claim
xp_level_claim
actor_relationship_claim
reputation_claim
source_uncertain
protected_card
unknown
```

Any owner_state_claim blocks live write in PC16. Hidden lore leak blocks normal context and card mirror unless reveal-safe.

---

# 29. Operation keys

Required context operation keys:

```text
context.packet.<packetType>.<turnBucket>.<sourceHash>
context.section.<sectionType>.<sourceHash>
context.summary.<summaryFamily>.<sourceHash>
context.block.<blockReason>.<sourceHash>
context.stale.<staleFamily>.<staleKey>.<sourceHash>
context.priority.<targetFamily>.<targetKey>.<reason>
context.noncanon.policy.<policyVersion>
```

Required Card Sync operation keys:

```text
card.mirror.<mirrorType>.<targetCardKey>.<sourceHash>
card.sync.plan.<action>.<targetCardKey>.<sourceHash>
card.protected.<cardKey>.<protectionLevel>
card.writeProposal.<targetCardKey>.<proposalHash>
card.sync.audit.<auditType>.<targetCardKey>.<sourceHash>
```

Non-canon policy keys and protected card keys are durable.

---

# 30. Dirty targets

Context/card updates should mark:

```text
context
memory
card_registry_dry_run
stateaudit
releasecheck if policies change
```

Owner dirty targets may trigger context rebuilds, but context rebuilds must not trigger owner state mutation.

---

# 31. Command updates

## `/context`

Shows context packet preview, included sections, omitted sections with reasons, budget used, hidden lore filtering, and non-canon warning.

## `/memory`

Shows priority facts, active/current memories, summarized old facts, blocked/stale memory warnings.

## `/cardaudit`

Shows Card Sync mode dry-run, protected cards, proposed mirrors, blocked write risks, and confirmation that no live writes occurred.

## `/stateaudit`

Shows context/source-blocking policy status, stale context checks, memory priority warnings, and card sync safety status.

All command output starts with `[AIDRPG]` and remains non-canon.

---

# 32. Good and bad context examples

Good:

```text
Player: True race Human; currently physically in wolf form. No human hands, quadruped movement, altered speech.
Location: Market Lane, crowded street near the old fountain.
Quest: Clear the Mill Wolves is active; Old Bren promised 15 gold, not paid.
Inventory: You carry a red healing potion. Mira holds the Crown of Dawn; it is not yours.
Ability: Ember Ward is a candidate under training, not stable.
Lore: Local rumor says wolves fear silver bells; unconfirmed. Hidden wolf-bell truth is not known.
```

Bad:

```text
You are a wolf species. You own the Crown. Old Bren paid you 15 gold. You know Ember Ward. Wolves fear silver bells. You are in the Chapel.
```

Bad because it promotes or overwrites current form into species, custody into ownership, promise into payment, candidate into stable, rumor/hidden lore into fact, and wrong location into current state.

---

# 33. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC16-specific gates:

```text
PC16-G01 ContextPacketRecord schema exists or equivalent packet structure exists
PC16-G02 ContextSectionRecord schema exists or equivalent section structure exists
PC16-G03 MemoryPriorityRecord and ContextBudgetRecord exist or equivalents exist
PC16-G04 ContextSourceRecord, ContextBlockRecord, and ContextStalenessRecord exist or equivalents exist
PC16-G05 ContextSummaryRecord preserves uncertainty and is not safe for mutation
PC16-G06 ContextNonCanonPolicyRecord exists and blocks command/context/debug/card mirror output from mutation
PC16-G07 ContextBuilder filters hidden lore and player-unknown lore correctly
PC16-G08 ContextBuilder does not promote candidate/rumor/visible/offered/unpaid/pending state
PC16-G09 ContextBudgetManager preserves critical current state under budget pressure
PC16-G10 ContextStalenessChecker prevents stale context from beating owner state
PC16-G11 CardMirrorRecord and CardSyncPlanRecord exist or equivalents exist
PC16-G12 CardSyncLite is dry-run only by default
PC16-G13 ProtectedCardRecord and CardWriteProposalRecord exist or equivalents exist

PC16-G18 live Card Sync remains disabled unless future gated spec enables it

```

ReleaseCheck must fail if context text can be parsed back into state, hidden lore leaks into normal context, stale context overwrites owner state, or Card Sync writes live cards by default.

# 40. Beginner implementation order

```text
1. Copy WorldLedgerV0.17.15_WORLD_BIBLE_LORE_BOUNDARIES_ACCEPTED.txt.
2. Create WorldLedgerV0.17.16_CONTEXT_CARD_SAFETY_WORKING.txt.
3. Add context helpers: ensureContextSystemShape, makeContextPacketRecord, makeContextSectionRecord, makeMemoryPriorityRecord, makeContextBudgetRecord, makeContextSourceRecord, makeContextBlockRecord, makeContextStalenessRecord, makeContextSummaryRecord, makeContextNonCanonPolicyRecord.
4. Add card helpers: makeCardMirrorRecord, makeCardSyncPlanRecord, makeProtectedCardRecord, makeCardWriteProposalRecord, makeCardSyncAuditRecord.
5. Add default non-canon source policy blocking command/context/debug/releasecheck/stateaudit/cardaudit/card mirror/model summary output from mutation.
6. Update ContextBuilder section renderers for player/body/form, inventory, abilities, quests, actors, location/time, combat/threat, progression/stats, lore/knowledge, and warnings.
7. Add ContextBudgetManager with critical budget reservation and omitted-section reasons.
8. Add ContextStalenessChecker comparing old summaries to owner reducer state.
9. Add CardSyncLite dry-run records only; no live writes.
10. Update /context, /memory, /cardaudit, and /stateaudit displays.
11. Update IntegrityChecker for source-block, hidden lore, stale sections, and protected cards.
12. Add PC16-G01 through PC16-G19 ReleaseCheck gates.
13. Add PC16-001 through PC16-025 tests.
14. Run /foundationcheck, /releasecheck, /integrity, /playerbreakercheck, /regressioncheck, /context, /memory, /cardaudit, /stateaudit.
15. Save WorldLedgerV0.17.16_CONTEXT_CARD_SAFETY_ACCEPTED.txt.
```

It should not produce live Card Sync writes, unprotected Story Card rewrites, context-to-state mutation, hidden lore leakage, owner-state claims from card mirrors, or manual live card sync commands.

# 42. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

```text
context packets/sections have source, priority, budget, and staleness metadata
context builder filters hidden lore and player-unknown lore
context preserves candidate/rumor/visible/offered/unpaid/pending wording
critical current state survives budget pressure
stale context cannot beat owner state
command/context/debug/card mirror output is blocked from mutation
CardSyncLite is dry-run only
protected cards cannot be overwritten automatically
card write proposals with owner-state or hidden-lore risks are blocked/manual-review only
/context /memory /cardaudit /stateaudit display honestly
cleanup preserves non-canon/source-block/protected-card policies
PC16 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC16 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

```text
live Card Sync remains disabled
some commands are folded into /stateaudit instead of separate commands
budget estimates are character-based instead of true token counts
card plans require manual review and are not applied
some low-priority lore is omitted under tight budgets
```

## FAIL

```text
context output can mutate gameplay state
command output can mutate gameplay state
card mirror output can mutate gameplay state
hidden lore leaks into normal context
candidate ability shown as stable
visible/custody item shown as owned
unpaid reward shown as paid
old stale location/inventory/form beats current owner state
budget drops critical identity/body/location/quest/combat warnings while keeping low-priority lore
CardSync writes live cards by default
a protected card is overwritten automatically
cleanup deletes non-canon/source-block/protected-card safety records
```

---

# 43. Handoff to Production Chunk 17

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 17 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 17 — Creature Ecology, Species Rules, and Bestiary Boundaries
```

Production Chunk 17 may use context/lore/card safety to surface creature facts and species rules without spawning encounters or granting powers from lore.

---

# 44. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 16 is where memory becomes safe to use.

Final rule:

```text
The ledger is canon.
Context is a carefully filtered window into canon.
Cards are protected references.
Summaries are non-canon.
Hidden lore stays hidden.
Budget pressure must remove the least important safe text, not the most important truth.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen creature ecology, species rules, and bestiary boundaries in Production Chunk 17.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 17 — Creature Ecology, Species Rules, and Bestiary Boundaries

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 17 — Creature Ecology, Species Rules, and Bestiary Boundaries  
**Production sequence:** 17 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.16 context/card-safety accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Add creature ecology records, species rules, bestiary knowledge, observed signs, habitat logic, behavior boundaries, creature weaknesses/resistances as knowledge-gated facts, loot/body-part boundaries, and creature encounter safety without spawning fights, granting powers, inventing stats, or leaking hidden bestiary secrets.  
**Status:** Core world/gameplay systems production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 16 — Context Builder Budget, Memory Priority, and Card Sync Safety.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 17 is one of the most important gameplay chunks because it decides whether creatures are just random enemy names or actual living parts of the world.

The final AIDRPG system needs creatures to feel like they have:

```text
species identity
habitats
behaviors
signs/tracks/sounds
territory
threat patterns
body parts
loot/source materials
weaknesses and resistances
knowledge gates
regional presence
social/ecological roles
encounter boundaries
combat boundaries
hidden bestiary facts
```

But it must not repeat the old failure loops:

```text
bestiary lore spawning active encounters
creature card text creating enemies in the scene
rumored weakness becoming confirmed fact
hidden bestiary facts leaking into context
player instantly knowing every creature weakness
species lore granting player powers
creature mention creating loot
creature death automatically giving loot or XP
creature threat scaling to player level
generic animals becoming full actors forever
wrong creature signs creating wrong encounters
cleanup deleting active creature evidence or knowledge
```

This chunk creates the creature ecology and bestiary boundary layer.

It is not a full random encounter generator.

It is not full creature combat AI.

It is not automatic loot-table farming.

It is the reliable system that lets those future systems work without corrupting the ledger.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
Creature lore is not an encounter.
Creature sign is not creature presence.
Creature presence is not combat.
Creature defeat is not XP.
Creature corpse is not owned loot.
Creature body part is not harvested item.
Rumored weakness is not confirmed weakness.
Hidden bestiary fact is not player knowledge.
Species trait is not player power.
Habitat supports possibility, not spawning.
Ecology guides the world; it does not bypass event validation.
```

Creatures should feel real because they have rules and context.

They should stay safe because those rules do not mutate unrelated systems without owner validation.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 15 created lore authority and knowledge boundaries.

Production Chunk 16 made context/card output safe and budgeted.

Creature ecology comes now because it depends on both:

```text
world bible lore authority
player knowledge boundaries
hidden lore filtering
threat benchmarks
encounter activation rules
combat event validation
inventory ownership/custody/loot rules
body/form/race constraints
actor presence and generic group handling
place/region/travel context
quest objective separation
progression XP boundaries
context/card non-canon rules
```

If creature ecology is built before those boundaries, it will become a source of accidental spawns, hidden spoilers, free loot, fake XP, and unstable combat.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 17 may change:

```text
Creature ecology schema helpers
Bestiary schema helpers
CreatureKnowledge helper
CreatureSign helper
SpeciesRule helper
CreatureEcologyManager
BestiaryManager
CanonValidator creature/ecology/bestiary rules
ProposalBuilder creature event types
EventScannerLite creature/ecology hints
ThreatBenchmark integration for creature threat labels
EncounterState reference integration
Inventory loot/body-part reference boundary
Combat creature target reference boundary
ContextBuilder creature/bestiary packet
CommandRenderers /bestiary, /creatures, /threat, /encounter if supported
OperationLedger creature/bestiary keys
DirtyQueue creature/lore/threat/context targets
IntegrityChecker creature checks
ReleaseCheck creature gates
Regression/player-breaker creature tests
```

Production Chunk 17 may add:

```text
CreatureSpeciesRecord schema
CreatureVariantRecord schema
CreatureInstanceRecord schema
BestiaryEntryRecord schema
CreatureKnowledgeRecord schema
CreatureSignRecord schema
CreatureHabitatRecord schema
CreatureBehaviorRecord schema
CreatureThreatProfileRecord schema
CreatureWeaknessResistanceRecord schema
CreatureLootProfileRecord schema
CreatureBodyPartRecord schema
CreatureEncounterBoundaryRecord schema
CreatureEcologyObservationRecord schema
CreatureRumorRecord schema
CreatureHiddenFactRecord schema
CreatureContextRuleRecord schema
```

Production Chunk 17 may not add:

```text
full random encounter generator
full creature stat block generator
full tactical creature AI
full loot-table harvesting automation
automatic creature XP rewards
automatic creature spawn from lore/cards/context
automatic player race/species power grants from creature lore
full faction/ecosystem simulation
manual /spawncreature /killcreature /harvest commands
live CardSync writes
old card prose creature import as active state
```

Advanced combat/party/absorption/warfare remains later.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
CreatureState helpers
BestiaryState helpers
CreatureEcologyManager
BestiaryManager
CreatureKnowledgeManager
CreatureSignManager
SpeciesRuleManager shell
CanonValidator creature/ecology rules
ProposalBuilder creature proposals
EventScannerLite creature hints
Resolver creature/species target binding
ThreatBenchmark helper references
EncounterState helper references
ContextBuilderLite creature/bestiary packet
CommandRenderers: /bestiary, /creatures, /threat, /encounter if available
OperationLedger creature keys
DirtyQueue creature/lore/context dirty targets
AuditLogger creature lines
InvariantChecker creature checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC17 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
CombatReducer except creature target references
InventoryReducer except available corpse/loot/body-part reference events remain owner-routed
ProgressionReducer except no automatic creature XP
QuestReducer except creature objective references only
PlaceReducer except habitat/region references only
ActorReducer except generic creature/animal scene presence references
WorldBible import except creature lore reading through existing boundaries
CardSync live writes
manual mutation commands
```

Creature ecology can inform these systems.

It cannot replace them.

---

# 5. Creature/bestiary state model

Add or verify a creature ecology bucket.

Recommended shape:

```text
creatures: {
  species: {},
  variants: {},
  instances: {},
  bestiary: {},
  knowledge: {},
  signs: {},
  habitats: {},
  behaviors: {},
  threatProfiles: {},
  weaknessesResistances: {},
  lootProfiles: {},
  bodyParts: {},
  encounterBoundaries: {},
  observations: {},
  rumors: {},
  hiddenFacts: {},
  contextRules: {},
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

If the existing build uses `worldBible.creatureLore`, this chunk should bridge it to `creatures` as a controlled read/reference path.

Do not create competing creature truth roots.

A clean implementation may use:

```text
worldBible.creatureLore = lore/reference source
creatures.species/bestiary = gameplay-aware bestiary records
encounters.records = active/candidate encounter state
combat.events = combat resolution state
inventory.sceneItems = loot/body part ownership state
```

---

# 6. CreatureSpeciesRecord schema

CreatureSpeciesRecord describes a species as a world/entity category, not an active enemy.

Required shape:

```text
CreatureSpeciesRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  category,
  taxonomyTags,
  confirmed,
  authorityKey,
  sourceKeys,
  habitatKeys,
  behaviorKeys,
  threatProfileKey,
  weaknessResistanceKeys,
  lootProfileKey,

  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Categories:

```text

ooze
swarm
giant
humanoid_creature
aberration_deferred
celestial_deferred
infernal_deferred
fae_deferred
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
species record is not active encounter
species record is not combatant
species record is not loot
species record is not player form/race

```

# 22. CreatureContextRuleRecord schema

Creature context must be filtered and careful.

```text
  sourceOperationKey,

```

Rule types:

```text
show_known_signs

show_rumored_weakness_as_rumor
hide_hidden_weakness
```
