```text
avoid_auto_spawn_wording
avoid_auto_loot_wording
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
context wording must not imply active presence unless encounter/scene validates it
context wording must not imply confirmed knowledge if player only heard rumor
```

---

# 23. Species rules and player race/form boundary

Creature species rules do not grant player powers.

Examples:

```text
Wolves track by scent.
Dragons breathe fire.
Vampires resist normal weapons.
Slimes split when cut.
Ghouls carry disease.
Harpies can fly.
```

Allowed:

```text
create bestiary/species facts
create player-known or hidden knowledge records
create combat-deferred rule labels
create threat/deadliness context
```

Forbidden by default:

```text
player in wolf form automatically gains perfect scent tracking
player with dragon lineage automatically gains breath weapon
vampire lore changes player death boundary
slime lore changes player body rules
harpy lore grants player flight
```

Player form/race power activation remains owner-routed through PC13/Ability/Combat future rules.

---

# 24. Creature lore and encounter separation

Creature lore can support encounter plausibility.

It cannot activate encounters.

Examples:

```text
World lore: Wolves live near the Old Mill.
Expected: habitat/species record, no encounter.

Output: You hear wolves howling beyond the trees.
Expected: sign/candidate danger, no combat.

Output: A wolf steps onto the path and growls.
Expected: creature instance + active encounter candidate, still no damage.

Output: The wolf lunges and bites your arm.
Expected: encounter/combat/damage owner-routed through Combat/Body.
```

---

# 25. Creature loot and inventory separation

Creature loot is possibility, not ownership.

Rules:

```text
loot profile does not create items
creature death does not create owned loot
corpse presence may create scene item/corpse reference if validated
harvesting requires accepted inventory/item event
body parts require acquisition validation
loot duplicates require operation keys
```

Examples:

```text
The wolf has valuable pelts. -> lore/loot possibility, no item
The wolf falls dead. -> corpse/death if combat validates, no pelt owned
I skin the wolf and take its pelt. -> possible inventory acquisition only if validated
```

---

# 26. Creature weakness and combat separation

Known weakness helps narration and future validation, but does not automatically win.

Rules:

```text
weakness fact must be player-known to appear in normal context
rumored weakness must be labeled rumor
hidden weakness omitted
combat weakness effects are deferred unless Combat/Formula owner rule active
using weakness still requires action and combat validation
```

Example:

```text
Known: trolls fear fire.
Input: I threaten the troll with a torch.
Expected: context/possible behavior influence, not automatic victory.
```

---

# 27. Creature threat integration

Creature threat connects to PC14.

Rules:

```text
creature threat profile can create/update ThreatBenchmarkRecord
objective threat does not scale to player level
relative danger can consider player condition/form/current resources
creature ecology does not generate enemy stats
```

Example:

```text
A cave bear is serious objective threat.
If player is wounded and trapped, relative danger may be deadly.
The bear did not scale.
```

---

# 28. Creature behavior integration

Behavior can influence encounter context.

Examples:

```text
territorial wolves may growl/block path before attacking
scavenger ghouls may follow blood scent
nocturnal predators more active at night
nest guardians defend eggs
herd animals flee unless cornered
```

Rules:

```text
behavior informs context and candidate predictions
behavior does not force combat outcome
behavior does not spawn encounter by itself
```

---

# 29. Creature knowledge discovery flow

Safe knowledge flow:

```text
1. Lore/source creates species fact, rumor, hidden fact, or blocked claim.
2. Player sees sign/creature/behavior or hears/reads information.
3. Observation/discovery record updates CreatureKnowledgeRecord.
4. BestiaryEntryRecord updates visible known/suspected facts.
5. ContextBuilder includes only player-known/relevant/safe creature facts.
```

Do not skip from:

```text
hidden bestiary fact -> normal context
world lore -> player expert knowledge
rumor -> confirmed weakness
```

---

# 30. Creature CanonValidator expansion

CanonValidator must enforce creature safety.

## 30.1 Accept species/bestiary lore when

```text
source is allowed by LoreAuthority/WorldBible boundaries
claim is creature/species/habitat/behavior/threat/weakness/loot lore
claim does not attempt active encounter/combat/loot mutation
operation key not duplicate
```

Result:

```text
species/bestiary/lore records only
no active encounter or inventory/combat mutation
```

## 30.2 Accept creature sign when

```text
safe output directly shows tracks/sounds/marks/nest/etc.
place/scene target is bound enough
not command/context/card source
```

Result:

```text
CreatureSignRecord and possible observation/candidate danger
no creature presence unless output directly confirms it
```

## 30.3 Accept creature presence when

```text
safe output directly places creature in current/near scene
species/instance target can be bound
not rumor/lore/book/card/context/command source
```

Result:

```text
CreatureInstanceRecord present/nearby/distant according to text
EncounterState may receive candidate/active encounter proposal if pressure exists
no combat damage
```

## 30.4 Accept creature behavior observation when

```text
safe output directly shows behavior
creature/species target bound
not merely lore/rumor unless stored as rumor/lore
```

## 30.5 Accept weakness/resistance knowledge when

```text
player directly observes it or learns from accepted source
knowledge status/confidence assigned
hidden boundary respected
```

Result:

```text
CreatureKnowledgeRecord update
no automatic combat effect
```

## 30.6 Reject creature mutation when

Any are true:

```text
Story Card tries to spawn creature
context says creature is present
command output says encounter active
lore mentions creature habitat only
rumor says creature attacks here
quest objective names creature
hidden bestiary fact tries to enter normal context
creature lore tries to grant player power
creature lore tries to create loot/XP
```

---

```text
creature.species_lore_candidate

creature.weakness_candidate
creature.resistance_candidate
creature.loot_profile_candidate
creature.body_part_candidate
creature.hidden_fact_candidate
creature.rumor_candidate
creature.instance_candidate
creature.presence_candidate
creature.corpse_candidate
creature.encounter_boundary_candidate
creature.blocked_mutation_candidate
```

Current chunk may commit safe:

```text
species/variant lore
bestiary entries
player creature knowledge
signs/tracks/sounds/nests
habitats
behaviors
threat profile references
weakness/resistance knowledge records
loot/body part possibility records
creature instances/presence references
hidden facts/rumors

```

Current chunk must not commit:

```text
spawned combat
owned loot
XP
player powers
full enemy stat blocks
```

# 32. EventScannerLite creature hints

EventScannerLite may detect creature hints, but detection is not canon.

Hint families:

```text
creature names/species names
tracks/claw marks/fur/scales/scent/howls/nests
habitat words like lair, den, nest, territory
behavior words like stalks, hunts, scavenges, guards, flees
weakness/resistance words like vulnerable to, immune to, fears, avoids
loot/body part words like pelt, fang, scale, venom gland
bestiary/lore words like legends say, hunters know, scholar writes
presence words like appears, steps out, circles overhead
corpse words like carcass, corpse, remains
```

Must flag:

```text
loreOnly
rumorOnly
hiddenFactRisk
signOnly
presenceConfirmed
encounterPressure
combatEngagement
lootPossibilityOnly
harvestAttempt
weaknessRumor
weaknessObserved
resistanceObserved
cardSource
contextSource
commandSource
auditSource
duplicateRisk
playerPowerGrantRisk
spawnRisk
xpLootRisk
```

Parser cannot mutate creature state directly.

---

# 33. CreatureEcologyManager behavior

CreatureEcologyManager should:

```text
ensure creature state shape
create/update species records from safe lore
create/update variants
create/update habitats/behaviors/threat profiles
record signs and observations
update player knowledge through discovery rules
produce safe context facts
create hidden fact/rumor records
record blocked mutation attempts
```

It should not:

```text
spawn encounters from lore
start combat
award XP
grant loot
grant player powers
mutate form/race/body directly
scale creature threat to player level
write live cards
```

---

# 34. BestiaryManager behavior

BestiaryManager should:

```text
build player-facing bestiary entries from CreatureKnowledgeRecords
separate known/rumored/hidden facts
keep hidden facts out of normal context
show misinformation as suspected if tracked
preserve authority/source labels
support /bestiary display
```

BestiaryManager should not:

```text
show hidden weakness before reveal
convert rumor into fact
create combat bonuses by itself
spawn bestiary creatures
```

---

# 35. Creature defensive guards

Creature reducers/managers must reject/no-op if routed result has unsafe markers:

```text
command source
context source
card source
audit/debug source
lore-only as active encounter
sign-only as creature presence
presence as combat damage
rumor as confirmed fact
hidden fact as context
weakness as auto-win
species trait as player power
loot profile as owned item
corpse as harvested item
defeat as XP
player level scaling creature threat
wrong owner reducer
```

Reducers must not blindly trust upstream.

---

# 36. Operation keys

Required creature operation keys:

```text
creature.species.<speciesKey>.<sourceHash>
creature.variant.<speciesKey>.<variantKey>.<sourceHash>
creature.bestiary.<speciesKey>.<knowledgeHash>
creature.knowledge.<speciesKey>.<knowledgeType>.<discoveryHash>
creature.sign.<signType>.<placeKey>.<sourceHash>
creature.habitat.<speciesKey>.<habitatKey>.<sourceHash>
creature.behavior.<speciesKey>.<behaviorType>.<sourceHash>
creature.threat.<speciesKey>.<threatTier>.<sourceHash>
creature.weakness.<speciesKey>.<factHash>
creature.resistance.<speciesKey>.<factHash>
creature.lootProfile.<speciesKey>.<sourceHash>
creature.bodyPart.<speciesKey>.<bodyPartKey>.<sourceHash>
creature.hiddenFact.<speciesKey>.<factHash>
creature.rumor.<speciesKey>.<rumorHash>
creature.instance.<speciesKey>.<placeKey>.<sourceHash>
creature.boundary.<boundaryType>.<policyVersion>
creature.blockedMutation.<ownerSystem>.<claimHash>
```

Rules:

```text
species/knowledge/hidden/boundary keys are durable
sign keys may be scene/time scoped
presence/instance keys prevent duplicate creatures from replay
blocked mutation keys prevent repeated warning spam
```

---

# 37. Dirty targets

Creature updates should mark:

```text
creature_ecology
world_lore
context
threat if threat profile changes
encounter if active/candidate presence validates
inventory_equipment only if owner-routed corpse/loot scene item changes
combat only if combat owner validates engagement
stateaudit/releasecheck if boundaries/conflicts change
```

Mapping:

```text
species/habitat/behavior lore -> creature_ecology, world_lore, context if known/relevant
sign/observation -> creature_ecology, context, threat if danger relevant
presence -> creature_ecology, encounter, context
weakness/resistance knowledge -> creature_ecology, context if player-known
hidden fact -> creature_ecology only, not normal context
loot profile -> creature_ecology, context if player-known/relevant, no inventory mutation
```

---

# 38. `/bestiary`, `/creatures`, `/threat`, `/encounter` updates

## 38.1 `/bestiary`

Should show:

```text
known species
rumored species
known signs
known habitats
known behaviors
known/rumored weaknesses and resistances
known loot possibilities
hidden fact count without spoilers
misinformation/conflict warnings if tracked
```

## 38.2 `/creatures`

Should show:

```text
present creature instances
nearby/distant creature signs
known species records
creature instances tied to active encounter
corpse/carcass references if validated
```

## 38.3 `/threat`

Should show creature threat labels carefully:

```text
objective threat
relative danger warning
source authority
no-rubber-band policy
```

## 38.4 `/encounter`

Should distinguish:

```text
sign/candidate encounter
active encounter
combat engagement
resolved encounter
```

All command output starts with `[AIDRPG]` and remains non-canon.

---

# 39. ContextBuilder creature packet

ContextBuilder should include only relevant, player-safe creature facts.

Include:

```text
active creature encounter/presence facts from owner state
known creature signs in current scene
known behavior relevant to immediate scene
known/rumored weakness only if player knows it and labeled correctly
hidden facts only if revealed
creature threat warnings from PC14
form/body constraints relevant to creature encounter
```

Do not include:

```text
hidden weakness before discovery
full bestiary dump
species lore as active creature presence
rumored creature as confirmed encounter
loot profile as loot owned
creature behavior as guaranteed action
```

Good context example:

```text
Creature signs: Fresh wolf tracks cross the mud near Old Mill. This suggests wolves may be nearby, but none are currently confirmed in the scene. Known rumor: locals say wolves avoid silver bells; unconfirmed.
```

Bad context example:

```text
Wolves are here. They fear silver bells. You can use silver bells to win. You will get wolf pelts if you kill them.
```

---

# 40. IntegrityChecker creature checks

Add checks:

```text
creature ecology bucket exists
species records do not create active encounters
sign records do not mark creature present
bestiary hidden facts not included in normal context
rumors not marked confirmed without authority
loot profiles not present in inventory as owned items
weakness/resistance records do not create combat effects unless owner rule active
creature threat profiles do not scale to player level
creature instance presence source not command/card/context
encounter/combat/inventory/progression boundaries intact
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad records.

It may not spawn creatures, award loot/XP, or grant powers.

---

# 41. Cleanup creature protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old duplicate sign spam
old blocked mutation warning spam
old low-confidence rumor duplicates
old resolved creature instance summaries
old context-only bestiary section caches
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
confirmed species records
player knowledge/discovery records
hidden fact controls
active creature instances
active signs in current scene
active encounter-linked creature records
threat profiles
creature boundary records
blocked mutation records needed for audit
```

Cleanup must not:

```text
confirm rumors
reveal hidden facts
spawn/despawn active creatures without owner validation
grant loot
grant XP
grant player powers
```

---

# 42. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC17-specific gates:

```text
PC17-G01 CreatureSpeciesRecord schema exists or equivalent species structure exists
PC17-G02 CreatureVariantRecord and CreatureInstanceRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC17-G03 BestiaryEntryRecord and CreatureKnowledgeRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC17-G04 CreatureSignRecord and CreatureEcologyObservationRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC17-G05 CreatureHabitatRecord and CreatureBehaviorRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC17-G06 CreatureThreatProfileRecord integrates with no-rubber-band threat policy
PC17-G07 CreatureWeaknessResistanceRecord separates known/rumored/hidden/mechanical-deferred facts
PC17-G08 CreatureLootProfileRecord and CreatureBodyPartRecord do not grant inventory
PC17-G09 CreatureEncounterBoundaryRecord exists and blocks lore/sign/presence/combat/loot/XP shortcuts
PC17-G10 CanonValidator rejects card/context/command creature claims as active gameplay mutation
PC17-G11 signs do not become confirmed presence unless presence validates
PC17-G12 lore/habitat does not spawn encounters
PC17-G13 presence does not become combat/damage without CombatValidator
PC17-G14 defeat/corpse does not grant XP/loot without owner events
PC17-G15 species traits do not grant player powers without owner rules
PC17-G16 hidden bestiary facts do not enter normal context before reveal
PC17-G17 BestiaryManager displays known/rumored/hidden facts honestly
PC17-G18 cleanup preserves species/knowledge/hidden/boundary/active records
PC17-G19 random encounter generator and full creature stat AI remain disabled
PC17-G20 player-breaker creature/bestiary tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if creature lore spawns encounters, hidden weakness leaks to context, creature death grants loot/XP automatically, or species lore grants player powers.

# 51. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 17 passes if:

```text
creature species/variant/instance/bestiary structures exist
creature signs are separated from presence
presence is separated from combat
bestiary knowledge separates known/rumored/hidden facts
hidden bestiary facts stay out of normal context
weakness/resistance facts do not auto-win or auto-null combat
loot/body-part profiles do not grant inventory
creature defeat/corpse does not grant XP/loot automatically
species traits do not grant player powers
creature threat profiles respect no-rubber-band policy
card/context/command creature claims cannot become active state
/bestiary and /creatures display honestly
cleanup preserves species/knowledge/hidden/boundary/active records
PC17 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC17 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
random encounters remain disabled
creature stat blocks are not generated
advanced creature combat AI is deferred
loot harvesting is owner-routed but not fully automated
some species facts remain lore-only
some weaknesses/resistances are mechanical-deferred
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
creature lore spawns encounter
creature sign creates present enemy
creature presence deals damage
hidden weakness leaks to context
rumored weakness becomes confirmed fact without authority
creature death grants XP
creature corpse grants owned loot
species lore grants player powers
player level scales creature threat
card/context/command creature text mutates active state
cleanup reveals hidden facts or deletes active creature records
CreatureEcologyManager trusts unsafe routed data
```

---

# 52. Handoff to Production Chunk 18

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 18 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 18 — Economy, Shops, Trade, Debt, and Service Contracts
```

Production Chunk 18 may use creature loot profiles as possible trade goods, but it must not treat loot profiles as owned inventory.

Production Chunk 17 must leave creature/bestiary state reliable enough that PC18 can ask:

```text
Is this creature material actually owned?
Is it merely a possible loot profile?
Does the player know the material value?
Was the creature harvested through valid inventory events?
Is the source a rumor, hidden fact, or confirmed observation?
```

---

# 53. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 17 is where the world’s creatures become believable without becoming unsafe automation.

Final rule:

```text
Creatures have ecology.
Bestiary knowledge is gated.
Signs are evidence, not enemies.
Lore is possibility, not presence.
Presence is pressure, not combat.
Death is not loot.
Weakness is knowledge, not victory.
Species facts are not player powers.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen economy, shops, trade, debt, and service contracts in Production Chunk 18.

# AIDRPG Production Chunk 18 — Economy, Shops, Trade, Debt, and Service Contracts

## 0. Document status

**Document name:** AIDRPG Production Chunk 18 — Economy, Shops, Trade, Debt, and Service Contracts  
**Production sequence:** 18 of 25 remaining production chunks  
**Build target:** WorldLedger V0.17.17 creature-ecology accepted build / AIDRPG Core MVP expansion path  
**Purpose:** Add economy records, shop offers, price quotes, buy/sell/trade validation, merchant stock boundaries, debt/loan/service-contract handling, deposits/escrow, currency transaction safety, and duplicate-safe payment behavior without turning listed prices, promises, shop stock, or service offers into owned inventory or paid currency.  
**Status:** Core gameplay-depth production chunk.  
**Follows:** Production Chunk 17 — Creature Ecology, Species Rules, and Bestiary Boundaries.  
**This document does not contain final script code.**

Production Chunk 18 exists because economy systems are easy to make useful, but even easier to corrupt.

Old and rough builds risked:

```text
merchant stock becoming player inventory
price listings becoming purchases
purchase intent spending money before the seller accepts
item offers becoming owned items
promised payment becoming paid currency
selling an item without removing it
buying an item without subtracting currency
duplicate output charging twice or granting twice
barter text duplicating both items
service offers completing before service is performed
loans/debts becoming free money or repeated debt
Card/context shop text creating items or prices
```

This chunk makes economy gameplay practical for AI Dungeon: shops, prices, trade, debt, and services can appear naturally in story, but every actual item/currency change must be validated, owner-routed, and duplicate-safe.

---

# 1. Chunk doctrine

```text
A price is not a purchase.
A shop listing is not owned inventory.
An offer is not acceptance.
A promise to pay is not payment.
A debt is not currency received unless funds are handed over.
A service offer is not a completed service.
A sale removes the sold item and adds payment only if both sides validate.
A purchase removes payment and adds item only if both sides validate.
A barter is two owner-routed transfers, not a vague swap.
Duplicate trade output cannot charge, grant, sell, or repay twice.
```

Economy truth must be transactional, owner-routed, and reversible/auditable.

---

# 2. Why this chunk comes now

Production Chunk 17 made creature and bestiary boundaries safe.

Economy comes next because it depends on earlier systems:

```text
inventory ownership, custody, scene items, and currency from PC05
quest reward promise/payment separation from PC07
actors/merchants from PC08
places/shops/current scene from PC09
progression/stat boundaries from PC10–PC11
combat/loot boundaries from PC12
threat/encounter loot separation from PC14
lore/card safety from PC15–PC16
creature parts/material boundaries from PC17
```

AI Dungeon gameplay frequently involves merchants, taverns, inn fees, bribes, loans, bounties, repairs, healing services, training fees, crafting commissions, transport, and debt.

This chunk creates the safe economy foundation without building the full advanced market simulation yet.

---

# 3. What this chunk is allowed to change

Production Chunk 18 may change:

```text
Economy schema helpers
EconomyReducerLite
Shop/Trade helper
PriceQuote helper
Debt/ServiceContract helper
CanonValidator economy/trade rules
ProposalBuilder economy event types
EventScannerLite economy hints
CommandRenderers /inventory, /quests, optional /shop, optional /economy
ContextBuilder economy packet
OperationLedger economy/currency/trade keys
DirtyQueue economy/inventory/quest/actor targets
IntegrityChecker economy checks
ReleaseCheck economy gates
Regression/player-breaker economy tests
```

Production Chunk 18 may add:

```text
EconomyState schema
CurrencyAccountRecord schema
CurrencyTransactionRecord schema
PriceQuoteRecord schema
ShopRecord schema
ShopStockRecord schema
TradeOfferRecord schema
PurchaseRecord schema
SaleRecord schema
BarterRecord schema
ServiceContractRecord schema
DebtRecord schema
LoanRecord schema
DepositEscrowRecord schema
EconomicDisputeRecord schema
MerchantRelationNote shell
```

Production Chunk 18 may not add:

```text
full dynamic economy simulation
regional inflation/market generation
merchant AI pricing engine
crafting economy automation
full reputation/faction discounts
full banking/investment system
manual /buy /sell /givemoney mutation commands
live CardSync shop writes
shop card import as inventory truth
```

Crafting/material systems are later.

Faction/reputation pricing is later.

Advanced settlement/domain economy is later.

---

# 4. Modules touched

Allowed modules:

```text
EconomyState helpers
EconomyReducerLite
TradeTransactionHelper
PriceQuoteHelper
DebtServiceHelper
InventoryReducer split-owner handoff
QuestReducer reward/payment references
ActorReducer merchant/service references
PlaceReducer shop/place references
CanonValidator economy rules
ProposalBuilder economy proposals
EventScannerLite economy hints
CommandRenderers: /inventory, /quests, optional /shop, optional /economy
ContextBuilderLite economy packet
OperationLedger economy keys
DirtyQueue economy dirty targets
AuditLogger economy lines
InvariantChecker economy checks
ReleaseCheckLite PC18 gates
Regression harness
PlayerBreaker harness
```

Avoid major changes to:

```text
Inventory ownership rules except validated split transactions
Quest reward logic except references to service/payment/debt
Actor relationship/reputation automation
Full shop generation
World market simulation
Crafting/material recipes
Manual mutation commands
CardSync live writes
```

EconomyReducer coordinates transactions.

InventoryReducer still owns item and currency truth.

---

# 5. Economy state model

Add or verify an economy bucket.

Recommended shape:

```text
economy: {
  accounts: {},
  transactions: {},
  priceQuotes: {},
  shops: {},
  shopStock: {},
  tradeOffers: {},
  purchases: {},
  sales: {},
  barters: {},
  serviceContracts: {},
  debts: {},
  loans: {},
  depositsEscrow: {},
  disputes: {},
  warnings: [],
  notes: []
}
```

If currency currently lives entirely inside inventory, do not duplicate currency truth.

Economy may store account references and transaction metadata, but InventoryReducer remains the owner of player currency amounts.

---

# 6. CurrencyAccountRecord schema

Currency accounts describe holders, not just player totals.

Required shape:

```text
CurrencyAccountRecord {
  id,
  key,
  holderType,
  holderKey,
  displayName,
  currencyTypes,
  ownerSystem,
  knownToPlayer,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Holder types:

```text
player
actor
shop
faction_deferred
bank_deferred
quest_escrow
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
player currency truth remains with InventoryReducer unless architecture already owns it elsewhere
merchant currency may be unknown and should not be invented
shop accounts can be reference-only unless needed for transaction audit
```

---

# 7. CurrencyTransactionRecord schema

Every actual currency change needs a transaction record or equivalent audit.

Required shape:

```text
CurrencyTransactionRecord {
  id,
  key,
  transactionType,
  amount,
  currencyType,
  fromHolderKey,
  toHolderKey,
  relatedTradeKey,
  relatedQuestKey,
  relatedDebtKey,
  sourceOperationKey,
  inventoryOperationKey,
  status,
  createdTurn,
  appliedTurn,
  duplicateOf,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Transaction types:

```text
purchase_payment
sale_payment
quest_reward_payment
service_payment
debt_issued
debt_repayment
loan_principal
loan_repayment
bribe_deferred
fine_deferred
deposit
escrow_hold
refund
unknown
```

Statuses:

```text
proposed
accepted
applied
deferred
rejected
disputed
skipped_duplicate
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
transaction applied only if InventoryReducer currency result validates
promise/quote/listing creates no currency transaction applied
duplicate transaction keys prevent double charges/payments
```

---

# 8. PriceQuoteRecord schema

Price quotes are information, not transactions.

Required shape:

```text
PriceQuoteRecord {
  id,
  key,
  itemKey,
  serviceKey,
  displayName,
  amount,
  currencyType,
  quoteType,
  sellerActorKey,
  shopKey,
  validScope,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  expiresTurn,
  status,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Quote types:

```text
ask_price
sell_offer
buy_offer
service_fee
inn_fee
training_fee
repair_fee
transport_fee
bounty_offer
rumored_price
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
quote does not spend currency
quote does not grant item/service
quote can be used to validate later purchase if still relevant
rumored price is not confirmed shop price
```

---

# 9. ShopRecord schema

Shop records describe commerce locations/actors.

Required shape:

```text
ShopRecord {
  id,
  key,
  displayName,
  shopType,
  placeKey,
  ownerActorKey,
  merchantActorKeys,
  stockKeys,
  serviceKeys,
  pricePolicyKey,
  status,
  sourceOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Shop types:

```text
general_store
blacksmith
alchemist
inn
tavern
healer
trainer
stable
market_stall
fence_deferred
bank_deferred
unknown
```

Statuses:

```text
known
open
closed
unavailable
rumored
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
shop exists does not mean current location
shop stock does not mean player ownership
shop services do not mean completed service
```

---

# 10. ShopStockRecord schema

Shop stock is available-for-sale or known-stock, not owned by player.

Required shape:

```text
ShopStockRecord {
  id,
  key,
  shopKey,
  itemKey,
  displayName,
  quantity,
  quantityKnown,
  priceQuoteKey,
  stockStatus,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Stock statuses:

```text
available
reserved
sold_out
unknown_stock
rumored_stock
not_for_sale
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
available stock is not player inventory
reserved stock is not owned unless purchase validates
sold out stock cannot be purchased without restock validation
quantity unknown should not be guessed
```

---

# 11. TradeOfferRecord schema

Trade offers are proposals.

Required shape:

```text
TradeOfferRecord {
  id,
  key,
  offerType,
  offeredByHolderKey,
  offeredToHolderKey,
  giveItemKeys,
  receiveItemKeys,
  giveCurrency,
  receiveCurrency,
  serviceKeys,
  conditionsText,
  status,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  acceptedTurn,
  rejectedTurn,
  notes
}
```

Offer types:

```text
purchase_offer
sale_offer
barter_offer
service_offer
loan_offer
debt_repayment_offer
reward_offer
unknown
```

Statuses:

```text
offered
accepted
rejected
countered
expired
disputed
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
offered does not transfer items/currency
accepted still needs owner-routed transaction effects
counteroffer does not complete old offer
```

---

# 12. PurchaseRecord schema

Purchases must route both currency and item ownership.

Required shape:

```text
PurchaseRecord {
  id,
  key,
  buyerHolderKey,
  sellerHolderKey,
  shopKey,
  itemKey,
  itemDisplayName,
  quantity,
  priceAmount,
  currencyType,
  priceQuoteKey,
  paymentOperationKey,
  inventoryReceiveOperationKey,
  status,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  appliedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Statuses:

```text
intent
accepted
payment_applied
item_received
completed
failed
rejected
skipped_duplicate
disputed
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
purchase intent does not spend currency
purchase accepted does not complete until payment and item handoff validate
completed purchase should have both payment and item ownership records or explicit deferred warning
```

---

# 13. SaleRecord schema

Sales must remove/surrender item and add payment.

Required shape:

```text
SaleRecord {
  id,
  key,
  sellerHolderKey,
  buyerHolderKey,
  itemKey,
  itemDisplayName,
  quantity,
  priceAmount,
  currencyType,
  itemTransferOperationKey,
  paymentOperationKey,
  status,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  appliedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
sale offer does not remove item
sale completed requires item transfer/removal and currency gain validation
cannot sell item not owned/carried unless custody policy supports it
```

---

# 14. BarterRecord schema

Barter is two or more transfers with no assumed currency.

Required shape:

```text
BarterRecord {
  id,
  key,
  partyAHolderKey,
  partyBHolderKey,
  partyAOffers,
  partyBOffers,
  status,
  partyATransferKeys,
  partyBTransferKeys,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  appliedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Rules:

```text
barter proposal does not transfer
completed barter requires both owner-routed sides
if one side fails, mark disputed/deferred rather than silently completing
```

---

# 15. ServiceContractRecord schema

Services are common in AI Dungeon: healing, repairs, training, lodging, transport, information, crafting commissions.

Required shape:

```text
ServiceContractRecord {
  id,
  key,
  serviceType,
  providerActorKey,
  customerHolderKey,
  shopKey,
  description,
  priceAmount,
  currencyType,
  status,
  paymentOperationKey,
  resultOperationKeys,
  relatedQuestKey,
  createdTurn,
  paidTurn,
  completedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Service types:

```text
healing
repair
training
lodging
transport
information
crafting_commission
identification_deferred
curse_removal_deferred
legal_service_deferred
unknown
```

Statuses:

```text
offered
accepted
paid
in_progress
completed
failed
refunded
disputed
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
service offer is not completed service
service payment is not service result
healing service result must route through BodyReducer
repair service result must route through Inventory/Equipment/Crafting owner rules
training service result does not grant ability/XP unless owner rules validate
transport service does not move player until PlaceReducer validates travel/arrival
```

---

# 16. DebtRecord and LoanRecord schemas

Debt and loans need separate promise, issuance, and repayment states.

DebtRecord:

```text
DebtRecord {
  id,
  key,
  debtorHolderKey,
  creditorHolderKey,
  amount,
  currencyType,
  reason,
  status,
  dueText,
  dueTurn,
  issuedOperationKey,
  repaymentOperationKeys,
  relatedQuestKey,
  createdTurn,
  updatedTurn,
  settledTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

LoanRecord:

```text
LoanRecord {
  id,
  key,
  lenderHolderKey,
  borrowerHolderKey,
  principalAmount,
  currencyType,
  interestPolicyDeferred,
  collateralKeys,
  status,
  principalPaymentOperationKey,
  repaymentOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  issuedTurn,
  settledTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Debt statuses:

```text
proposed
owed
issued
partially_repaid
repaid
forgiven
defaulted_deferred
disputed
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
debt owed does not add currency to player
loan principal adds currency only when funds are handed over
repayment subtracts currency only when payment validates
interest/default consequences deferred unless later rules exist
```

---

# 17. DepositEscrowRecord schema

Deposits and escrow prevent ownership confusion.

Required shape:

```text
DepositEscrowRecord {
  id,
  key,
  holderKey,
  counterpartyKey,
  itemKeys,
  currencyAmount,
  currencyType,
  purpose,
  status,
  releaseConditionText,
  depositOperationKeys,
  releaseOperationKeys,
  createdTurn,
  releasedTurn,
  warnings,
  notes
}
```

Statuses:

```text
proposed
held
released
forfeited
disputed
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
escrow item/currency is not free inventory/currency
release requires validation
forfeit requires explicit validated event or future policy
```

---

# 18. EconomicDisputeRecord schema

Economy needs dispute records because AI Dungeon often contradicts itself.

Required shape:

```text
EconomicDisputeRecord {
  id,
  key,
  disputeType,
  relatedTradeKey,
  claimA,
  claimB,
  resolvedStatus,
  sourceOperationKey,
  createdTurn,
  notes
}
```

Dispute types:

```text
price_changed
seller_refuses
insufficient_funds
item_not_owned
stock_unavailable
payment_claimed_but_not_validated
item_claimed_but_not_validated
service_paid_but_not_completed
duplicate_transaction
unknown
```

Rules:

```text
dispute blocks unsafe transfers
dispute appears in /shop or /stateaudit
```

---

# 19. Price and value policy

This chunk should not invent a full pricing economy.

Allowed:

```text
record explicit prices from safe story output
record shop quotes
record offer/counteroffer
record service fees
record debt amounts
record barter terms
```

Forbidden by default:

```text
auto-price every item
calculate regional inflation
auto-discount from reputation
scale prices to player level
generate shop stock from lore/cards
```

If price is unknown:

```text
show price unknown
ask story to establish price naturally
```

Do not guess.

---

# 20. Economy CanonValidator expansion

## 20.1 Accept price quote when

```text
safe output directly states seller/shop/service price
price amount and currency are bound
not command/context/card/debug source
not rumor unless stored as rumored price
```

Result:

```text
PriceQuoteRecord only
no currency/item mutation
```

## 20.2 Accept purchase when

All are true:

```text
safe output confirms buyer pays or seller hands over after accepted purchase
item/service target bound
amount/currency bound if payment applies
not merely price/intention/offer
not command/context/card source
operation key not duplicate
```

Result:

```text
PurchaseRecord and split owner events:
InventoryReducer currency loss
InventoryReducer item gain
or service result owner event if service
```

## 20.3 Accept sale when

```text
safe output confirms player sells/transfers item and receives payment
item is owned/available to sell
amount/currency bound
operation key not duplicate
```

Result:

```text
SaleRecord and split owner events:
InventoryReducer item removal/transfer
InventoryReducer currency gain
```

## 20.4 Accept service contract when

```text
safe output offers/accepts/pays/completes service
service type and provider/customer bound enough
```

Result:

```text
service record updates
service result only routes to owner if validated
```

## 20.5 Reject economy mutation when

Any are true:

```text
price listing only
shop stock listing only
player intent only
merchant promise only
quest reward promise only
service offer only
command/context/card/debug source
insufficient ownership/currency if strict validation active
ambiguous item/currency target
duplicate operation key
```

---

# 22. EventScannerLite economy hints

EventScannerLite may detect economy hints, but detection is not canon.

Hint families:

```text
buy/purchase/pay/sell/trade/barter
price/cost/fee/quote/offer
merchant/shop/stall/market/inn/tavern/blacksmith/alchemist/healer/trainer
loan/debt/owe/borrow/repay/interest
service/repair/heal/train/lodging/transport/commission
refund/deposit/escrow/collateral
```

Must flag:

```text
priceOnly
stockOnly
intentOnly
offerOnly
paymentConfirmed
handoffConfirmed
serviceCompleted
promiseOnly
debtOnly
insufficientFundsRisk
ownershipRisk
ambiguousCurrency
ambiguousItem
commandSource
contextSource
cardSource
auditSource
duplicateRisk
```

Parser cannot mutate economy/inventory.

# 23. EconomyReducerLite behavior

EconomyReducerLite owns:

```text
economy.transactions
economy.priceQuotes
economy.shops
economy.shopStock
economy.tradeOffers
economy.purchases
economy.sales
economy.barters
economy.serviceContracts
economy.debts
economy.loans
economy.depositsEscrow
economy.disputes
```

## 23.1 Price/shop/stock commit

Accepted quote/stock/shop should:

```text
create/update shop/quote/stock records
not mutate player inventory or currency
mark economy/context dirty
```

## 23.2 Purchase commit

Accepted purchase should:

```text
create/update PurchaseRecord
validate payment/handoff split
route currency loss to InventoryReducer
route item gain to InventoryReducer
mark completed only when both sides apply or explicitly defer with warning
```

## 23.3 Sale commit

Accepted sale should:

```text
create/update SaleRecord
route item removal/transfer to InventoryReducer
route currency gain to InventoryReducer
mark completed only when both sides apply or explicitly defer with warning
```

## 23.4 Service commit

Accepted service update should:

```text
create/update ServiceContractRecord
route payment to InventoryReducer
route result to appropriate owner only if validated
keep paid vs completed separate
```

## 23.5 Debt/loan/repayment commit

Accepted debt/loan should:

```text
record obligation separately from currency
apply loan funds only when handed over
apply repayment only when payment validates
preserve due/deadline links if any
```

## 23.6 Barter/deposit/escrow commit

Accepted barter/deposit/escrow should:

```text
record terms
route item/currency custody through InventoryReducer when actual handoff validates
mark disputed/deferred if any side fails
```

---

# 24. Split-owner economy contract

EconomyReducer coordinates, but owner reducers apply truth.

```text
InventoryReducer owns player items/currency.
QuestReducer owns quest reward states.
ActorReducer owns merchant/social memory references.
PlaceReducer owns shop/current location references.
BodyReducer owns healing service results.
AbilityReducer owns training service ability evidence/stability.
ProgressionReducer owns XP training/reward results.
```

If split-owner routing cannot safely apply both sides:

```text
record economy transaction as deferred/disputed
show warning
never silently complete
```

---

# 25. Operation keys

Required economy operation keys:

```text
economy.price.<sellerKey>.<itemOrServiceKey>.<amount>.<sourceHash>
economy.shop.<shopKey>.<sourceHash>
economy.stock.<shopKey>.<itemKey>.<sourceHash>
economy.offer.<offerType>.<partyA>.<partyB>.<sourceHash>
economy.purchase.<buyerKey>.<sellerKey>.<itemKey>.<amount>.<sourceHash>
economy.sale.<sellerKey>.<buyerKey>.<itemKey>.<amount>.<sourceHash>
economy.barter.<partyA>.<partyB>.<termsHash>
economy.service.<serviceType>.<providerKey>.<customerKey>.<sourceHash>
economy.debt.<debtorKey>.<creditorKey>.<amount>.<sourceHash>
economy.loan.issue.<lenderKey>.<borrowerKey>.<amount>.<sourceHash>
economy.debt.repay.<debtorKey>.<creditorKey>.<amount>.<sourceHash>
economy.deposit.<holderKey>.<counterpartyKey>.<termsHash>
economy.escrow.release.<escrowKey>.<sourceHash>
economy.dispute.<disputeType>.<sourceHash>
```

Linked Inventory keys:

```text
currency.loss.<amount>.<currencyType>.<economyTransactionKey>
currency.gain.<amount>.<currencyType>.<economyTransactionKey>
item.purchase.receive.<itemKey>.<economyTransactionKey>
item.sale.transfer.<itemKey>.<economyTransactionKey>
item.barter.transfer.<itemKey>.<economyTransactionKey>
```

Rules:

```text
purchase/sale/payment keys are durable
stock/quote keys can be update-safe
same output replay cannot charge or grant twice
```

---

# 26. Dirty targets

Economy updates should mark:

```text
economy
inventory_equipment
quest_log when tied to quest reward/payment/service
social_memory when tied to merchant/actor promise or dispute
current_location when shop/place display changes
context
```

Mapping:

```text
quote/shop/stock -> economy, context
purchase/sale/barter -> economy, inventory_equipment, context
service -> economy, inventory_equipment if payment, body/ability/progression owner if result applies, context
debt/loan -> economy, inventory_equipment if funds/payment apply, quest_log if linked, context
```

Dirty targets do not write cards directly.

---

# 27. `/inventory`, optional `/shop`, optional `/economy` updates

## 27.1 `/inventory`

Must not show shop stock as owned.

Should distinguish:

```text
owned currency
promised rewards
shop prices
available shop stock
items held in escrow/deposit
items sold/transferred away
```

## 27.2 Optional `/shop`

If command budget permits:

```text
/shop
```

Shows:

```text
current/known shop
available stock
price quotes
services offered
trade offers
purchase/sale warnings
```

## 27.3 Optional `/economy`

Shows:

```text
recent transactions
debts/loans
service contracts
deposits/escrow
disputes
```

All command output starts with `[AIDRPG]` and remains non-canon.

---

# 28. ContextBuilder economy packet

Context should include only relevant economy facts.

Include:

```text
current shop/service offers if in scene
active unpaid debts
active service contract paid/completed status
important trade disputes
promised vs paid money distinctions
```

Do not include:

```text
shop stock as owned inventory
price quote as completed purchase
offer as accepted trade
debt as money received
paid service as completed healing/training unless owner result validates
```

Example:

```text
Economy: The blacksmith offers an iron dagger for 8 gold. You have not bought it. You owe Old Bren 5 gold due before sunset. The healer was paid 3 silver, but the wound has not been confirmed healed yet.
```

---

# 29. IntegrityChecker economy checks

Add checks:

```text
shop stock not in player owned inventory unless purchased
price quotes not applied as transactions
purchase completed has payment and item handoff or explicit deferred warning
sale completed has item transfer/removal and payment or explicit deferred warning
debt/loan/repayment states are separated
service paid vs completed separated
currency transactions have operation keys
no duplicate purchase/sale/payment keys
command/card/context source not active economy truth
```

IntegrityChecker may warn/quarantine bad economy records.

It may not grant items/currency, complete services, repay debts, or alter shop stock.

---

# 30. Cleanup economy protections

Cleanup may compact:

```text
old expired price quotes
old rejected offer spam
old duplicate transaction warnings
old completed low-value transaction summaries after audit retention
```

Cleanup must not delete:

```text
active debts/loans
unsettled service contracts
deposits/escrow
unresolved disputes
recent/important transactions
operation keys preventing duplicate charges/grants
promised vs paid reward/payment distinctions
```

Cleanup must not settle debts, complete services, delete owed money, or convert shop stock into owned items.

---

# 31. ReleaseCheck gates for this chunk

Add or verify PC18-specific gates:

```text
PC18-G01 EconomyState schema exists or equivalent economy bucket exists
PC18-G02 CurrencyTransactionRecord schema exists or equivalent transaction audit exists
PC18-G03 PriceQuoteRecord, ShopRecord, and ShopStockRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC18-G04 TradeOfferRecord, PurchaseRecord, SaleRecord, and BarterRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC18-G05 ServiceContractRecord, DebtRecord, LoanRecord, and DepositEscrowRecord schemas exist or equivalents exist
PC18-G06 CanonValidator separates price/stock/offer/intent from purchase/payment/handoff
PC18-G07 EconomyReducer rejects unsafe routed economy events
PC18-G08 purchase/sale/barter route item/currency changes through InventoryReducer
PC18-G09 service payment and service result are separated and owner-routed
PC18-G10 debts/loans/repayments are stateful and not free currency
PC18-G11 duplicate payment/purchase/sale/repayment keys exist and are durable
PC18-G12 /inventory and /shop or /economy display shop stock, quotes, owned inventory, and debts honestly
PC18-G13 command/context/card economy text cannot become canon
PC18-G14 cleanup preserves active debts/contracts/escrow/disputes/transaction keys
PC18-G15 full market simulation/reputation discounts/crafting economy remain deferred
PC18-G16 player-breaker economy tests registered
```

ReleaseCheck must fail if shop stock becomes owned inventory, price quote spends currency, purchase duplicates, sale pays without item transfer, debt creates free money, or command/card/context shop text mutates state.

---

```text
no ability/XP/stat gain unless owner rules separately validate
```

# 35. Parser/proposal/validator changes

Allowed:

```text
price/stock/offer/purchase/sale/barter/service/debt/loan/deposit detection
promise/payment/handoff separation
shop stock vs owned inventory separation
service paid vs service completed separation
operation key suggestions
```

Forbidden:

```text
manual buy/sell/givemoney parser
shop stock generation from lore/card
market simulation parser
reputation discount parser
crafting economy automation parser
card/context economy import
```

---

# 36. Reducer/checker changes

Allowed:

```text
EconomyReducer records and transaction coordination
InventoryReducer split item/currency handoff
service owner-routing shell
currency transaction audit
trade/debt/escrow duplicate skip results
economy integrity checks
```

Forbidden:

```text
EconomyReducer directly rewriting inventory/currency outside owner contract
free money from debt
free item from shop stock
service result without owner validation
manual economy mutation reducer
```

---

# 37. Command/context/card changes

Allowed:

```text
/inventory economy distinctions
/shop optional command
/economy optional command
/context economy packet
/cardaudit economy risk labels
/releasecheck PC18 gates
/playerbreakercheck economy cases
```

Forbidden:

```text
/buy active mutation command
/sell active mutation command
/givemoney
/setprice
/clearDebt active command
live card writes that create shop/economy truth
```

# 38. Beginner implementation order

When implementing this chunk in code, follow this order.

## Step 1 — Copy accepted creature ecology build

Start from:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.17_CREATURE_ECOLOGY_ACCEPTED.txt
```

Create working file:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.18_ECONOMY_TRADE_CONTRACTS_WORKING.txt
```

## Step 2 — Add/verify economy helper functions

Create helpers for:

```text
ensureEconomyShape
makeCurrencyAccountRecord
makeCurrencyTransactionRecord
makePriceQuoteRecord
makeShopRecord
makeShopStockRecord
makeTradeOfferRecord
makePurchaseRecord
makeSaleRecord
makeBarterRecord
makeServiceContractRecord
makeDebtRecord
makeLoanRecord
makeDepositEscrowRecord
makeEconomicDisputeRecord
isUnsafeEconomySource
isPriceOnly
isOfferOnly
isPaymentConfirmed
isHandoffConfirmed
```

## Step 3 — Update economy CanonValidator rules

Add strict accept/reject/pending logic for:

```text
price quote
shop stock
purchase intent
purchase payment/handoff
sale offer/payment/handoff
barter offer/transfer
service offer/payment/completion
debt/loan/repayment
deposit/escrow/refund
```

## Step 4 — Update ProposalBuilder economy support

Ensure proposals carry:

```text
buyer/seller/provider/customer
item/service target
amount/currency
shop/place/actor keys
payment/handoff flags
source/localClause/safety flags
operation key suggestion
```

## Step 5 — Implement EconomyReducerLite records

Start with records and audits before complex behavior.

Do not mutate inventory directly.

## Step 6 — Wire split owner effects

Route:

```text
currency losses/gains -> InventoryReducer
item gains/removals/transfers -> InventoryReducer
healing service results -> BodyReducer
training service results -> Ability/Progression only if validated
travel/lodging effects -> Place/Time/Body only if validated
```

## Step 7 — Update commands and context

Update:

```text
/inventory
/shop if available
/economy if available
ContextBuilder economy packet
CardAudit economy risk labels
```

## Step 8 — Update IntegrityChecker

Add checks for:

```text
stock not owned
quote not purchase
purchase/sale split effects
service paid/completed separation
debt/loan/repayment separation
duplicate transaction keys
unsafe source blocking
```

## Step 9 — Add PC18 ReleaseCheck gates

Add PC18-G01 through PC18-G16.

## Step 10 — Add PC18 tests

Add PC18-001 through PC18-028.

## Step 11 — Run baseline checks

Run:

```text
/foundationcheck
/releasecheck
/integrity
/playerbreakercheck
/regressioncheck
/inventory
/shop if available
/economy if available
/context
```

## Step 12 — Save accepted economy build

Recommended output:

```text
WorldLedgerV0.17.18_ECONOMY_TRADE_CONTRACTS_ACCEPTED.txt
```

# 39. What this chunk produces

Production Chunk 18 should produce:

```text
WorldLedger V0.17.18 economy/trade/contracts accepted script if code changes are made
EconomyState records
price/shop/stock/trade schemas
purchase/sale/barter/service/debt/loan/deposit schemas
EconomyReducerLite coordination behavior
economy CanonValidator refinement
split owner contract with InventoryReducer and other owners
/inventory /shop /economy display improvements
economy context packet
economy integrity checks
PC18 ReleaseCheck gates
PC18 regression/player-breaker tests
economy acceptance report
updated planning zip with Production Chunk 18 added
```

It should not produce:

```text
full dynamic market simulation
manual economy mutation commands
crafting economy automation
reputation pricing engine
live CardSync shop writes
shop card import as inventory truth
```

---

# 40. Pass / warn / fail criteria

## PASS

Production Chunk 18 passes if:

```text
price/stock/offer/intent are separated from purchase/payment/handoff
shop stock does not become owned inventory
purchase/sale/barter split item/currency effects are owner-routed
duplicate payment/purchase/sale/repayment keys work
service paid vs completed remains separated
debt/loan/repayment states are separated
command/context/card economy text cannot become canon
/inventory and /shop or /economy display honestly
cleanup preserves active obligations and transaction keys
PC18 tests pass
ReleaseCheck has no PC18 FAIL/BLOCKED gates
```

## WARN

Warn but allow progress if:

```text
regional pricing is deferred
reputation discounts are deferred
crafting economy is deferred
shop stock generation is manual/story-driven only
merchant currency is unknown/reference-only
some service effects are owner-deferred
```

## FAIL

Fail and stop if:

```text
price quote spends currency
shop stock becomes owned
purchase intent buys item
sale offer removes item or adds money
purchase duplicates charge/grant twice
sale duplicates pay twice
debt creates free money
service payment heals/trains/transports without owner validation
command/context/card text mutates economy/inventory
cleanup deletes active debt/contract/escrow/dispute or transaction keys
EconomyReducer bypasses InventoryReducer for item/currency truth
```

---

# 41. Handoff to Production Chunk 19

When this chunk passes, Production Chunk 19 begins:

```text
Production Chunk 19 — Crafting, Materials, Repair, Enchanting Boundaries, and Recipe Safety
```

Production Chunk 19 may use economy prices, service contracts, shop stock, creature materials, and inventory truth to define crafting and repair.

Production Chunk 18 must leave economy reliable enough that PC19 can ask:

```text
Is this material owned, visible, shop stock, promised, or held in custody?
Was payment made?
Was the service only offered, paid, or completed?
Was the item sold or still owned?
Is this debt active, repaid, disputed, or only proposed?
Can crafting consume materials without duplicating economy/payment effects?
```

---

# 42. Final doctrine

Production Chunk 18 is where money stops being magic text.

Final rule:

```text
Economy is a transaction pipeline.
Prices, offers, stock, promises, debts, payments, handoffs, services, and results are separate.
Every real item or currency change must be owner-routed, duplicate-safe, and display-honest.
```

Once this passes, the project can safely deepen crafting, materials, repair, enchanting boundaries, and recipe safety in Production Chunk 19.
